   230	    180,
   231	    100,
   232	    50,
   233	)
   234	
   235	
   236	def is_standard_fill(beverage: BeverageType, ml: float) -> bool | None:
   237	    """True/False for spirits and wine; None for malt beverages (no federal standards of fill)."""
   238	    if beverage is BeverageType.malt:
   239	        return None
   240	    sizes = SPIRITS_FILL_ML if beverage is BeverageType.spirits else WINE_FILL_ML
   241	    if any(abs(ml - s) <= max(0.005 * s, 0.5) for s in sizes):
   242	        return True
   243	    # 4.72(b): wine of 4 L and larger is allowed in even liters
   244	    return beverage is BeverageType.wine and ml >= 4000 and abs(ml / 1000 - round(ml / 1000)) < 0.005
   245	
   246	
   247	def fill_rule(beverage: BeverageType) -> str:
   248	    return "27 CFR 5.203" if beverage is BeverageType.spirits else "27 CFR 4.72"
   249	
   250	
   251	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- beverage rules
   252	def alcohol_statement_required(beverage: BeverageType, class_type: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   253	    """Whether a numeric alcohol statement is federally required, with the reason (simplified)."""
   254	    if beverage is BeverageType.spirits:
   255	        return True, "Required for distilled spirits (27 CFR 5.65)."
   256	    if beverage is BeverageType.wine:
   257	        ct = fold(class_type)
   258	        # "Table Wine", "Light Wine", and the registry's class descriptions "Table Red Wine",
   259	        # "Table White Wine": the designation may carry a color word in the middle.
   260	        if _TABLE_OR_LIGHT_WINE.search(ct):
   261	            return False, "Wine of 7-14% may state 'Table Wine' or 'Light Wine' instead of a number (27 CFR 4.36)."
   262	        return True, "Required for wine unless designated Table Wine or Light Wine (27 CFR 4.36)."
   263	    return False, (
   264	        "Optional for malt beverages under federal rules unless alcohol comes from added flavors "
   265	        "(27 CFR 7.63, 7.65); state law may require it."
   266	    )


===== FILE: app/pipeline/match.py (current text at 3d9bdad, line-numbered) =====
     1	"""Find the label text that best corresponds to an application value.
     2	
     3	Brand names and class/type designations wrap across lines on real labels, so candidates are
     4	single lines and joins of up to three vertically adjacent lines on the same image.
     5	"""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	
     9	import statistics
    10	from dataclasses import dataclass
    11	
    12	from rapidfuzz import fuzz
    13	
    14	from app.schemas import Evidence, OcrLine, Status
    15	
    16	from .normalize import case_only_difference, collapse_ws, fold, key
    17	
    18	
    19	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    20	class Candidate:
    21	    text: str
    22	    score: int  # 0-100
    23	    lines: tuple[OcrLine, ...]
    24	    identical: bool  # same text after whitespace collapse
    25	    fold_equal: bool  # equal after case/accents/quotes normalization
    26	    key_equal: bool  # equal after dropping punctuation entirely
    27	
    28	    @property
    29	    def evidence(self) -> list[Evidence]:
    30	        return [Evidence(image_index=ln.image_index, box=ln.box, text=ln.text) for ln in self.lines]
    31	
    32	
    33	def _center_y(ln: OcrLine) -> float:
    34	    return sum(p[1] for p in ln.box) / 4
    35	
    36	
    37	def _height(ln: OcrLine) -> float:
    38	    ys = [p[1] for p in ln.box]
    39	    return max(ys) - min(ys)
    40	
    41	
    42	def reading_order(lines: list[OcrLine]) -> dict[int, list[OcrLine]]:
    43	    """Lines grouped by image, in reading order: rows top-to-bottom, left-to-right within a row.
    44	
    45	    Two consecutive lines (by vertical center) belong to the same row when their vertical extents
    46	    overlap by at least half of the shorter line's height. This handles mixed sizes (a huge brand
    47	    next to small body text) and tight line spacing, where fixed-height buckets fail.
    48	    """
    49	    by_image: dict[int, list[OcrLine]] = {}
    50	    for ln in lines:
    51	        by_image.setdefault(ln.image_index, []).append(ln)
    52	    for idx, group in by_image.items():
    53	        ordered = sorted(group, key=_center_y)
    54	        rows: list[list[OcrLine]] = []
    55	        for ln in ordered:
    56	            if rows and _vertical_overlap(rows[-1][-1], ln) >= 0.5 * min(_height(rows[-1][-1]), _height(ln)):
    57	                rows[-1].append(ln)
    58	            else:
    59	                rows.append([ln])
    60	        # Within a row, left to right; but lines whose left edges sit within half a line's thickness
    61	        # of each other share a column and stay top to bottom (tight body text in a narrow column has
    62	        # boxes that overlap vertically, and a few pixels of edge jitter must not swap lines).
    63	        tol = max(1.0, 0.5 * statistics.median(_thickness(ln) for ln in group))
    64	        by_image[idx] = [ln for row in rows for ln in _columns_then_down(row, tol)]
    65	    return by_image
    66	
    67	
    68	def _thickness(ln: OcrLine) -> float:
    69	    """The extent across the text direction: height for a horizontal line, width for a vertical one."""
    70	    xs, ys = [p[0] for p in ln.box], [p[1] for p in ln.box]
    71	    return max(1.0, min(max(xs) - min(xs), max(ys) - min(ys)))
    72	
    73	
    74	def _columns_then_down(row: list[OcrLine], tol: float) -> list[OcrLine]:
    75	    by_x = sorted(row, key=lambda ln: min(p[0] for p in ln.box))
    76	    cols: list[list[OcrLine]] = []
    77	    for ln in by_x:
    78	        x = min(p[0] for p in ln.box)
    79	        if cols and x - min(p[0] for p in cols[-1][0].box) <= tol:
    80	            cols[-1].append(ln)
    81	        else:
    82	            cols.append([ln])
    83	    return [ln for col in cols for ln in sorted(col, key=_center_y)]
    84	
    85	
    86	def _vertical_overlap(a: OcrLine, b: OcrLine) -> float:
    87	    a0, a1 = min(p[1] for p in a.box), max(p[1] for p in a.box)
    88	    b0, b1 = min(p[1] for p in b.box), max(p[1] for p in b.box)
    89	    return max(0.0, min(a1, b1) - max(a0, b0))
    90	
    91	
    92	def _spans(lines: list[OcrLine], max_join: int) -> list[tuple[OcrLine, ...]]:
    93	    """Single lines plus joins of up to max_join consecutive, vertically adjacent lines."""
    94	    if not lines:
    95	        return []
    96	    med_h = statistics.median(_height(ln) for ln in lines) or 1.0
    97	    spans: list[tuple[OcrLine, ...]] = []
    98	    for i in range(len(lines)):
    99	        spans.append((lines[i],))
   100	        for j in range(i + 1, min(i + max_join, len(lines))):
   101	            gap = _center_y(lines[j]) - _center_y(lines[j - 1])
   102	            if gap > 2.5 * med_h or gap < 0:
   103	                break
   104	            spans.append(tuple(lines[i : j + 1]))
   105	    return spans
   106	
   107	
   108	def score_texts(expected: str, candidate: str) -> int:
   109	    e, c = key(expected), key(candidate)
   110	    if not e or not c:
   111	        return 0
   112	    full = fuzz.ratio(e, c)
   113	    # When the candidate carries extra words (e.g. "... Aged 4 Years"), allow a substring match
   114	    # but discount it slightly so an exact full-line match wins ties.
   115	    partial = fuzz.partial_ratio(e, c) - 3 if len(c) > len(e) * 1.25 else 0
   116	    return int(max(full, partial))
   117	
   118	
   119	def best_span(expected: str, lines: list[OcrLine], *, max_join: int = 3) -> Candidate | None:
   120	    best: Candidate | None = None
   121	    for group in reading_order(lines).values():
   122	        for span in _spans(group, max_join):
   123	            text = collapse_ws(" ".join(ln.text for ln in span))
   124	            sc = score_texts(expected, text)
   125	            if best is None or sc > best.score or (sc == best.score and len(span) < len(best.lines)):
   126	                best = Candidate(
   127	                    text=text,
   128	                    score=sc,
   129	                    lines=span,
   130	                    identical=collapse_ws(expected) == text,
   131	                    fold_equal=fold(expected) == fold(text),
   132	                    key_equal=key(expected) == key(text),
   133	                )
   134	    return best
   135	
   136	
   137	def status_for(cand: Candidate | None, expected: str, *, review_at: int, mismatch_at: int) -> tuple[Status, str]:
   138	    """Map a candidate to a status and a plain-language note."""
   139	    if cand is None:
   140	        return Status.not_found, "No text was read from the label images."
   141	    if cand.identical:
   142	        return Status.match, "Exact match."
   143	    if cand.fold_equal:
   144	        if case_only_difference(expected, cand.text):
   145	            return Status.match, "Matches; only letter case differs on the label."
   146	        return Status.match, "Matches; only accents, quote style or spacing differ on the label."
   147	    if cand.key_equal:
   148	        return Status.needs_review, "Same letters and digits; punctuation differs. Confirm on the image."
   149	    if cand.score >= review_at:
   150	        return Status.needs_review, "Close match. Could be OCR noise or a real difference; compare with the image."
   151	    if cand.score >= mismatch_at:
   152	        return Status.mismatch, "The closest text on the label differs from the application."
   153	    return Status.not_found, "Nothing on the label resembles the application value."


===== FILE: app/pipeline/images.py (current text at 3d9bdad, line-numbered) =====
     1	"""Image intake: format sniffing, decompression-bomb guard, EXIF orientation, downscaling, and
     2	the coordinate transforms that map OCR boxes back to the oriented original image.
     3	
     4	Canonical space = the image after EXIF transpose, at its original resolution. Every box the API
     5	returns lives there.
     6	"""
     7	
     8	from __future__ import annotations
     9	
    10	from collections.abc import Sequence
    11	from dataclasses import dataclass
    12	from io import BytesIO
    13	
    14	import numpy as np
    15	from PIL import Image, ImageOps
    16	
    17	from app.schemas import Quad
    18	
    19	_SIGNATURES: tuple[tuple[bytes, str], ...] = (
    20	    (b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n", "png"),
    21	    (b"\xff\xd8\xff", "jpeg"),
    22	    (b"GIF87a", "gif"),
    23	    (b"GIF89a", "gif"),
    24	    (b"II*\x00", "tiff"),
    25	    (b"MM\x00*", "tiff"),
    26	    (b"BM", "bmp"),
    27	)
    28	SUPPORTED = ("png", "jpeg", "gif", "webp", "tiff", "bmp")
    29	
    30	
    31	class ImageError(Exception):
    32	    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, hint: str | None = None) -> None:
    33	        super().__init__(message)
    34	        self.code, self.message, self.hint = code, message, hint
    35	
    36	
    37	def sniff_format(data: bytes) -> str | None:
    38	    for sig, fmt in _SIGNATURES:
    39	        if data.startswith(sig):
    40	            return fmt
    41	    if data[:4] == b"RIFF" and data[8:12] == b"WEBP":
    42	        return "webp"
    43	    return None
    44	
    45	
    46	@dataclass
    47	class DecodedImage:
    48	    array: np.ndarray  # RGB uint8, downscaled working copy
    49	    width: int  # canonical (oriented original) width
    50	    height: int  # canonical height
    51	    scale: float  # working = canonical * scale
    52	    format: str
    53	    filename: str | None
    54	
    55	
    56	def decode_image(data: bytes, *, max_pixels: int, max_side: int, filename: str | None = None) -> DecodedImage:
    57	    fmt = sniff_format(data)
    58	    if fmt is None:
    59	        head = data[:5]
    60	        if head.startswith(b"%PDF"):
    61	            raise ImageError(
    62	                "unsupported_format",
    63	                "PDF files are not supported.",
    64	                "Export the label artwork as PNG or JPEG and upload that.",
    65	            )
    66	        if head.lstrip().startswith(b"<"):
    67	            raise ImageError(
    68	                "unsupported_format",
    69	                "SVG and other text-based files are not supported.",
    70	                "Upload PNG, JPEG, GIF, WebP, TIFF or BMP.",
    71	            )
    72	        if data[4:12] in (b"ftypheic", b"ftypheix", b"ftypmif1", b"ftypmsf1"):
    73	            raise ImageError(
    74	                "unsupported_format",
    75	                "HEIC photos are not supported.",
    76	                "On iPhone, share the photo as JPEG, or set Camera > Formats to Most Compatible.",
    77	            )
    78	        raise ImageError(
    79	            "unsupported_format", "This file is not a supported image.", "Upload PNG, JPEG, GIF, WebP, TIFF or BMP."
    80	        )
    81	    try:
    82	        im = Image.open(BytesIO(data))
    83	        w, h = im.size
    84	        if w * h > max_pixels:
    85	            raise ImageError(
    86	                "image_too_large",
    87	                f"This image is {w} x {h} pixels, above the {max_pixels // 1_000_000} megapixel limit.",
    88	                "Resize the image below the limit and upload it again.",
    89	            )
    90	        if fmt == "gif":
    91	            im.seek(0)
    92	        # The canonical size is the oriented original, fixed here from the header before any
    93	        # reduced-size decode can change im.size. EXIF orientations 5-8 swap width and height.
    94	        swapped = im.getexif().get(0x0112, 1) in (5, 6, 7, 8)
    95	        canon_w, canon_h = (h, w) if swapped else (w, h)
    96	        if fmt == "jpeg" and min(w, h) >= 2 * max_side:
    97	            # libjpeg decodes at 1/2, 1/4 or 1/8 size while keeping both sides at or above max_side:
    98	            # a 12 MP phone photo costs a quarter of the memory and time. Coordinates are unaffected
    99	            # because scale is computed against the canonical size recorded above.
   100	            im.draft("RGB", (max_side, max_side))
   101	        im = ImageOps.exif_transpose(im) or im
   102	        im = im.convert("RGB")
   103	    except ImageError:
   104	        raise
   105	    except Exception as exc:  # Pillow raises many types for corrupt data
   106	        raise ImageError(
   107	            "corrupt_image",
   108	            "This image could not be decoded.",
   109	            "The file may be truncated or corrupt. Re-export it and try again.",
   110	        ) from exc
   111	    scale = min(1.0, max_side / max(canon_w, canon_h))
   112	    target = (max(1, round(canon_w * scale)), max(1, round(canon_h * scale)))
   113	    if im.size != target:
   114	        im = im.resize(target, Image.LANCZOS)
   115	    return DecodedImage(array=np.asarray(im), width=canon_w, height=canon_h, scale=scale, format=fmt, filename=filename)
   116	
   117	
   118	def rotate_array(arr: np.ndarray, degrees: int) -> np.ndarray:
   119	    """Rotate counter-clockwise by 90, 180 or 270 degrees (numpy convention)."""
   120	    k = {0: 0, 90: 1, 180: 2, 270: 3}[degrees]
   121	    return np.ascontiguousarray(np.rot90(arr, k)) if k else arr
   122	
   123	
   124	def unrotate_point(x: float, y: float, degrees: int, rot_w: int, rot_h: int) -> tuple[float, float]:
   125	    """Map a point in a rotated array back to the unrotated array's coordinates.
   126	
   127	    rot_w, rot_h are the rotated array's width and height. Derived from numpy.rot90 semantics:
   128	    k=1 (90 CCW): rotated (x, y) came from original (rot_h - y, x)
   129	    k=2 (180):    original (rot_w - x, rot_h - y)
   130	    k=3 (270 CCW = 90 CW): original (y, rot_w - x)
   131	    """
   132	    if degrees == 0:
   133	        return x, y
   134	    if degrees == 90:
   135	        return rot_h - y, x
   136	    if degrees == 180:
   137	        return rot_w - x, rot_h - y
   138	    if degrees == 270:
   139	        return y, rot_w - x
   140	    raise ValueError(degrees)
   141	
   142	
   143	def to_canonical(
   144	    box: np.ndarray | Sequence[Sequence[float]], *, scale: float, degrees: int, rot_w: int, rot_h: int
   145	) -> Quad:
   146	    """OCR box (4 points, in the working array as fed to OCR, possibly rotated) -> canonical quad."""
   147	    pts = []
   148	    for x, y in box:
   149	        ux, uy = unrotate_point(float(x), float(y), degrees, rot_w, rot_h)
   150	        pts.append((round(ux / scale, 1), round(uy / scale, 1)))
   151	    return (pts[0], pts[1], pts[2], pts[3])


===== FILE: app/services.py (current text at 3d9bdad, line-numbered) =====
     1	"""Orchestration: bytes in, verified result out. The only module that touches both the OCR pool
     2	and the pure pipeline."""
     3	
     4	from __future__ import annotations
     5	
     6	import asyncio
     7	import time
     8	import uuid
     9	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    10	
    11	import numpy as np
    12	
    13	from app.config import Settings
    14	from app.ocr.base import RawLine
    15	from app.ocr.pool import OcrPool, Runner
    16	from app.pipeline.compare import compare
    17	from app.pipeline.extract import extract_fields
    18	from app.pipeline.images import DecodedImage, decode_image, rotate_array, to_canonical
    19	from app.pipeline.typeface import measure_line, to_gray
    20	from app.pipeline.warning import WarningSpan, find_warning
    21	from app.schemas import (
    22	    ApplicationFields,
    23	    EngineInfo,
    24	    ExtractResponse,
    25	    ImageInfo,
    26	    ImageQuality,
    27	    OcrLine,
    28	    Timing,
    29	    VerifyResponse,
    30	)
    31	
    32	
    33	def new_request_id() -> str:
    34	    return uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
    35	
    36	
    37	@dataclass
    38	class Upload:
    39	    data: bytes
    40	    filename: str | None
    41	
    42	
    43	@dataclass
    44	class ProcessedImage:
    45	    info: ImageInfo
    46	    lines: list[OcrLine]
    47	    queue_ms: int
    48	    ocr_ms: int
    49	    # Orientations the rotation retry read but did not keep (degrees -> lines in canonical
    50	    # coordinates). The warning rescue looks there first; it costs nothing.
    51	    alternates: dict[int, list[OcrLine]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    52	
    53	
    54	# One extra read for the warning rescue: the image, its upload, and the turn in degrees (None =
    55	# once more upright at bounded full resolution).
    56	RescueRead = tuple[ProcessedImage, Upload, int | None]
    57	
    58	
    59	def _to_lines(
    60	    raw: list[RawLine],
    61	    dec: DecodedImage,
    62	    index: int,
    63	    degrees: int,
    64	    rot_w: int,
    65	    rot_h: int,
    66	    arr: np.ndarray | None = None,
    67	) -> list[OcrLine]:
    68	    """Canonical lines from a read, with the type weight of each line measured on the array the
    69	    engine read (``arr``), so the warning report can judge bold type without the pixels."""
    70	    out: list[OcrLine] = []
    71	    gray = to_gray(arr) if arr is not None and raw else None
    72	    for r in raw:
    73	        lw = measure_line(gray, r.box, r.text) if gray is not None else None
    74	        out.append(
    75	            OcrLine(
    76	                image_index=index,
    77	                text=r.text,
    78	                confidence=round(r.confidence, 4),
    79	                box=to_canonical(r.box, scale=dec.scale, degrees=degrees, rot_w=rot_w, rot_h=rot_h),
    80	                weight=lw.weight if lw else None,
    81	                weight_head=lw.head if lw else None,
    82	                weight_tail=lw.tail if lw else None,
    83	            )
    84	        )
    85	    return out
    86	
    87	
    88	def _read_score(raw: list[RawLine]) -> float:
    89	    """How much confident text was read: sum of confidences of lines at or above 0.5."""
    90	    return float(sum(r.confidence for r in raw if r.confidence >= 0.5))
    91	
    92	
    93	def _quality(raw: list[RawLine]) -> ImageQuality:
    94	    if not raw:
    95	        return ImageQuality(
    96	            mean_confidence=0.0,
    97	            line_count=0,
    98	            readable=False,
    99	            reason="No text was detected. The image may be blank, too small, or not a label.",
   100	        )
   101	    mean = float(np.mean([r.confidence for r in raw]))
   102	    if mean < 0.6:
   103	        return ImageQuality(
   104	            mean_confidence=round(mean, 3),
   105	            line_count=len(raw),
   106	            readable=False,
   107	            reason="Text was detected but read with low confidence. The image may be blurry, "
   108	            "low contrast, or photographed at an angle. Request a clearer image.",
   109	        )
   110	    return ImageQuality(mean_confidence=round(mean, 3), line_count=len(raw), readable=True)
   111	
   112	
   113	async def process_image(
   114	    up: Upload, index: int, settings: Settings, run: Runner, *, queue_ms: int = 0
   115	) -> ProcessedImage:
   116	    """Decode one upload and read it on the given slot, retrying sideways orientations when the
   117	    first read is poor (the one failure mode the engine does not recover on its own). The reads
   118	    that lose the retry are kept as alternates rather than thrown away."""
   119	    dec = decode_image(
   120	        up.data, max_pixels=settings.max_image_pixels, max_side=settings.ocr_max_side, filename=up.filename
   121	    )
   122	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
   123	    raw = await run(dec.array)
   124	    h, w = dec.array.shape[:2]
   125	    reads: dict[int, tuple[list[RawLine], int, int, np.ndarray]] = {0: (raw, w, h, dec.array)}
   126	    chosen = 0
   127	    mean = float(np.mean([r.confidence for r in raw])) if raw else 0.0
   128	    if mean < settings.ocr_low_conf_retry or len(raw) < settings.ocr_min_lines_retry:
   129	        for degrees in (90, 270):
   130	            rot = rotate_array(dec.array, degrees)
   131	            raw2 = await run(rot)
   132	            reads[degrees] = (raw2, rot.shape[1], rot.shape[0], rot)
   133	            if _read_score(raw2) > _read_score(reads[chosen][0]) * 1.15:
   134	                chosen = degrees
   135	    ocr_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
   136	    raw_best, rw, rh, arr_best = reads[chosen]
   137	    lines = _to_lines(raw_best, dec, index, chosen, rw, rh, arr_best)
   138	    alternates = {d: _to_lines(r, dec, index, d, w2, h2, a2) for d, (r, w2, h2, a2) in reads.items() if d != chosen}
   139	    info = ImageInfo(
   140	        index=index,
   141	        filename=up.filename,
   142	        width=dec.width,
   143	        height=dec.height,
   144	        format=dec.format,
   145	        rotated_degrees=chosen,
   146	        quality=_quality(raw_best),
   147	    )
   148	    return ProcessedImage(info=info, lines=lines, queue_ms=queue_ms, ocr_ms=ocr_ms, alternates=alternates)
   149	
   150	
   151	async def process_images(
   152	    uploads: list[Upload], settings: Settings, pool: OcrPool, *, interactive: bool
   153	) -> list[ProcessedImage]:
   154	    """Interactive: each image takes its own slot so a front+back pair costs one image-time, and a
   155	    statement that went unread gets one more round of reads (``_rescue_warning``).
   156	    Batch: one slot is held for all the images of the request, so it can never be refused halfway,
   157	    and every image is read exactly once. The batch screen reads images before it knows which
   158	    application they belong to, so a front label, which carries no statement by design, must not
   159	    trigger re-reads (measured: they cut batch throughput to a quarter, docs/LOADTEST.md)."""
   160	    if interactive:
   161	
   162	        async def one(i: int, u: Upload) -> ProcessedImage:
   163	            async with pool.slot(interactive=True) as (run, queue_ms):
   164	                return await process_image(u, i, settings, run, queue_ms=queue_ms)
   165	
   166	        processed = list(await asyncio.gather(*(one(i, u) for i, u in enumerate(uploads))))
   167	        if settings.warning_rescue:
   168	            await _rescue_warning(uploads, processed, settings, pool)
   169	        return processed
   170	    async with pool.slot(interactive=False) as (run, queue_ms):
   171	        return [await process_image(u, i, settings, run, queue_ms=queue_ms) for i, u in enumerate(uploads)]
   172	
   173	
   174	async def _rescue_warning(
   175	    uploads: list[Upload], processed: list[ProcessedImage], settings: Settings, pool: OcrPool
   176	) -> None:
   177	    """Two layouts hide the statement from the ordinary read: printed sideways along the edge of a
   178	    small label that otherwise reads perfectly upright (so the low-confidence retry never fires),
   179	    and printed in small type on large artwork (unreadable once the image is scaled to the working
   180	    size). When no usable statement was found (none, or a span far from the required text): first
   181	    look at the orientations the rotation retry already read, then run ONE round of extra reads in
   182	    parallel on their own slots, at most one per worker: turned 270 then 90 degrees and, for large
   183	    artwork, once more at bounded full resolution (that one first when the heading was seen but the
   184	    body was not). One round keeps the interactive path inside its time budget whatever the number
   185	    of workers, and the round is the same for the same input and configuration. The best span that
   186	    beats the upright read is kept. A read that cannot get a slot is refused like any other
   187	    interactive read (BusyError, HTTP 429), never skipped silently."""
   188	    upright = find_warning([ln for p in processed for ln in p.lines])
   189	    floor = upright.similarity if upright else 0.0
   190	    if floor >= settings.warning_rescue_below:
   191	        return
   192	    for p in processed:  # free: what the retry read and did not keep
   193	        for lines in p.alternates.values():
   194	            span = find_warning(lines)
   195	            if span is not None and span.similarity > floor:
   196	                _adopt(p, span)
   197	                floor = span.similarity
   198	    if floor >= settings.warning_rescue_below:
   199	        return
   200	    plans: list[list[RescueRead]] = []
   201	    for up, p in zip(uploads, processed, strict=True):
   202	        if not p.info.quality.readable:
   203	            continue  # nothing legible at all; turning it will not help
   204	        seen = {p.info.rotated_degrees, *p.alternates}
   205	        # 270 first: it unrolls a statement printed up a label's right edge, reading bottom to top,
   206	        # which is how three of the four such labels in the real sample print it (docs/EVAL_REAL.md).
   207	        turns: list[RescueRead] = [(p, up, d) for d in (270, 90) if d not in seen]
   208	        side = min(settings.warning_rescue_max_side, max(p.info.width, p.info.height))
   209	        full: list[RescueRead] = [(p, up, None)] if side > settings.ocr_max_side else []
   210	        heading_seen = find_warning(p.lines) is not None
   211	        plans.append(full + turns if heading_seen else turns + full)
   212	    # The first read of every image before any second read, so a front+back pair shares the round.
   213	    depth = max((len(plan) for plan in plans), default=0)
   214	    ordered = [plan[k] for k in range(depth) for plan in plans if k < len(plan)]
   215	    reads = ordered[: pool.workers]
   216	    if not reads:
   217	        return
   218	    spans = await asyncio.gather(*(_rescue_read(p, up, degrees, settings, pool) for p, up, degrees in reads))
   219	    best: dict[int, WarningSpan] = {}
   220	    for (p, _, _), span in zip(reads, spans, strict=True):
   221	        idx = p.info.index
   222	        if span is not None and span.similarity > floor and (idx not in best or span.similarity > best[idx].similarity):
   223	            best[idx] = span
   224	    for p in processed:
   225	        if p.info.index in best:
   226	            _adopt(p, best[p.info.index])
   227	
   228	
   229	def _adopt(p: ProcessedImage, span: WarningSpan) -> None:
   230	    """Add a rescued statement to an image's lines and drop what the kept read had in the same
   231	    places: the same strip of print read in another orientation is garbage, and two readings of
   232	    one region must not both be there."""
   233	    p.lines = [*(ln for ln in p.lines if not any(_same_place(ln, s) for s in span.lines)), *span.lines]
   234	
   235	
   236	def _same_place(a: OcrLine, b: OcrLine) -> bool:
   237	    """True when the boxes cover mostly the same area (more than half of the smaller one)."""
   238	    ax = [pt[0] for pt in a.box]
   239	    ay = [pt[1] for pt in a.box]
   240	    bx = [pt[0] for pt in b.box]
   241	    by = [pt[1] for pt in b.box]
   242	    inter_w = max(0.0, min(max(ax), max(bx)) - max(min(ax), min(bx)))
   243	    inter_h = max(0.0, min(max(ay), max(by)) - max(min(ay), min(by)))
   244	    area_a = max(1.0, (max(ax) - min(ax)) * (max(ay) - min(ay)))
   245	    area_b = max(1.0, (max(bx) - min(bx)) * (max(by) - min(by)))
   246	    return inter_w * inter_h / min(area_a, area_b) > 0.5
   247	
   248	
   249	async def _rescue_read(
   250	    p: ProcessedImage, up: Upload, degrees: int | None, settings: Settings, pool: OcrPool
   251	) -> WarningSpan | None:
   252	    async with pool.slot(interactive=True) as (run, queue_ms):
   253	        t0 = time.perf_counter()
   254	        if degrees is None:
   255	            side = min(settings.warning_rescue_max_side, max(p.info.width, p.info.height))
   256	            dec = decode_image(up.data, max_pixels=settings.max_image_pixels, max_side=side, filename=up.filename)
   257	            arr, turn = dec.array, 0
   258	        else:
   259	            dec = decode_image(
   260	                up.data, max_pixels=settings.max_image_pixels, max_side=settings.ocr_max_side, filename=up.filename
   261	            )
   262	            arr, turn = rotate_array(dec.array, degrees), degrees
   263	        raw = await run(arr)
   264	        lines = _to_lines(raw, dec, p.info.index, turn, arr.shape[1], arr.shape[0], arr)
   265	        p.queue_ms += queue_ms
   266	        p.ocr_ms += int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
   267	    return find_warning(lines)
   268	
   269	
   270	def engine_info(pool: OcrPool) -> EngineInfo:
   271	    return EngineInfo(name="rapidocr-onnxruntime", models=pool.info(), workers=pool.workers)
   272	
   273	
   274	def _timing(t0: float, processed: list[ProcessedImage]) -> Timing:
   275	    return Timing(
   276	        total_ms=int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000),
   277	        queue_ms=max((p.queue_ms for p in processed), default=0),
   278	        ocr_ms=[p.ocr_ms for p in processed],
   279	    )
   280	
   281	
   282	async def verify(
   283	    app: ApplicationFields,
   284	    uploads: list[Upload],
   285	    settings: Settings,
   286	    pool: OcrPool,
   287	    *,
   288	    interactive: bool = True,
   289	    request_id: str | None = None,
   290	) -> VerifyResponse:
   291	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
   292	    processed = await process_images(uploads, settings, pool, interactive=interactive)
   293	    lines = [ln for p in processed for ln in p.lines]
   294	    images = [p.info for p in processed]
   295	    result = compare(app, lines, images, settings)
   296	    return VerifyResponse(
   297	        request_id=request_id or new_request_id(),
   298	        application=app,
   299	        images=images,
   300	        lines=lines,
   301	        timing=_timing(t0, processed),
   302	        engine=engine_info(pool),
   303	        **result.model_dump(),
   304	    )
   305	
   306	
   307	async def extract(
   308	    uploads: list[Upload],
   309	    settings: Settings,
   310	    pool: OcrPool,
   311	    *,
   312	    interactive: bool = False,
   313	    request_id: str | None = None,
   314	) -> ExtractResponse:
   315	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
   316	    processed = await process_images(uploads, settings, pool, interactive=interactive)
   317	    lines = [ln for p in processed for ln in p.lines]
   318	    return ExtractResponse(
   319	        request_id=request_id or new_request_id(),
   320	        images=[p.info for p in processed],
   321	        lines=lines,
   322	        fields=extract_fields(lines),
   323	        timing=_timing(t0, processed),
   324	        engine=engine_info(pool),
   325	    )

     1	"""Pydantic models shared by the API and the pipeline.
     2	
     3	Coordinates: every box is a quadrilateral of four (x, y) points in the *oriented original
     4	image* space (after EXIF transpose, before any downscaling). The client draws in that space.
     5	"""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations

     9	from enum import StrEnum

    13	Point = tuple[float, float]
    14	Quad = tuple[Point, Point, Point, Point]

    19	    wine = "wine"
    20	    malt = "malt"

    25	    needs_review = "needs_review"
    26	    mismatch = "mismatch"

    28	    not_checked = "not_checked"
    29	    info = "info"

    33	    ready_for_approval = "ready_for_approval"
    34	    needs_review = "needs_review"
    35	    issues_found = "issues_found"
    36	    unreadable = "unreadable"
    37	
    38	
    39	class ApplicationFields(BaseModel):
    40	    """What the agent has in front of them from the COLA application, as written."""
    41	
    42	    model_config = ConfigDict(str_strip_whitespace=True)
    43	
    44	    application_id: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=64, description="Agent's reference, e.g. a TTB ID")
    45	    beverage_type: BeverageType
    46	    brand_name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=200)
    47	    class_type: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=200)
    48	    alcohol_content: str | None = Field(
    49	        default=None, max_length=100, description='As written, e.g. "45% Alc./Vol. (90 Proof)"'
    50	    )
    51	    net_contents: str | None = Field(
    52	        default=None,
    53	        max_length=100,
    54	        description='As written, e.g. "750 mL". May be blank (the COLA form carries none): the result then '
    55	        "shows what the label says and asks the agent to confirm it",
    56	    )
    57	    bottler: str | None = Field(
    58	        default=None, max_length=300, description="Name and address of bottler/producer/importer"
    59	    )
    60	    country_of_origin: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=100)
    61	    imported: bool = False
    62	
    63	
    64	class OcrLine(BaseModel):
    65	    image_index: int = Field(ge=0, le=64)
    66	    text: str = Field(max_length=1000)
    67	    confidence: float = Field(ge=0, le=1)
    68	    box: Quad
    69	    # Type weight measured from the pixels (stroke width over type height; app/pipeline/typeface.py).
    70	    # None when the print was too small or faint to measure. head/tail split a line that carries
    71	    # the warning heading into the heading and the rest.
    72	    weight: float | None = Field(default=None, ge=0, le=2)
    73	    weight_head: float | None = Field(default=None, ge=0, le=2)
    74	    weight_tail: float | None = Field(default=None, ge=0, le=2)
    75	
    76	
    77	class Evidence(BaseModel):
    78	    image_index: int
    79	    box: Quad
    80	    text: str
    81	
    82	
    83	class Check(BaseModel):

    85	    label: str

    87	    expected: str | None = None
    88	    found: str | None = None
    89	    score: float | None = Field(default=None, description="0-100 similarity where applicable")
    90	    note: str = ""
    91	    rule: str | None = Field(default=None, description="Regulatory citation where applicable")
    92	    evidence: list[Evidence] = Field(default_factory=list)
    93	
    94	
    95	class WarningReport(BaseModel):
    96	    present: bool

    98	    assessment: str = Field(
    99	        default="absent",
   100	        description="exact | noise | wording | absent | not_required (how the read text relates to 16.21; "
   101	        "exact ignores letter case and spacing, the anchor's capitals are anchor_caps)",
   102	    )
   103	    similarity: float

   105	    diff: str | None = Field(default=None, description="Compact word diff: -expected +found")
   106	    anchor_caps: Status
   107	    anchor_bold: Status
   108	    body_not_bold: Status
   109	    evidence: list[Evidence] = Field(default_factory=list)
   110	    notes: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
   111	    rule: str = "27 CFR 16.21 (text), 16.22 (format)"
   112	
   113	
   114	class ImageQuality(BaseModel):
   115	    mean_confidence: float
   116	    line_count: int
   117	    readable: bool

   119

   152	    largest_text: str | None = Field(default=None, description="Tallest line, usually the brand")
   153

     1	"""Application settings, loaded from environment variables (prefix TTB_).
     2	
     3	Every limit that a reviewer might probe is here, named, and documented in LIMITS.md.
     4	"""
     5	
     6	from __future__ import annotations
     7	
     8	import os
     9	from functools import lru_cache
    10	from pathlib import Path
    11	
    12	from pydantic import Field
    13	from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict
    14	
    15	REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    16	MODELS_DIR = REPO_ROOT / "app" / "models"
    17	STATIC_DIR = REPO_ROOT / "app" / "static"
    18	
    19	
    20	class Settings(BaseSettings):
    21	    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(env_prefix="TTB_", env_file=".env", extra="ignore")
    22	
    23	    # --- deployment identity
    24	    app_name: str = "Label Check (prototype)"
    25	    agency_name: str | None = Field(default=None, description="Optional agency name for internal branding")
    26	    git_sha: str = Field(default_factory=lambda: os.environ.get("GIT_SHA", "dev"))
    27	    trust_proxy: bool = Field(default=False, description="Trust the first X-Forwarded-For hop for client identity")
    28	
    29	    # --- OCR engine
    30	    ocr_workers: int = Field(default_factory=lambda: max(1, min(8, os.cpu_count() or 1)))
    31	    ocr_intra_op_threads: int = 1
    32	    ocr_det_limit_side_len: int = 640
    33	    ocr_rec_batch_num: int = 16
    34	    ocr_use_cls: bool = False
    35	    ocr_ascii_alphabet: bool = Field(
    36	        default=True,
    37	        description="Decode with the recognizer's alphabet restricted to printable ASCII (English label text)",
    38	    )
    39	    ocr_max_side: int = Field(default=1280, description="Images are downscaled to this longest side before OCR")
    40	    ocr_low_conf_retry: float = Field(default=0.80, description="Mean confidence below which a 90-degree retry runs")
    41	    ocr_min_lines_retry: int = Field(default=3, description="Fewer detected lines than this also triggers the retry")
    42	    warning_rescue: bool = Field(
    43	        default=True,
    44	        description="Interactive requests only: when no usable warning statement is found, one more round "
    45	        "of reads (at most one per worker, in parallel): the images turned sideways, since small labels "
    46	        "print the statement vertically along an edge, and large artwork once at full resolution. "
    47	        "Batch requests read every image exactly once",
    48	    )
    49	    warning_rescue_below: float = Field(
    50	        default=0.5,
    51	        description="Similarity of the best upright statement below which the extra round runs "
    52	        "(a garbled but present statement scores higher and would not be helped)",
    53	    )
    54	    warning_rescue_max_side: int = Field(
    55	        default=2048,
    56	        description="Longest side for the one full-resolution re-read of large artwork whose statement "
    57	        "was unreadable at the working size",
    58	    )
    59	    # --- type weight of the warning statement (docs/DECISIONS.md D-044; calibrated on the synthetic
    60	    # corpus, where a bold heading over a regular body measures 1.18-1.28 and all-bold 1.04; strokes under
    61	    # 3.5 px or type under 24 px are not measured, see app/pipeline/typeface.py)
    62	    type_weight_heading_min_ratio: float = Field(
    63	        default=1.15, description="Heading stroke weight over body stroke weight at or above which the heading is bold"
    64	    )
    65	    type_weight_same_max_ratio: float = Field(
    66	        default=1.05, description="At or below this ratio the heading and the body are the same weight"
    67	    )
    68	    models_dir: Path = MODELS_DIR
    69	
    70	    # --- request limits (see LIMITS.md)
    71	    max_image_bytes: int = 10 * 1024 * 1024
    72	    max_request_bytes: int = 40 * 1024 * 1024
    73	    max_image_pixels: int = 25_000_000
    74	    max_images_per_application: int = 6
    75	    max_compare_items: int = 100
    76	    max_csv_rows: int = 5000
    77	    max_csv_bytes: int = 2 * 1024 * 1024
    78	    per_client_inflight: int = 4
    79	    global_inflight: int = Field(default=24, description="Concurrent metered requests across all clients")
    80	    interactive_wait_seconds: float = Field(
    81	        default=8.0, description="How long an interactive request may wait for a worker slot"
    82	    )
    83	
    84	    # --- matching thresholds (tuned against tests/fixtures and real labels; see docs/EVAL_REAL.md)
    85	    match_review_threshold: int = Field(
    86	        default=80,
    87	        description="Fuzzy score at/above which a non-exact match is Needs review rather than a mismatch "
    88	        "(decorative fonts on real labels read at 80-89 while genuinely different names score under 70)",
    89	    )
    90	    match_mismatch_threshold: int = Field(default=70, description="Below this the best candidate counts as Not found")
    91	    warning_mismatch_similarity: float = Field(
    92	        default=0.80, description="Below this the warning is treated as wording differs"
    93	    )
    94	    net_contents_tolerance: float = Field(default=0.01, description="Relative tolerance for unit-conversion rounding")
    95	
    96	    @property
    97	    def static_dir(self) -> Path:
    98	        return STATIC_DIR
    99	
   100	
   101	@lru_cache
   102	def get_settings() -> Settings:
   103	    return Settings()

     1	"""CSV intake for batch mode: tolerant of Excel exports, strict about the result.
     2	
     3	Accepts UTF-8 (with or without BOM) or cp1252; sniffs comma, semicolon and tab delimiters; maps

     5	single-application screen uses; returns per-row errors instead of failing the whole file.

   111	@dataclass
   112	class CsvRow:
   113	    row_number: int
   114	    application: ApplicationFields | None
   115	    images: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
   116	    errors: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

   119	@dataclass
   120	class CsvResult:
   121	    rows: list[CsvRow]
   122	    columns: list[str]
   123	    unmapped_columns: list[str]
   124	    delimiter: str
   125	    warnings: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

   127	
   128	def _norm_header(h: str) -> str:
   129	    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", h.strip().lower().lstrip("﻿")).strip("_")
   130	
   131	
   132	def _decode(data: bytes) -> tuple[str, str | None]:
   133	    for enc in ("utf-8-sig", "utf-8"):
   134	        try:
   135	            return data.decode(enc), None
   136	        except UnicodeDecodeError:
   137	            continue
   138	    return data.decode("cp1252", errors="replace"), "File was not UTF-8; decoded as Windows-1252."
   139	
   140	
   141	def _beverage(value: str) -> BeverageType | None:
   142	    v = value.strip().lower()
   143	    if v in ("spirits", "spirit", "distilled spirits", "ds"):
   144	        return BeverageType.spirits
   145	    if v in ("wine", "w"):
   146	        return BeverageType.wine
   147	    if v in ("malt", "malt beverage", "beer", "mb"):
   148	        return BeverageType.malt
   149	    for pat, bev in _BEV:
   150	        if pat.search(v):
   151	            return bev
   152	    return None
   153	
   154	
   155	def template_csv() -> str:
   156	    buf = io.StringIO()
   157	    w = csv.writer(buf, lineterminator="\r\n")
   158	    w.writerow(TEMPLATE_HEADER)
   159	    w.writerow(TEMPLATE_EXAMPLE)
   160	    return buf.getvalue()
   161	
   162	
   163	def parse_csv(data: bytes, *, max_rows: int) -> CsvResult:
   164	    text, warn = _decode(data)
   165	    warnings = [warn] if warn else []
   166	    sample = text[:4096]
   167	    try:
   168	        dialect = csv.Sniffer().sniff(sample, delimiters=",;\t|")
   169	        delimiter = dialect.delimiter
   170	    except csv.Error:
   171	        delimiter = ","
   172	    reader = csv.reader(io.StringIO(text), delimiter=delimiter)
   173	    try:
   174	        raw_header = next(reader)
   175	    except StopIteration:
   176	        return CsvResult(rows=[], columns=[], unmapped_columns=[], delimiter=delimiter, warnings=["The file is empty."])
   177	    headers = [_norm_header(h) for h in raw_header]
   178	    mapping = {i: _ALIASES[h] for i, h in enumerate(headers) if h in _ALIASES}
   179	    unmapped = [raw_header[i] for i, h in enumerate(headers) if h not in _ALIASES and h]
   180	    if "brand_name" not in mapping.values():
   181	        warnings.append("No brand name column was recognized. Expected a header like 'brand_name' or 'Brand'.")
   182	
   183	    rows: list[CsvRow] = []
   184	    for n, rec in enumerate(reader, start=2):
   185	        if not any(cell.strip() for cell in rec):
   186	            continue
   187	        if len(rows) >= max_rows:
   188	            warnings.append(f"Only the first {max_rows} rows were read.")
   189	            break
   190	        fields: dict[str, str] = {}
   191	        for i, cell in enumerate(rec):
   192	            key = mapping.get(i)
   193	            if key and cell.strip():
   194	                fields[key] = cell.strip() if key not in fields else fields[key] + " " + cell.strip()
   195	        errors: list[str] = []
   196	        images = [s.strip() for s in re.split(r"[;|]", fields.pop("images", "")) if s.strip()]
   197	        bev_raw = fields.pop("beverage_type", "")
   198	        bev = _beverage(bev_raw) if bev_raw else None
   199	        if bev is None:
   200	            bev = _beverage(fields.get("class_type", "")) or None
   201	            if bev is None:
   202	                errors.append("beverage_type missing or not recognized (use spirits, wine or malt).")
   203	        imported = fields.pop("imported", "").strip().lower() in _TRUE
   204	        app: ApplicationFields | None = None
   205	        if not errors:
   206	            try:
   207	                app = ApplicationFields(beverage_type=bev, imported=imported, **fields)
   208	            except ValidationError as exc:
   209	                errors.extend(f"{'.'.join(str(p) for p in e['loc'])}: {e['msg']}" for e in exc.errors())
   210	        rows.append(CsvRow(row_number=n, application=app, images=images, errors=errors))
   211	    return CsvResult(
   212	        rows=rows,
   213	        columns=list(dict.fromkeys(mapping.values())),
   214	        unmapped_columns=unmapped,
   215	        delimiter=delimiter,
   216	        warnings=warnings,
   217	    )


===== FILE: app/security.py (current text at 3d9bdad, line-numbered) =====
     1	"""Security headers, request ids, body-size guard, admission caps before body parsing, error envelope."""
     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	import logging
     6	import threading
     7	import time
     8	import uuid
     9	from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable, Mapping
    10	
    11	from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request, Response
    12	from fastapi.exceptions import RequestValidationError
    13	from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
    14	from starlette.middleware.base import BaseHTTPMiddleware
    15	from starlette.types import ASGIApp
    16	
    17	from app.config import Settings
    18	from app.ocr.pool import BusyError
    19	from app.pipeline.images import ImageError
    20	from app.schemas import ErrorResponse
    21	
    22	log = logging.getLogger("app.request")
    23	
    24	CSP = (
    25	    "default-src 'self'; img-src 'self' blob: data:; style-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; "
    26	    "connect-src 'self'; font-src 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'self'"
    27	)
    28	SECURITY_HEADERS = {
    29	    "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",
    30	    "Referrer-Policy": "no-referrer",
    31	    "Permissions-Policy": "camera=(self), microphone=(), geolocation=()",
    32	    "Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy": "same-origin",
    33	    "Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy": "same-origin",
    34	    "Content-Security-Policy": CSP,
    35	}
    36	# Endpoints that parse bodies and cost CPU; capped before the body is read.
    37	METERED_PREFIXES = ("/api/v1/verify", "/api/v1/extract", "/api/v1/compare", "/api/v1/csv/parse")
    38	
    39	
    40	def request_id_of(request: Request) -> str:
    41	    return getattr(request.state, "request_id", "") or ""
    42	
    43	
    44	def apply_security_headers(response: Response) -> Response:
    45	    for k, v in SECURITY_HEADERS.items():
    46	        response.headers[k] = v
    47	    response.headers.setdefault("Cache-Control", "no-store")
    48	    return response
    49	
    50	
    51	def error_response(
    52	    status: int,
    53	    code: str,
    54	    message: str,
    55	    hint: str | None = None,
    56	    request_id: str | None = None,
    57	    headers: Mapping[str, str] | None = None,
    58	) -> JSONResponse:
    59	    body = ErrorResponse(code=code, message=message, hint=hint, request_id=request_id).model_dump()
    60	    resp = JSONResponse(status_code=status, content=body, headers=dict(headers or {}))
    61	    if request_id:
    62	        resp.headers["X-Request-ID"] = request_id
    63	    apply_security_headers(resp)
    64	    return resp
    65	
    66	
    67	class AdmissionLimiter:
    68	    """Per-client and global caps on concurrent metered requests, enforced before body parsing.
    69	
    70	    Client identity: the connection address, or, when ``trust_proxy`` is set, the LAST value of
    71	    X-Forwarded-For, which is the one appended by the trusted ingress (the first value can be
    72	    forged by the client).
    73	    """
    74	
    75	    def __init__(self, settings: Settings) -> None:
    76	        self.per_client = settings.per_client_inflight
    77	        self.global_limit = settings.global_inflight
    78	        self.trust_proxy = settings.trust_proxy
    79	        self._lock = threading.Lock()
    80	        self._counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    81	        self._total = 0
    82	
    83	    def client_id(self, request: Request) -> str:
    84	        if self.trust_proxy:
    85	            fwd = request.headers.get("x-forwarded-for")
    86	            if fwd:
    87	                return fwd.split(",")[-1].strip()
    88	        return request.client.host if request.client else "unknown"
    89	
    90	    def acquire(self, request: Request) -> str | None:
    91	        """Returns None when admitted, else the reason code."""
    92	        cid = self.client_id(request)
    93	        with self._lock:
    94	            if self._total >= self.global_limit:
    95	                return "service_busy"
    96	            if self._counts.get(cid, 0) >= self.per_client:
    97	                return "too_many_inflight"
    98	            self._counts[cid] = self._counts.get(cid, 0) + 1
    99	            self._total += 1
   100	        request.state.admitted_client = cid
   101	        return None
   102	
   103	    def release(self, request: Request) -> None:
   104	        cid = getattr(request.state, "admitted_client", None)
   105	        if cid is None:
   106	            return
   107	        request.state.admitted_client = None  # a second release for the same request is a no-op
   108	        with self._lock:
   109	            self._counts[cid] -= 1
   110	            if self._counts[cid] <= 0:
   111	                del self._counts[cid]
   112	            self._total -= 1
   113	
   114	    @property
   115	    def in_flight(self) -> int:
   116	        return self._total
   117	
   118	
   119	class SecurityMiddleware(BaseHTTPMiddleware):
   120	    """Request id, Content-Length guard, admission caps, security headers, Server-Timing.
   121	    Logs method, path, status and milliseconds only."""
   122	
   123	    def __init__(self, app: ASGIApp, settings: Settings, limiter: AdmissionLimiter) -> None:
   124	        super().__init__(app)
   125	        self.settings = settings
   126	        self.limiter = limiter
   127	
   128	    async def dispatch(self, request: Request, call_next: Callable[[Request], Awaitable[Response]]) -> Response:
   129	        rid = uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
   130	        request.state.request_id = rid
   131	        t0 = time.perf_counter()
   132	        metered = request.method == "POST" and request.url.path.startswith(METERED_PREFIXES)
   133	
   134	        def early(resp: Response) -> Response:  # responses that never reach a route still carry timing
   135	            resp.headers["Server-Timing"] = f"total;dur={(time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000:.0f}"
   136	            return resp
   137	
   138	        if request.method in ("POST", "PUT", "PATCH"):
   139	            length = request.headers.get("content-length")
   140	            if length is None:
   141	                return early(
   142	                    error_response(411, "length_required", "Uploads must declare Content-Length.", request_id=rid)
   143	                )
   144	            try:
   145	                declared = int(length)
   146	                if declared < 0:
   147	                    raise ValueError
   148	            except ValueError:
   149	                return early(
   150	                    error_response(400, "bad_request", "Content-Length is not a valid number.", request_id=rid)
   151	                )
   152	            if declared > self.settings.max_request_bytes:
   153	                mb = self.settings.max_request_bytes // (1024 * 1024)
   154	                return early(
   155	                    error_response(
   156	                        413,
   157	                        "request_too_large",
   158	                        f"The upload is larger than the {mb} MB request limit.",
   159	                        "Send fewer or smaller images per request.",
   160	                        request_id=rid,
   161	                    )
   162	                )
   163	        admitted = False
   164	        if metered:
   165	            reason = self.limiter.acquire(request)
   166	            if reason == "too_many_inflight":
   167	                return early(
   168	                    error_response(
   169	                        429,
   170	                        reason,
   171	                        "Too many requests in flight from this client.",
   172	                        "Limit concurrent uploads and retry shortly.",
   173	                        request_id=rid,
   174	                        headers={"Retry-After": "1"},
   175	                    )
   176	                )
   177	            if reason == "service_busy":
   178	                return early(
   179	                    error_response(
   180	                        503,
   181	                        reason,
   182	                        "The service is handling as many requests as it can right now.",
   183	                        "Retry in a moment.",
   184	                        request_id=rid,
   185	                        headers={"Retry-After": "2"},
   186	                    )
   187	                )
   188	            admitted = True
   189	        try:
   190	            response = await call_next(request)
   191	        finally:
   192	            if admitted:
   193	                self.limiter.release(request)
   194	        response.headers["X-Request-ID"] = rid
   195	        response.headers["Server-Timing"] = f"total;dur={(time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000:.0f}"
   196	        apply_security_headers(response)
   197	        if request.url.path.startswith("/api/"):
   198	            log.info(
   199	                "%s %s %s %.0fms rid=%s",
   200	                request.method,
   201	                request.url.path,
   202	                response.status_code,
   203	                (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000,
   204	                rid,
   205	            )
   206	        return response
   207	
   208	
   209	def install_error_handlers(app: FastAPI) -> None:
   210	    @app.exception_handler(ImageError)
   211	    async def _image_error(request: Request, exc: ImageError) -> JSONResponse:
   212	        status = {"unsupported_format": 415, "image_too_large": 413}.get(exc.code, 400)
   213	        return error_response(status, exc.code, exc.message, exc.hint, request_id_of(request))
   214	
   215	    @app.exception_handler(BusyError)
   216	    async def _busy(request: Request, exc: BusyError) -> JSONResponse:
   217	        return error_response(
   218	            429,
   219	            "busy",
   220	            "The verification service is at capacity right now.",
   221	            "Retry in a moment; batch clients back off automatically.",
   222	            request_id_of(request),
   223	            headers={"Retry-After": str(exc.retry_after)},
   224	        )
   225	
   226	    @app.exception_handler(HTTPException)
   227	    async def _http(request: Request, exc: HTTPException) -> JSONResponse:
   228	        detail = exc.detail if isinstance(exc.detail, str) else "error"
   229	        messages = {
   230	            "not_ready": ("The OCR engine is still starting.", "Retry in a few seconds."),
   231	        }
   232	        hint: str | None
   233	        if detail.startswith("warm-up failed"):
   234	            message, hint = detail, "The service cannot start; see the container logs."
   235	        else:
   236	            message, hint = messages.get(detail, (detail, None))
   237	        return error_response(exc.status_code, detail, message, hint, request_id_of(request), headers=exc.headers)
   238	
   239	    @app.exception_handler(RequestValidationError)
   240	    async def _validation(request: Request, exc: RequestValidationError) -> JSONResponse:
   241	        first = exc.errors()[0] if exc.errors() else {}
   242	        loc = ".".join(str(p) for p in first.get("loc", []) if p not in ("body",))
   243	        return error_response(
   244	            422,
   245	            "invalid_request",
   246	            f"{loc or 'request'}: {first.get('msg', 'invalid')}",
   247	            "Check the field names and values.",
   248	            request_id_of(request),
   249	        )
   250	
   251	    @app.exception_handler(Exception)
   252	    async def _unhandled(request: Request, exc: Exception) -> JSONResponse:
   253	        log.exception("unhandled error rid=%s", request_id_of(request))
   254	        return error_response(
   255	            500,
   256	            "internal_error",
   257	            "Something went wrong on our side.",
   258	            "Try again; if it persists, report the request id.",
   259	            request_id_of(request),
   260	        )


===== FILE: app/main.py (current text at 3d9bdad, line-numbered) =====
     1	"""FastAPI application factory."""
     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	import asyncio
     6	import logging
     7	from collections.abc import AsyncIterator, Callable
     8	from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
     9	
    10	from fastapi import FastAPI
    11	from fastapi.responses import FileResponse
    12	from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
    13	from starlette.formparsers import MultiPartParser
    14	
    15	from app import __version__
    16	from app.config import Settings, get_settings
    17	from app.ocr.base import OcrEngine
    18	from app.ocr.pool import OcrPool
    19	from app.ocr.rapid import RapidEngine
    20	from app.routes.api import router as api_router
    21	from app.routes.csv_routes import router as csv_router
    22	from app.security import AdmissionLimiter, SecurityMiddleware, install_error_handlers
    23	
    24	logging.basicConfig(
    25	    level=logging.INFO, format='{"ts":"%(asctime)s","level":"%(levelname)s","logger":"%(name)s","msg":"%(message)s"}'
    26	)
    27	log = logging.getLogger("app")
    28	
    29	
    30	def create_app(
    31	    settings: Settings | None = None,
    32	    *,
    33	    warm: bool = True,
    34	    engine_factory: Callable[[Settings], OcrEngine] | None = None,
    35	) -> FastAPI:
    36	    settings = settings or get_settings()
    37	    make_engine = engine_factory or (lambda s: RapidEngine(s))
    38	    # Multipart parts larger than this are spooled to disk by Starlette. Keep every image (per-image cap)
    39	    # in memory so uploads never touch the filesystem; the request-size cap bounds total memory.
    40	    MultiPartParser.spool_max_size = settings.max_image_bytes + 1024 * 1024
    41	
    42	    @asynccontextmanager
    43	    async def lifespan(app: FastAPI) -> AsyncIterator[None]:
    44	        pool = OcrPool(settings, lambda: make_engine(settings))
    45	        app.state.pool = pool
    46	        if warm:
    47	            loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
    48	            # Warm in the background so health answers immediately with ready=false; verify returns 503 until warm.
    49	            warm_task = loop.run_in_executor(None, pool.warmup)
    50	            warm_task.add_done_callback(
    51	                lambda fut: (
    52	                    log.error("OCR warm-up failed; /ready stays 503: %s", fut.exception())
    53	                    if fut.exception()
    54	                    else log.info("OCR warm-up complete")
    55	                )
    56	            )
    57	            app.state.warm_task = warm_task
    58	        yield
    59	        pool.shutdown()
    60	
    61	    app = FastAPI(
    62	        title="Label Check (prototype)",
    63	        version=__version__,
    64	        description=(
    65	            "Prototype for verifying alcohol beverage label artwork against COLA application data. "
    66	            "The tool recommends; the compliance agent decides. Nothing is stored."
    67	        ),
    68	        lifespan=lifespan,
    69	        docs_url=None,
    70	        redoc_url=None,  # no CDN-hosted docs UI (restricted networks)
    71	        openapi_url="/api/v1/openapi.json",
    72	    )
    73	    app.state.settings = settings
    74	    limiter = AdmissionLimiter(settings)
    75	    app.state.limiter = limiter
    76	    app.add_middleware(SecurityMiddleware, settings=settings, limiter=limiter)
    77	    install_error_handlers(app)
    78	    app.include_router(api_router)
    79	    app.include_router(csv_router)
    80	
    81	    static_dir = settings.static_dir
    82	    if static_dir.exists():
    83	        app.mount("/static", StaticFiles(directory=str(static_dir)), name="static")
    84	
    85	        @app.get("/", include_in_schema=False)
    86	        async def index() -> FileResponse:
    87	            return FileResponse(static_dir / "index.html", headers={"Cache-Control": "no-cache"})
    88	
    89	    return app
    90

    28	def _settings(request: Request) -> Settings:
    29	    return cast(Settings, request.app.state.settings)
    30	
    31	
    32	def _pool(request: Request) -> OcrPool:
    33	    pool = cast(OcrPool, request.app.state.pool)
    34	    if not pool.ready:
    35	        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="not_ready", headers={"Retry-After": "3"})
    36	    return pool
    37	
    38	
    39	async def _read_uploads(files: list[UploadFile], settings: Settings) -> list[Upload]:
    40	    if not files:
    41	        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="At least one image is required.")
    42	    if len(files) > settings.max_images_per_application:
    43	        raise HTTPException(
    44	            status_code=422, detail=f"At most {settings.max_images_per_application} images per application."
    45	        )
    46	    uploads: list[Upload] = []
    47	    for f in files:
    48	        chunks: list[bytes] = []
    49	        total = 0
    50	        while True:
    51	            chunk = await f.read(1024 * 1024)
    52	            if not chunk:
    53	                break
    54	            total += len(chunk)
    55	            if total > settings.max_image_bytes:
    56	                mb = settings.max_image_bytes // (1024 * 1024)
    57	                raise ImageError(
    58	                    "image_too_large",
    59	                    f"'{f.filename}' is larger than the {mb} MB per-image limit.",
    60	                    "Export the artwork at a smaller size or higher compression.",
    61	                )
    62	            chunks.append(chunk)
    63	        if total == 0:
    64	            raise ImageError("empty_file", f"'{f.filename}' is empty.", "Choose the image file again.")
    65	        uploads.append(Upload(data=b"".join(chunks), filename=f.filename))
    66	    return uploads
    67	
    68	
    69	def _parse_application(raw: str) -> ApplicationFields:
    70	    try:
    71	        return ApplicationFields.model_validate(json.loads(raw))
    72	    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
    73	        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="application must be a JSON object.") from exc
    74	    except ValidationError as exc:
    75	        first = exc.errors()[0]
    76	        loc = ".".join(str(p) for p in first["loc"])
    77	        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=f"application.{loc}: {first['msg']}") from exc
    78

   100	    if not pool.ready:
   101	        detail = f"warm-up failed: {pool.error}" if pool.error else "not_ready"
   102	        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail=detail, headers={"Retry-After": "3"})
