===== FILE: app/ocr/rapid.py (current text at 3d9bdad, line-numbered) =====
     1	"""RapidOCR (ONNX Runtime) engine with vendored models and explicit thread settings.
     2	
     3	Model files come only from ``settings.models_dir`` (see tools/vendor_models.py and
     4	app/models/MANIFEST.json). Nothing is downloaded.
     5	"""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	
     9	import json
    10	import logging
    11	from pathlib import Path
    12	from typing import Any
    13	
    14	import numpy as np
    15	
    16	from app.config import Settings
    17	
    18	from .alphabet import AlphabetConstrainedDecode
    19	from .base import RawLine
    20	
    21	log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    22	
    23	
    24	class RapidEngine:
    25	    name = "rapidocr-onnxruntime"
    26	
    27	    def __init__(self, settings: Settings) -> None:
    28	        from rapidocr import RapidOCR
    29	
    30	        manifest = json.loads((settings.models_dir / "MANIFEST.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    31	        models = {role: settings.models_dir / m["file"] for role, m in manifest["models"].items()}
    32	        for role, path in models.items():
    33	            if not Path(path).exists():
    34	                raise FileNotFoundError(f"vendored OCR model missing: {role} -> {path}")
    35	        params = {
    36	            "Global.use_cls": settings.ocr_use_cls,
    37	            "Global.log_level": "warning",
    38	            "Det.model_path": str(models["det"]),
    39	            "Rec.model_path": str(models["rec"]),
    40	            "Det.limit_side_len": settings.ocr_det_limit_side_len,
    41	            "Rec.rec_batch_num": settings.ocr_rec_batch_num,
    42	            "EngineConfig.onnxruntime.intra_op_num_threads": settings.ocr_intra_op_threads,
    43	            "EngineConfig.onnxruntime.inter_op_num_threads": 1,
    44	        }
    45	        if "cls" in models:
    46	            params["Cls.model_path"] = str(models["cls"])
    47	        self._ocr = RapidOCR(params=params)
    48	        self.alphabet = "full"
    49	        if settings.ocr_ascii_alphabet:
    50	            rec: Any = self._ocr.text_rec  # the library types this as CTCLabelDecode; we wrap it
    51	            rec.postprocess_op = AlphabetConstrainedDecode(rec.postprocess_op)
    52	            dec = rec.postprocess_op
    53	            self.alphabet = f"printable ASCII ({dec.suppressed_classes} of {len(dec.character)} classes suppressed)"
    54	        self._models = {role: f"{m['file']} (sha256 {m['sha256'][:12]})" for role, m in manifest["models"].items()}
    55	        try:
    56	            from importlib.metadata import version
    57	
    58	            self._version = f"rapidocr {version('rapidocr')} / onnxruntime {version('onnxruntime')}"
    59	        except Exception:  # pragma: no cover - metadata missing in odd environments
    60	            self._version = "rapidocr"
    61	
    62	    def recognize(self, rgb: np.ndarray) -> list[RawLine]:
    63	        bgr = np.ascontiguousarray(rgb[:, :, ::-1])
    64	        result: Any = self._ocr(bgr)
    65	        boxes, txts, scores = (
    66	            getattr(result, "boxes", None),
    67	            getattr(result, "txts", None),
    68	            getattr(result, "scores", None),
    69	        )
    70	        if boxes is None or not txts:
    71	            return []
    72	        lines: list[RawLine] = []
    73	        confs = list(scores) if scores is not None else [0.0] * len(txts)
    74	        for box, text, score in zip(boxes, txts, confs, strict=True):
    75	            if len(box) != 4 or not text or not str(text).strip():
    76	                continue
    77	            quad = (
    78	                (float(box[0][0]), float(box[0][1])),
    79	                (float(box[1][0]), float(box[1][1])),
    80	                (float(box[2][0]), float(box[2][1])),
    81	                (float(box[3][0]), float(box[3][1])),
    82	            )
    83	            lines.append(RawLine(text=str(text).strip(), confidence=float(score), box=quad))
    84	        return lines
    85	
    86	    def info(self) -> dict[str, str]:
    87	        return {"engine": self._version, "alphabet": self.alphabet, **self._models}


===== FILE: app/ocr/pool.py (current text at 3d9bdad, line-numbered) =====
     1	"""Worker pool and admission control for OCR.
     2	
     3	One engine per worker thread, one ONNX intra-op thread per engine (measured to scale near
     4	linearly, see docs/OCR_EVAL.md). A single capacity limiter of N slots protects the CPU.
     5	
     6	Admission policy (pinned by tests/unit/test_pool.py):
     7	- Interactive requests may wait briefly for a slot (a person is watching a spinner).
     8	- Batch requests never wait. They are refused with BusyError (HTTP 429 + Retry-After) when every
     9	  slot is busy, or when an interactive request is already waiting, so the next free slot goes to
    10	  the person rather than to the queue. When nobody is waiting, batch may use every slot.
    11	- A slot is held for as long as an OCR thread is actually running. If the awaiting request is
    12	  cancelled (client disconnect), the slot is released when the thread finishes, not before:
    13	  ONNX Runtime inference cannot be interrupted, so pretending the capacity is free would let more
    14	  jobs onto the CPU than there are workers.
    15	- A slot can be held across several images (batch requests use one slot for all their images), so a
    16	  multi-image request is never refused halfway through.
    17	
    18	Health checks never touch the pool, so they answer even when it is saturated.
    19	"""
    20	
    21	from __future__ import annotations
    22	
    23	import asyncio
    24	import logging
    25	import threading
    26	import time
    27	from collections.abc import AsyncIterator, Awaitable, Callable
    28	from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
    29	from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
    30	
    31	import numpy as np
    32	
    33	from app.config import Settings
    34	
    35	from .base import OcrEngine, RawLine
    36	
    37	log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    38	
    39	Runner = Callable[[np.ndarray], Awaitable[list[RawLine]]]
    40	
    41	
    42	class BusyError(Exception):
    43	    def __init__(self, retry_after: int = 1) -> None:
    44	        super().__init__("OCR capacity is fully in use")
    45	        self.retry_after = retry_after
    46	
    47	
    48	class OcrPool:
    49	    def __init__(self, settings: Settings, engine_factory: Callable[[], OcrEngine]) -> None:
    50	        self.workers = max(1, settings.ocr_workers)
    51	        self.interactive_wait_s = settings.interactive_wait_seconds
    52	        self._factory = engine_factory
    53	        self._executor = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=self.workers, thread_name_prefix="ocr")
    54	        self._local = threading.local()
    55	        self._sem = asyncio.Semaphore(self.workers)
    56	        self._lock = threading.Lock()
    57	        self._active = 0  # slots held (running or reserved)
    58	        self._interactive_waiting = 0
    59	        self._info: dict[str, str] = {}
    60	        self.ready = False
    61	        self.error: str | None = None
    62	
    63	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ engines
    64	    def _engine(self) -> OcrEngine:
    65	        eng = getattr(self._local, "engine", None)
    66	        if eng is None:
    67	            eng = self._factory()
    68	            self._local.engine = eng
    69	            self._info = eng.info()
    70	        return eng
    71	
    72	    def _run(self, rgb: np.ndarray) -> list[RawLine]:
    73	        return self._engine().recognize(rgb)
    74	
    75	    def warmup(self) -> None:
    76	        """Build one engine per worker thread and run a tiny image through each."""
    77	        blank = np.full((64, 256, 3), 255, dtype=np.uint8)
    78	        barrier = threading.Barrier(self.workers)
    79	
    80	        def job() -> None:
    81	            self._engine().recognize(blank)
    82	            barrier.wait(timeout=120)  # hold the thread so every worker gets its own engine
    83	
    84	        try:
    85	            futures = [self._executor.submit(job) for _ in range(self.workers)]
    86	            for f in futures:
    87	                f.result(timeout=180)
    88	        except Exception as exc:  # surfaced through /health and /ready instead of dying silently
    89	            self.error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
    90	            log.exception("OCR pool warm-up failed")
    91	            raise
    92	        self.ready = True
    93	        log.info("OCR pool ready: %d worker(s), %s", self.workers, self._info)
    94	
    95	    def shutdown(self) -> None:
    96	        self._executor.shutdown(wait=False, cancel_futures=True)
    97	
    98	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ admission
    99	    @property
   100	    def in_flight(self) -> int:
   101	        return self._active
   102	
   103	    @property
   104	    def interactive_waiting(self) -> int:
   105	        return self._interactive_waiting
   106	
   107	    def info(self) -> dict[str, str]:
   108	        return dict(self._info)
   109	
   110	    async def _admit(self, interactive: bool) -> None:
   111	        if interactive:
   112	            with self._lock:
   113	                self._interactive_waiting += 1
   114	            try:
   115	                await asyncio.wait_for(self._sem.acquire(), timeout=self.interactive_wait_s)
   116	            except TimeoutError as exc:
   117	                raise BusyError(retry_after=2) from exc
   118	            finally:
   119	                with self._lock:
   120	                    self._interactive_waiting -= 1
   121	            with self._lock:
   122	                self._active += 1
   123	            return
   124	        with self._lock:
   125	            if self._active >= self.workers or self._interactive_waiting > 0:
   126	                raise BusyError(retry_after=1)
   127	            self._active += 1  # reserve before awaiting so concurrent batch callers cannot all pass
   128	        await self._sem.acquire()  # cannot block: holders + reservations never exceed the slot count
   129	
   130	    def _release(self) -> None:
   131	        with self._lock:
   132	            self._active -= 1
   133	        self._sem.release()
   134	
   135	    @asynccontextmanager
   136	    async def slot(self, *, interactive: bool) -> AsyncIterator[tuple[Runner, int]]:
   137	        """Hold one worker slot. Yields (run, queue_ms); ``await run(rgb)`` executes OCR on the slot.
   138	
   139	        The slot is released when the block exits, or, if the block is cancelled while a thread is
   140	        still running, when that thread finishes.
   141	        """
   142	        t0 = time.perf_counter()
   143	        await self._admit(interactive)
   144	        queue_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
   145	        loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
   146	        pending: list[asyncio.Future[list[RawLine]]] = []
   147	
   148	        async def run(rgb: np.ndarray) -> list[RawLine]:
   149	            fut = loop.run_in_executor(self._executor, self._run, rgb)
   150	            pending.append(fut)
   151	            try:
   152	                return await asyncio.shield(fut)
   153	            finally:
   154	                if fut.done():
   155	                    pending.remove(fut)
   156	
   157	        try:
   158	            yield run, queue_ms
   159	        finally:
   160	            running = [f for f in pending if not f.done()]
   161	            if running:
   162	                running[-1].add_done_callback(lambda _f: self._release())
   163	            else:
   164	                self._release()
   165	
   166	    async def recognize(self, rgb: np.ndarray, *, interactive: bool) -> tuple[list[RawLine], int, int]:
   167	        """Run OCR on one image in its own slot. Returns (lines, queue_ms, ocr_ms)."""
   168	        async with self.slot(interactive=interactive) as (run, queue_ms):
   169	            t1 = time.perf_counter()
   170	            lines = await run(rgb)
   171	            return lines, queue_ms, int((time.perf_counter() - t1) * 1000)


===== FILE: app/static/render.js (current text at 3d9bdad, line-numbered) =====
     1	// Results rendering: verdict, checklist, warning report, label figures with highlight overlays,
     2	// and evidence crops cut from the original image in the browser. All user/OCR text goes through
     3	// textContent; nothing is ever inserted as HTML.
     4	
     5	const STATUS = {
     6	  match: { word: "Match", icon: "check_circle" },
     7	  needs_review: { word: "Needs review", icon: "warning" },
     8	  mismatch: { word: "Mismatch", icon: "cancel" },
     9	  not_found: { word: "Not found", icon: "error" },
    10	  not_checked: { word: "Not checked", icon: "remove_circle" },
    11	  info: { word: "Info", icon: "info" },
    12	};
    13	
    14	const VERDICT = {
    15	  ready_for_approval: { heading: "Ready for your approval", kind: "success" },
    16	  needs_review: { heading: "Please confirm the items marked", kind: "warning" },
    17	  issues_found: { heading: "Issues found", kind: "error" },
    18	  unreadable: { heading: "Could not read the images", kind: "error" },
    19	};
    20	
    21	/** Tiny DOM helper: el("div", {class: "x", text: "hi"}, [children]) */
    22	export function el(tag, attrs = {}, children = []) {
    23	  const node = document.createElement(tag);
    24	  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(attrs)) {
    25	    if (v === null || v === undefined || v === false) continue;
    26	    if (k === "text") node.textContent = String(v);
    27	    else if (k === "class") node.className = v;
    28	    else if (k === "dataset") Object.assign(node.dataset, v);
    29	    else if (k.startsWith("on") && typeof v === "function") node.addEventListener(k.slice(2), v);
    30	    else node.setAttribute(k, v === true ? "" : String(v));
    31	  }
    32	  for (const c of [].concat(children)) {
    33	    if (c === null || c === undefined) continue;
    34	    node.append(typeof c === "string" ? document.createTextNode(c) : c);
    35	  }
    36	  return node;
    37	}
    38	
    39	export function icon(name, cls = "usa-icon") {
    40	  const svg = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "svg");
    41	  svg.setAttribute("class", cls);
    42	  svg.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
    43	  svg.setAttribute("focusable", "false");
    44	  svg.setAttribute("role", "img");
    45	  const use = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "use");
    46	  use.setAttribute("href", `#${name}`);
    47	  svg.append(use);
    48	  return svg;
    49	}
    50	
    51	export function statusTag(status) {
    52	  const s = STATUS[status] || STATUS.not_checked;
    53	  return el("span", { class: `status-tag status-${status}` }, [icon(s.icon), s.word]);
    54	}
    55	
    56	function seconds(ms) { return `${(ms / 1000).toFixed(1)} s`; }
    57	
    58	export function renderVerdict(container, result) {
    59	  const v = VERDICT[result.verdict] || VERDICT.needs_review;
    60	  container.replaceChildren(
    61	    el("div", { class: `usa-alert usa-alert--${v.kind} verdict`, role: "status" }, [
    62	      el("div", { class: "usa-alert__body" }, [
    63	        el("h2", { class: "usa-alert__heading", text: v.heading }),
    64	        el("p", { class: "usa-alert__text", text: result.summary }),
    65	        el("p", { class: "usa-alert__text timing" }, [
    66	          `Checked in ${seconds(result.timing.total_ms)}`,
    67	          el("span", { class: "text-normal" }, ` (target: under 5 seconds). ${result.images.length} image${result.images.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} read.`),
    68	        ]),
    69	      ]),
    70	    ]),
    71	  );

    74	/**
    75	 * @param {HTMLElement} container
    76	 * @param {object[]} checks
    77	 * @param {(check: object) => void} onSelect
    78	 * @param {Map<string, string>} crops check id -> data URL
    79	 */
    80	export function renderChecklist(container, checks, onSelect, crops) {
    81	  const rows = checks.map((c) => {
    82	    const tr = el("tr", { dataset: { check: c.id }, tabindex: "-1" }, [
    83	      el("td", {}, statusTag(c.status)),
    84	      el("td", {}, [
    85	        el("div", { class: "check-label", text: c.label }),
    86	        el("div", { class: "check-note", text: c.note }),
    87	        c.rule ? el("div", { class: "rule", text: c.rule }) : null,
    88	      ]),
    89	      el("td", { class: "expected", text: c.expected || "—" }),
    90	      el("td", {}, [
    91	        el("div", { class: "found", text: c.found || (c.status === "not_found" ? "nothing similar found" : "—") }),
    92	        crops.get(c.id) ? el("img", { class: "crop", src: crops.get(c.id), alt: `Label region where "${c.found || ""}" was found` }) : null,
    93	      ]),
    94	    ]);
    95	    tr.addEventListener("click", () => onSelect(c));
    96	    tr.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => { if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") { e.preventDefault(); onSelect(c); } });
    97	    tr.tabIndex = 0;
    98	    return tr;
    99	  });
   100	  container.replaceChildren(
   101	    el("table", { class: "usa-table usa-table--stacked checklist width-full" }, [
   102	      el("thead", {}, el("tr", {}, [
   103	        el("th", { scope: "col", text: "Status" }), el("th", { scope: "col", text: "Check" }),
   104	        el("th", { scope: "col", text: "Application says" }), el("th", { scope: "col", text: "Label says" }),
   105	      ])),
   106	      el("tbody", {}, rows),
   107	    ]),
   108	  );
   109	}
   110	
   111	function renderDiff(diff) {
   112	  // "-a b | +c" -> spans
   113	  const wrap = el("span", { class: "diff" });
   114	  for (const part of diff.split(" | ")) {
   115	    if (part.startsWith("-")) wrap.append(el("span", { class: "del", text: part.slice(1) }), " ");
   116	    else if (part.startsWith("+")) wrap.append(el("span", { class: "ins", text: part.slice(1) }), " ");
   117	    else wrap.append(part, " ");
   118	  }
   119	  return wrap;
   120	}
   121	
   122	export function renderWarning(container, w, onSelect) {
   123	  const notRequired = w.assessment === "not_required"; // under 0.5% alcohol: absence is not a finding
   124	  const overall = notRequired ? (w.present ? "info" : "not_checked")
   125	    : !w.present ? "not_found" : w.assessment === "exact" && w.anchor_caps === "match" ? "match"
   126	    : w.assessment === "wording" ? "mismatch" : "needs_review";
   127	  const headline = notRequired ? (w.present ? "Not required at this alcohol content; a statement is present" : "Not required at this alcohol content")
   128	    : !w.present ? "No warning statement found" : w.exact ? "Wording is exact" : "Wording is not exact";
   129	  const card = el("div", { class: "warning-card" }, [
   130	    el("div", { class: "warning-head" }, [
   131	      statusTag(overall),
   132	      el("span", { class: "text-bold", text: headline }),
   133	      w.present ? el("button", { type: "button", class: "usa-button usa-button--unstyled", text: "Show on label", onclick: () => onSelect({ id: "warning", evidence: w.evidence }) }) : null,
   134	    ]),
   135	    w.present ? el("dl", { class: "margin-top-2" }, [
   136	      el("dt", { class: "text-bold", text: "As read from the label" }),
   137	      el("dd", { class: "margin-left-0 margin-top-05" }, el("div", { class: "found-text", text: w.found_text })),
   138	      w.diff ? el("dt", { class: "text-bold margin-top-2", text: "Differences from the required text" }) : null,
   139	      w.diff ? el("dd", { class: "margin-left-0 margin-top-05" }, renderDiff(w.diff)) : null,
   140	      el("dt", { class: "text-bold margin-top-2", text: "Format checks" }),
   141	      el("dd", { class: "margin-left-0 margin-top-05" }, el("ul", { class: "usa-list usa-list--unstyled" }, [
   142	        el("li", {}, [statusTag(w.anchor_caps), " ", "GOVERNMENT WARNING in capital letters"]),
   143	        el("li", { class: "margin-top-05" }, [statusTag(w.anchor_bold), " ", "GOVERNMENT WARNING in bold type"]),
   144	        el("li", { class: "margin-top-05" }, [statusTag(w.body_not_bold), " ", "Remainder of the statement not in bold"]),
   145	      ])),
   146	    ]) : null,
   147	    el("ul", { class: "usa-list" }, w.notes.map((n) => el("li", { text: n }))),
   148	    el("p", { class: "rule text-base", text: w.rule }),
   149	  ]);
   150	  container.replaceChildren(card);
   151	}

   153	/**
   154	 * Render each image with an SVG overlay in canonical coordinates.
   155	 * @returns {Map<number, SVGSVGElement>} image index -> overlay
   156	 */
   157	export function renderFigures(container, images, files, lines) {
   158	  container.replaceChildren();
   159	  const overlays = new Map();
   160	  images.forEach((im, i) => {
   161	    const url = URL.createObjectURL(files[i]);
   162	    const img = el("img", { src: url, alt: `Label image ${i + 1}${im.filename ? `: ${im.filename}` : ""}` });
   163	    img.addEventListener("load", () => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), { once: true });
   164	    const svg = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "svg");
   165	    svg.setAttribute("class", "overlay");
   166	    svg.setAttribute("viewBox", `0 0 ${im.width} ${im.height}`);
   167	    svg.setAttribute("preserveAspectRatio", "none");
   168	    svg.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
   169	    overlays.set(i, svg);
   170	    const readLines = lines.filter((l) => l.image_index === i).length;
   171	    const q = im.quality;
   172	    const caption = [
   173	      im.filename || `Image ${i + 1}`, `${im.width} × ${im.height}`, `${readLines} line${readLines === 1 ? "" : "s"} read`,
   174	      im.rotated_degrees ? `rotated ${im.rotated_degrees}° to read` : null,
   175	      q.readable ? null : `Not readable: ${q.reason || ""}`,
   176	    ].filter(Boolean).join(" · ");
   177	    container.append(el("figure", { class: "label-figure" }, [
   178	      el("div", { class: "label-stage" }, [img, svg]),
   179	      el("figcaption", { text: caption }),
   180	    ]));
   181	  });
   182	  return overlays;
   183	}
   184	
   185	function polygonFor(quad, cls) {
   186	  const p = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "polygon");
   187	  p.setAttribute("points", quad.map(([x, y]) => `${x},${y}`).join(" "));
   188	  if (cls) p.setAttribute("class", cls);
   189	  return p;
   190	}
   191	
   192	/** Draw all evidence faintly; the active check's evidence strongly. */
   193	export function drawOverlays(overlays, checks, warning, activeId) {
   194	  for (const svg of overlays.values()) svg.replaceChildren();
   195	  const items = [...checks.map((c) => ({ id: c.id, status: c.status, evidence: c.evidence })),
   196	    { id: "warning", status: warning.exact ? "match" : "needs_review", evidence: warning.evidence || [] }];
   197	  for (const it of items) {
   198	    for (const ev of it.evidence) {
   199	      const svg = overlays.get(ev.image_index);
   200	      if (!svg) continue;
   201	      const bad = it.status === "mismatch" || it.status === "not_found";
   202	      svg.append(polygonFor(ev.box, [it.id === activeId ? "is-active" : "", bad ? "is-bad" : ""].join(" ").trim()));
   203	    }
   204	  }
   205	}
   206	
   207	/**
   208	 * Cut evidence crops from the original images. Uses the browser's EXIF-aware decoder so the
   209	 * bitmap is in the same oriented space as the server's coordinates; verifies dimensions match.
   210	 * @returns {Promise<Map<string, string>>} check id -> data URL
   211	 */
   212	export async function makeCrops(files, images, checks) {
   213	  const crops = new Map();
   214	  const bitmaps = new Map();
   215	  for (const c of checks) {
   216	    const ev = c.evidence && c.evidence[0];
   217	    if (!ev) continue;
   218	    const im = images[ev.image_index];
   219	    const file = files[ev.image_index];
   220	    if (!im || !file) continue;
   221	    try {
   222	      if (!bitmaps.has(ev.image_index)) {
   223	        bitmaps.set(ev.image_index, await createImageBitmap(file, { imageOrientation: "from-image" }));
   224	      }
   225	      const bmp = bitmaps.get(ev.image_index);
   226	      if (Math.abs(bmp.width - im.width) > 2 || Math.abs(bmp.height - im.height) > 2) {
   227	        continue; // browser and server disagree on orientation for this file: no crop rather than a wrong one
   228	      }
   229	      const sx = 1, sy = 1;
   230	      const onThisImage = c.evidence.filter((e) => e.image_index === ev.image_index); // one crop, one image
   231	      const xs = onThisImage.flatMap((e) => e.box.map((p) => p[0]));
   232	      const ys = onThisImage.flatMap((e) => e.box.map((p) => p[1]));
   233	      const pad = 8;
   234	      const x0 = Math.max(0, Math.min(...xs) - pad), y0 = Math.max(0, Math.min(...ys) - pad);
   235	      const x1 = Math.min(im.width, Math.max(...xs) + pad), y1 = Math.min(im.height, Math.max(...ys) + pad);
   236	      const w = Math.max(1, x1 - x0), h = Math.max(1, y1 - y0);
   237	      const scale = Math.min(1, 640 / w, 120 / h);
   238	      const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
   239	      canvas.width = Math.round(w * scale); canvas.height = Math.round(h * scale);
   240	      canvas.getContext("2d").drawImage(bmp, x0 * sx, y0 * sy, w * sx, h * sy, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
   241	      crops.set(c.id, canvas.toDataURL("image/png"));
   242	    } catch { /* crops are a convenience; never fail the result over them */ }
   243	  }
   244	  for (const b of bitmaps.values()) b.close?.();
   245	  return crops;
   246	}
   247	
   248	export function renderOcrLines(container, lines) {
   249	  const byImage = new Map();
   250	  for (const l of lines) (byImage.get(l.image_index) || byImage.set(l.image_index, []).get(l.image_index)).push(l);
   251	  container.replaceChildren(...[...byImage.entries()].map(([idx, ls]) =>
   252	    el("div", {}, [
   253	      el("h4", { class: "margin-bottom-05", text: `Image ${idx + 1}` }),
   254	      el("ol", { class: "ocr-lines" }, ls.map((l) => el("li", { text: `${l.text}  (${Math.round(l.confidence * 100)}%)` }))),
   255	    ])));
   256	}

   258	// ------------------------------------------------------------------ decisions and export (both screens)
   259	
   260	/** The "What to look at" lines for a result: every check that is not a plain match, plus the warning. */
   261	export function issueTexts(result) {
   262	  if (!result) return [];
   263	  const probs = result.checks.filter((c) => c.status !== "match" && c.status !== "info" && c.status !== "not_checked").map((c) => `${c.label}: ${c.status.replace("_", " ")}`);
   264	  const w = result.warning;
   265	  if (w.assessment === "not_required") { /* under 0.5% alcohol: no statement required */ }
   266	  else if (!w.present) probs.push("Warning: missing"); else if (!w.exact) probs.push("Warning: wording not exact"); else if (w.anchor_caps !== "match") probs.push("Warning: heading not all caps");
   267	  if (w.present && w.anchor_bold === "needs_review") probs.push("Warning: heading may not be bold");
   268	  if (w.present && w.body_not_bold === "needs_review") probs.push("Warning: statement may be all bold");
   269	  return probs;
   270	}

   272	/** Approve / Reject / Flag with a note. getCurrent() reads the live decision {decision, note} so a
   273	 * press never works from a stale copy; onChange(next) receives the whole new object. The pressed
   274	 * button carries a check mark in its text as well as a dark fill (Windows contrast themes drop fills). */
   275	export function decisionControls(getCurrent, onChange, noteLabel = "Note") {
   276	  const d = getCurrent() || {};
   277	  const btn = (val, label) => el("button", { type: "button", class: `usa-button usa-button--outline${d.decision === val ? " is-on" : ""}`,
   278	    text: d.decision === val ? `✓ ${label}` : label,
   279	    "aria-pressed": d.decision === val ? "true" : "false",
   280	    title: d.decision === val ? `${label}: press again to clear` : label,
   281	    onclick: () => { const cur = getCurrent() || {}; onChange({ ...cur, decision: cur.decision === val ? null : val }); } });
   282	  const note = el("input", { type: "text", class: "note-input", placeholder: "Note (optional)", value: d.note || "", "aria-label": noteLabel });
   283	  note.addEventListener("change", () => onChange({ ...(getCurrent() || {}), note: note.value }));
   284	  return el("div", {}, [el("div", { class: "decision-btns" }, [btn("approve", "Approve"), btn("reject", "Reject"), btn("flag", "Flag")]), note]);
   285	}

   287	export const EXPORT_HEAD = ["application_id", "brand_name", "class_type", "verdict", "what_to_look_at", "summary", "brand_status", "class_status",
   288	  "alcohol_status", "net_contents_status", "bottler_status", "origin_status", "warning_present", "warning_exact", "warning_anchor_caps",
   289	  "decision", "note", "images", "elapsed_ms", "exported_at"];
   290	
   291	/** One export row. elapsed_ms is the check's own time on the single screen and the time spent in the
   292	 * batch (queue plus reads) on the batch screen. */
   293	export function exportRow({ application, key, result, status, error, decision, files, elapsedMs }) {
   294	  const st = (id) => result?.checks.find((c) => c.id === id)?.status || "";
   295	  const w = result?.warning;
   296	  return [application?.application_id || key || "", application?.brand_name || "", application?.class_type || "",
   297	    result?.verdict || status || "", result ? (issueTexts(result).join("; ") || "All checks match") : "", result?.summary || error || "",
   298	    st("brand_name"), st("class_type"), st("alcohol_content"), st("net_contents"), st("bottler"), st("country_of_origin"),
   299	    w ? w.present : "", w ? w.exact : "", w ? w.anchor_caps : "", decision?.decision || "", decision?.note || "",
   300	    (files || []).map((f) => f.name).join(";"), elapsedMs ?? "", new Date().toISOString()];
   301	}
   302	
   303	export function csvCell(v) {
   304	  let s = v == null ? "" : String(v);
   305	  if (/^[=+\-@\t\r]/.test(s)) s = "'" + s; // neutralize spreadsheet formulas
   306	  return `"${s.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
   307	}
   308	
   309	/** Hand the browser a CSV file (UTF-8 with a byte order mark, so spreadsheets read accents). */
   310	export function downloadCsv(filename, rows) {
   311	  const lines = [EXPORT_HEAD.map(csvCell).join(","), ...rows.map((row) => row.map(csvCell).join(","))];
   312	  const blob = new Blob(["\ufeff" + lines.join("\r\n")], { type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8" });
   313	  const a = el("a", { href: URL.createObjectURL(blob), download: filename });
   314	  document.body.append(a); a.click(); a.remove();
   315	}
   316	
   317	export function exportStamp() {
   318	  return new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 19).replace(/[:T]/g, "-");
   319	}

    54	function humanSize(n) { return n > 1024 * 1024 ? `${(n / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)} MB` : `${Math.round(n / 1024)} KB`; }
    55	
    56	function renderFileList() {
    57	  const list = $("#file-list");
    58	  list.replaceChildren(...state.files.map((f, i) => {
    59	    const url = URL.createObjectURL(f);
    60	    const img = el("img", { src: url, alt: "" });
    61	    img.addEventListener("load", () => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), { once: true });
    62	    return el("li", {}, [
    63	      img,
    64	      el("div", {}, [el("div", { class: "file-name", text: f.name }), el("div", { class: "file-meta", text: `${f.type || "image"} · ${humanSize(f.size)}` })]),
    65	      el("button", { type: "button", class: "usa-button usa-button--unstyled", text: "Remove", "aria-label": `Remove ${f.name}`, onclick: () => { state.files.splice(i, 1); renderFileList(); } }),
    66	    ]);
    67	  }));
    68	  $("#clear-files").hidden = state.files.length === 0;
    69	}
    70	
    71	function addFiles(fileList) {
    72	  const problems = [];
    73	  for (const f of fileList) {
    74	    if (state.files.length >= MAX_IMAGES) { problems.push(`Only ${MAX_IMAGES} images per application; "${f.name}" was not added.`); break; }
    75	    if (f.type && !ACCEPT.test(f.type)) { problems.push(`"${f.name}" is not a supported image type (PNG, JPEG, GIF, WebP, TIFF, BMP).`); continue; }
    76	    if (f.size > MAX_BYTES) { problems.push(`"${f.name}" is ${humanSize(f.size)}; the limit is 10 MB per image.`); continue; }
    77	    if (f.size === 0) { problems.push(`"${f.name}" is empty.`); continue; }
    78	    if (state.files.some((g) => g.name === f.name && g.size === f.size)) continue; // same file twice
    79	    state.files.push(f);
    80	  }
    81	  renderFileList();
    82	  showFormError(problems.join(" "));
    83	}
    84	
    85	function wireDropzone() {
    86	  const dz = $("#dropzone");
    87	  const input = $("#file-input");
    88	  input.addEventListener("change", () => { addFiles(input.files); input.value = ""; });
    89	  ["dragenter", "dragover"].forEach((t) => dz.addEventListener(t, (e) => { e.preventDefault(); dz.classList.add("is-dragover"); }));
    90	  ["dragleave", "drop"].forEach((t) => dz.addEventListener(t, (e) => { e.preventDefault(); dz.classList.remove("is-dragover"); }));
    91	  dz.addEventListener("drop", (e) => addFiles(e.dataTransfer.files));
    92	  $("#clear-files").addEventListener("click", () => { state.files = []; renderFileList(); });
    93	  document.addEventListener("paste", (e) => {
    94	    const imgs = [...(e.clipboardData?.files || [])].filter((f) => ACCEPT.test(f.type));
    95	    if (imgs.length) addFiles(imgs);
    96	  });
    97	}

   117	function fillApplication(app) {
   118	  const form = $("#check-form");
   119	  form.querySelector(`input[name=beverage_type][value=${app.beverage_type}]`).checked = true;
   120	  for (const id of ["brand_name", "class_type", "alcohol_content", "net_contents", "bottler", "country_of_origin", "application_id"]) {
   121	    form.querySelector(`#${id}`).value = app[id] || "";
   122	  }
   123	  form.querySelector("#imported").checked = Boolean(app.imported);
   124	  const hasOptional = app.country_of_origin || app.application_id || app.imported;
   125	  const btn = form.querySelector("[aria-controls=optional-fields]");
   126	  if (hasOptional && btn.getAttribute("aria-expanded") !== "true") btn.click();
   127	}

   170	async function runCheck() {
   171	  const app = readApplication();
   172	  const missing = validate(app);
   173	  if (missing.length) { showFormError(`Please add ${missing.join(", ")}.`); return; }
   174	  showFormError("");
   175	  const btn = $("#check-btn");
   176	  btn.disabled = true;
   177	  setStatus(`Reading ${state.files.length} image${state.files.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}…`, true);
   178	  const t0 = performance.now();
   179	  try {
   180	    const result = await verify(state.files, app);
   181	    state.result = result;
   182	    state.app = app;
   183	    state.decision = null;
   184	    state.activeId = null;
   185	    const crops = await makeCrops(state.files, result.images, result.checks);
   186	    renderVerdict($("#verdict"), result);
   187	    renderChecklist($("#checklist"), result.checks, selectCheck, crops);
   188	    renderWarning($("#warning-report"), result.warning, selectCheck);
   189	    state.overlays = renderFigures($("#figures"), result.images, state.files, result.lines);
   190	    drawOverlays(state.overlays, result.checks, result.warning, null);
   191	    renderOcrLines($("#ocr-lines"), result.lines);
   192	    state.elapsedMs = Math.round(performance.now() - t0);
   193	    renderDecision();
   194	    const results = $("#results");
   195	    results.hidden = false;
   196	    results.focus({ preventScroll: false });
   197	    results.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
   198	    setStatus(`Done in ${((performance.now() - t0) / 1000).toFixed(1)} s.`);
   199	  } catch (err) {
   200	    if (err instanceof ApiError) {
   201	      const extra = err.status === 429 ? " The service is busy; try again in a moment." : "";
   202	      showFormError(err.message + extra, err.hint);
   203	    } else {
   204	      showFormError("Could not reach the verification service.", "Check your connection and try again.");
   205	    }
   206	    setStatus("");
   207	  } finally {
   208	    btn.disabled = false;
   209	  }
   210	}

===== FILE: app/static/batch.js (current text at 3d9bdad, line-numbered) =====
     1	// Batch screen. The browser orchestrates: every image is read once (/extract), images are paired
     2	// to application rows explicitly (CSV "images" column or filename prefix), each application is
     3	// compared (/compare), rows stream into the table, the agent records decisions, and everything can
     4	// be exported. The server stays stateless; a refresh clears the session and the page says so.
     5	import { ApiError, compare, extract, health } from "./api.js";
     6	import { decisionControls, downloadCsv, drawOverlays, el, exportRow, exportStamp, issueTexts, makeCrops, renderChecklist, renderFigures, renderWarning, statusTag } from "./render.js";
     7	
     8	const VERDICT_WORD = { ready_for_approval: "Ready", needs_review: "Needs review", issues_found: "Issues found", unreadable: "Unreadable" };
     9	const VERDICT_STATUS = { ready_for_approval: "match", needs_review: "needs_review", issues_found: "mismatch", unreadable: "not_found" };
    10	const SEVERITY = { issues_found: 0, unreadable: 1, needs_review: 2, ready_for_approval: 3 };
    11	const PAGE_SIZE = 50;
    12	const ACCEPT = /^image\/(png|jpeg|gif|webp|tiff|bmp)$/;
    13	const $ = (sel) => document.querySelector(sel);
    14	
    15	const state = {
    16	  images: new Map(),      // fileKey (relative path or name, lower-cased) -> File
    17	  inputVersion: 0, builtVersion: -1, detailCache: new Map(),
    18	  csv: null,              // parsed CSV response
    19	  items: [],              // application rows (or one per image when there is no CSV)
    20	  unpaired: [],           // file names not attached to any row
    21	  extractions: new Map(), // file name -> extract response
    22	  decisions: new Map(),   // item key -> {decision, note}
    23	  running: false, abort: null, maxConcurrency: 3, concurrency: 3, successes: 0, inflight: 0, waiters: [], renderTimer: 0,
    24	  filter: "all", page: 0, expanded: null, times: [], startedAt: 0,
    25	};
    26	
    27	// ------------------------------------------------------------------ helpers
    28	const norm = (name) => name.split(/[\\/]/).pop().toLowerCase();          // basename, lower-cased
    29	const stem = (name) => norm(name).replace(/\.[a-z0-9]+$/, "");
    30	const fileKey = (f) => (f.webkitRelativePath || f.name).toLowerCase();  // folder-aware identity
    31	function setStatus(text, busy = false) { const s = $("#batch-status"); s.textContent = text; s.classList.toggle("is-busy", busy); }
    32	function showError(msg) { const box = $("#batch-error"); box.querySelector("p").textContent = msg; box.hidden = !msg; }
    33	function seconds(ms) { return `${(ms / 1000).toFixed(1)} s`; }
    34	function percentile(arr, p) { if (!arr.length) return 0; const s = [...arr].sort((a, b) => a - b); return s[Math.min(s.length - 1, Math.floor(p * s.length))]; }

    36	// ------------------------------------------------------------------ intake
    37	function addImages(files) {
    38	  let added = 0;
    39	  for (const f of files) {
    40	    if (f.type && !ACCEPT.test(f.type)) continue;
    41	    if (f.size === 0 || f.size > 10 * 1024 * 1024) continue;
    42	    const key = fileKey(f);
    43	    if (!state.images.has(key)) { state.images.set(key, f); added++; }
    44	  }
    45	  state.inputVersion++;
    46	  const byName = new Map();
    47	  for (const k of state.images.keys()) byName.set(norm(k), (byName.get(norm(k)) || 0) + 1);
    48	  const dupes = [...byName.entries()].filter(([, n]) => n > 1);
    49	  let msg = `${state.images.size} image${state.images.size === 1 ? "" : "s"} ready${added ? ` (${added} added)` : ""}.`;
    50	  if (dupes.length) msg += ` ${dupes.length} file name${dupes.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} appear${dupes.length === 1 ? "s" : ""} in more than one folder (${dupes.slice(0, 3).map(([n]) => n).join(", ")}${dupes.length > 3 ? ", …" : ""}); those are kept apart and cannot be paired by name alone.`;
    51	  $("#batch-image-count").textContent = msg;
    52	  updateStartButton();
    53	}

    55	async function setCsv(file) {
    56	  const fd = new FormData();
    57	  fd.append("file", file, file.name);
    58	  const box = $("#batch-csv-summary");
    59	  try {
    60	    const resp = await fetch("/api/v1/csv/parse", { method: "POST", body: fd });
    61	    const body = await resp.json();
    62	    if (!resp.ok) throw new ApiError(resp.status, body);
    63	    state.csv = body;
    64	    state.inputVersion++;
    65	    const bad = body.rows.filter((r) => r.errors.length);
    66	    const none = bad.length > 0 && bad.length === body.rows.length;
    67	    const count = `${body.rows.length} row${body.rows.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;
    68	    box.replaceChildren(...[
    69	      el("p", { class: `text-bold margin-0${none ? " text-secondary-dark" : ""}`, text: none ? `${file.name}: ${count}, none usable (every row has a problem).` : `${file.name}: ${count}${bad.length ? `, ${bad.length} with problems` : ""}.` }),
    70	      body.warnings.length ? el("ul", { class: "usa-list usa-list--unstyled text-secondary-dark" }, body.warnings.map((w) => el("li", { text: w }))) : null,
    71	      bad.length ? el("details", none ? { open: "" } : {}, [el("summary", { text: "Rows with problems (they will be skipped)" }),
    72	        el("ul", { class: "usa-list" }, bad.slice(0, 20).map((r) => el("li", { text: `Row ${r.row_number}: ${r.errors.join("; ")}` })))]) : null,
    73	    ].filter(Boolean));
    74	  } catch (e) {
    75	    state.csv = null;
    76	    state.inputVersion++; // a failed replacement still changes the inputs: items must be rebuilt
    77	    box.replaceChildren(el("p", { class: "text-secondary-dark text-bold", text: e.message || "Could not read the CSV." }));
    78	  }
    79	  updateStartButton();
    80	}
    81	
    82	function updateStartButton() {
    83	  $("#batch-start").disabled = state.running || state.images.size === 0;
    84	}

    86	// ------------------------------------------------------------------ pairing (explicit only)
    87	function buildItems() {
    88	  if (state.items.length && state.builtVersion !== state.inputVersion) {
    89	    state.decisions.clear(); // decisions were made against the previous images or spreadsheet
    90	    state.times = [];
    91	  }
    92	  const files = new Map(state.images);       // copy; we remove as we attach
    93	  const items = [];
    94	  if (state.csv) {
    95	    for (const row of state.csv.rows) {
    96	      if (!row.application) continue;
    97	      const app = row.application;
    98	      const key = app.application_id || `row-${row.row_number}`;
    99	      const slash = (s) => s.toLowerCase().replace(/\\/g, "/");
   100	      const byName = (n) => [...files.keys()].filter((k) => norm(k) === norm(n));
   101	      // A relative path in the CSV ("folder-a/back.png") matches the file's path (which may start with
   102	      // the picked folder's own name) before falling back to the bare file name.
   103	      const byPath = (n) => { const p = slash(n); return [...files.keys()].filter((k) => slash(k) === p || slash(k).endsWith("/" + p)); };
   104	      let keys = [];
   105	      const missing = [];
   106	      for (const listed of row.images) {
   107	        const exact = byPath(listed);
   108	        const hits = exact.length ? exact : byName(listed);
   109	        if (hits.length === 1) keys.push(hits[0]);
   110	        else missing.push(hits.length ? `${listed} (ambiguous: ${hits.length} files)` : listed);
   111	      }
   112	      let method = row.images.length ? (missing.length ? `listed in CSV (${missing.length} not found)` : "listed in CSV") : "none";
   113	      if (!row.images.length && app.application_id) {
   114	        const id = app.application_id.toLowerCase();
   115	        keys = [...files.keys()].filter((k) => { const st = stem(k); return st === id || st.startsWith(id + "_") || st.startsWith(id + "-") || st.startsWith(id + " "); });
   116	        method = keys.length ? "filename prefix" : "none";
   117	      }
   118	      const attached = keys.map((k) => files.get(k)).filter(Boolean);
   119	      for (const k of keys) files.delete(k);
   120	      items.push({ key, row: row.row_number, application: app, files: attached, missing, method,
   121	        result: null, error: null, ms: 0, status: attached.length ? "pending" : "no-images" });
   122	    }
   123	    state.unpaired = [...files.keys()];
   124	  } else {
   125	    for (const [n, f] of files) {
   126	      items.push({ key: n, row: null, application: null, files: [f], missing: [], method: "extract only", result: null, error: null, ms: 0, status: "pending" });
   127	    }
   128	    state.unpaired = [];
   129	  }
   130	  state.items = items;
   131	  state.builtVersion = state.inputVersion;
   132	  state.detailCache.clear();
   133	}
   134	
   135	// ------------------------------------------------------------------ processing
   136	// Request-level limiter: at most state.concurrency extract calls in flight, whatever the number of
   137	// applications being worked on. Adaptive: shrinks on 429, grows back slowly after successes.
   138	function acquireSlot() {
   139	  if (state.inflight < state.concurrency) { state.inflight++; return Promise.resolve(); }
   140	  return new Promise((resolve) => state.waiters.push(resolve));
   141	}
   142	function releaseSlot() {
   143	  if (state.waiters.length && state.inflight <= state.concurrency) { state.waiters.shift()(); return; }
   144	  state.inflight--;
   145	}
   146	
   147	async function extractOne(file, signal) {
   148	  const key = fileKey(file);
   149	  if (state.extractions.has(key)) return state.extractions.get(key);
   150	  let delay = 0;
   151	  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 6; attempt++) {
   152	    if (delay) { await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, delay)); delay = 0; } // back off without holding a slot
   153	    await acquireSlot();
   154	    try {
   155	      const t0 = performance.now();
   156	      const res = await extract([file], { batch: true, signal });
   157	      state.times.push(performance.now() - t0);
   158	      state.extractions.set(key, res);
   159	      state.successes++;
   160	      if (state.successes % 20 === 0 && state.concurrency < state.maxConcurrency) state.concurrency++;
   161	      return res;
   162	    } catch (e) {
   163	      if (e.name === "AbortError") throw e;
   164	      if (e instanceof ApiError && (e.status === 429 || e.status === 503)) {
   165	        state.concurrency = Math.max(1, state.concurrency - 1);
   166	        delay = (e.retryAfter || 1) * 1000 * (attempt + 1) + Math.random() * 500;
   167	        continue;
   168	      }
   169	      throw e;
   170	    } finally {
   171	      releaseSlot();
   172	    }
   173	  }
   174	  throw new ApiError(429, { message: "The service stayed busy; this item was skipped. Resume to try again." });
   175	}
   176	
   177	function linesForItem(item) {
   178	  const lines = [], images = [];
   179	  item.files.forEach((f, idx) => {
   180	    const ex = state.extractions.get(fileKey(f));
   181	    if (!ex) return;
   182	    for (const im of ex.images) images.push({ ...im, index: idx });
   183	    for (const ln of ex.lines) lines.push({ ...ln, image_index: idx });
   184	  });
   185	  return { lines, images };
   186	}
   187	
   188	async function processItem(item, signal) {
   189	  item.status = "working";
   190	  const t0 = performance.now();
   191	  try {
   192	    await Promise.all(item.files.map((f) => extractOne(f, signal)));
   193	    const { lines, images } = linesForItem(item);
   194	    if (item.application) {
   195	      const resp = await compare([{ item_id: item.key, application: item.application, lines, images }], { signal });
   196	      item.result = resp.results[0];
   197	      item.lines = lines;
   198	      item.images = images;
   199	    } else {
   200	      const ex = state.extractions.get(fileKey(item.files[0]));
   201	      item.result = null;
   202	      item.fields = ex.fields;
   203	      item.lines = lines;
   204	      item.images = images;
   205	    }
   206	    item.status = "done";
   207	  } catch (e) {
   208	    if (e.name === "AbortError") { item.status = "pending"; return; }
   209	    item.status = "error";
   210	    item.error = e instanceof ApiError ? `${e.message}${e.hint ? " " + e.hint : ""}` : "Network error.";
   211	  } finally {
   212	    item.ms = Math.round(performance.now() - t0);
   213	  }
   214	}
   215	
   216	function scheduleRender() {
   217	  if (state.renderTimer) return;
   218	  state.renderTimer = setTimeout(() => { state.renderTimer = 0; renderProgress(); renderTable(); }, 250);
   219	}
   220	
   221	async function runPool(items, signal) {
   222	  const queue = [...items];
   223	  let active = 0;
   224	  await new Promise((resolve) => {
   225	    const pump = () => {
   226	      if (signal.aborted && active === 0) return resolve();
   227	      while (!signal.aborted && active < state.maxConcurrency * 2 && queue.length) {
   228	        const item = queue.shift();
   229	        active++;
   230	        processItem(item, signal).finally(() => { active--; scheduleRender(); pump(); });
   231	      }
   232	      if (!queue.length && active === 0) resolve();
   233	    };
   234	    pump();
   235	  });
   236	}
   237	
   238	async function start() {
   239	  showError("");
   240	  if (!state.items.length || state.builtVersion !== state.inputVersion || state.items.every((i) => i.status === "done")) buildItems();
   241	  const pending = state.items.filter((i) => i.status === "pending" || i.status === "error");
   242	  if (!state.items.length) {
   243	    if (state.csv) {
   244	      const bad = state.csv.rows.filter((r) => r.errors.length).length;
   245	      showError(`The spreadsheet has no usable rows: ${bad} of ${state.csv.rows.length} have problems (listed under the spreadsheet). Fix them, or remove the spreadsheet to read the images on their own.`);
   246	    } else {
   247	      showError("Add images first.");
   248	    }
   249	    return;
   250	  }
   251	  try { const h = await health(); state.maxConcurrency = Math.max(1, Math.min(4, h.max_concurrency)); } catch { state.maxConcurrency = 2; }
   252	  state.concurrency = state.maxConcurrency;
   253	  state.running = true;
   254	  state.abort = new AbortController();
   255	  state.startedAt = state.startedAt || performance.now();
   256	  $("#batch-start").hidden = true;
   257	  $("#batch-cancel").hidden = false;
   258	  $("#batch-progress").hidden = false;
   259	  $("#batch-results").hidden = false;
   260	  $("#batch-summary").hidden = false;
   261	  for (const it of pending) { it.status = "pending"; it.error = null; }
   262	  renderProgress(); renderTable(); renderUnpaired();
   263	  await runPool(pending, state.abort.signal);
   264	  state.running = false;
   265	  $("#batch-cancel").hidden = true;
   266	  $("#batch-start").hidden = false;
   267	  $("#batch-start").textContent = state.items.some((i) => i.status !== "done" && i.status !== "no-images") ? "Resume" : "Run again";
   268	  $("#batch-export").hidden = false;
   269	  updateStartButton();
   270	  renderProgress(); renderSummary(); renderTable();
   271	}
   272	
   273	function pause() { state.abort?.abort(); setStatus("Pausing after the images in progress…", true); }
   274	
   275	// ------------------------------------------------------------------ rendering
   276	function counts() {
   277	  const c = { total: state.items.length, done: 0, ready: 0, review: 0, issues: 0, unreadable: 0, errors: 0, noimages: 0, decided: 0 };
   278	  for (const it of state.items) {
   279	    if (it.status === "done") c.done++;
   280	    if (it.status === "error") c.errors++;
   281	    if (it.status === "no-images") c.noimages++;
   282	    const v = it.result?.verdict;
   283	    if (v === "ready_for_approval") c.ready++; else if (v === "needs_review") c.review++; else if (v === "issues_found") c.issues++; else if (v === "unreadable") c.unreadable++;
   284	    if (state.decisions.get(it.key)?.decision) c.decided++;
   285	  }
   286	  return c;
   287	}
   288	
   289	function renderProgress() {
   290	  const c = counts();
   291	  const finished = c.done + c.errors;
   292	  const workable = c.total - c.noimages;
   293	  const pct = workable ? Math.round((100 * finished) / workable) : 0;
   294	  const bar = $("#batch-progress .progress__bar");
   295	  bar.setAttribute("aria-valuenow", String(pct));
   296	  bar.querySelector("span").style.width = `${pct}%`;
   297	  const elapsed = performance.now() - state.startedAt;
   298	  const avgItem = finished ? elapsed / finished : 0;
   299	  const remaining = workable - finished;
   300	  const eta = state.running && finished ? ` · about ${seconds(avgItem * remaining / Math.max(1, state.concurrency))} left` : "";
   301	  $("#batch-progress .progress__text").textContent = `${finished} of ${workable} applications checked (${pct}%)${eta}`;
   302	  setStatus(state.running ? `Reading images, ${state.concurrency} at a time…` : finished ? `Done. ${finished} checked in ${seconds(elapsed)}.` : "", state.running);
   303	  renderSummary();
   304	}
   305	
   306	function renderSummary() {
   307	  const c = counts();
   308	  const tile = (num, lbl) => el("div", { class: "summary-tile" }, [el("div", { class: "num", text: String(num) }), el("div", { class: "lbl", text: lbl })]);
   309	  const perImage = state.times.length ? `${seconds(percentile(state.times, 0.5))} median · ${seconds(percentile(state.times, 0.95))} p95` : "—";
   310	  $("#batch-summary").replaceChildren(
   311	    tile(c.ready, "Ready for approval"), tile(c.review, "Need review"), tile(c.issues + c.unreadable, "Issues / unreadable"),
   312	    tile(c.errors, "Errors"), tile(state.unpaired.length, "Images not matched"), tile(`${c.decided}/${c.total}`, "Decided"),
   313	    el("div", { class: "summary-tile" }, [el("div", { class: "num", text: perImage.split(" · ")[0].replace(" median", "") }), el("div", { class: "lbl", text: state.times.length ? `per image, median (p95 ${perImage.split(" · ")[1].replace(" p95", "")})` : "per image" })]),
   314	  );
   315	}
   316	
   317	function visibleItems() {
   318	  const dec = (it) => state.decisions.get(it.key)?.decision;
   319	  let items = state.items.filter((it) => {
   320	    const v = it.result?.verdict;
   321	    if (state.filter === "attention") return it.status === "error" || it.status === "no-images" || v === "issues_found" || v === "needs_review" || v === "unreadable";
   322	    if (state.filter === "ready") return v === "ready_for_approval";
   323	    if (state.filter === "undecided") return it.status === "done" && !dec(it);
   324	    return true;
   325	  });
   326	  items = items.sort((a, b) => (SEVERITY[a.result?.verdict] ?? 4) - (SEVERITY[b.result?.verdict] ?? 4) || (a.row ?? 0) - (b.row ?? 0));
   327	  return items;
   328	}
   329	
   330	function issueList(item) {
   331	  if (!item.result) return null;
   332	  const probs = issueTexts(item.result);
   333	  return probs.length ? el("ul", { class: "usa-list usa-list--unstyled issues" }, probs.map((p) => el("li", { text: p }))) : el("span", { class: "text-base", text: "All checks match" });
   334	}
   335	
   336	function decisionCell(item) {
   337	  return decisionControls(
   338	    () => state.decisions.get(item.key) || {},
   339	    (next) => { state.decisions.set(item.key, next); renderTable(); renderSummary(); },
   340	    `Note for ${item.key}`,
   341	  );
   342	}
   343	
   344	function renderTable() {
   345	  const items = visibleItems();
   346	  const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(items.length / PAGE_SIZE));
   347	  state.page = Math.min(state.page, pages - 1);
   348	  const slice = items.slice(state.page * PAGE_SIZE, (state.page + 1) * PAGE_SIZE);
   349	  const rows = [];
   350	  for (const it of slice) {
   351	    const v = it.result?.verdict;
   352	    const statusCell = it.status === "working" ? el("span", { class: "status-line is-busy", text: "Reading" })
   353	      : it.status === "error" ? el("span", { class: "status-tag status-mismatch", text: "Error" }) : it.status === "no-images" ? statusTag("not_checked")
   354	      : it.status === "pending" ? el("span", { class: "text-base", text: "Waiting" })
   355	      : v ? el("span", { class: `status-tag status-${VERDICT_STATUS[v]}`, text: VERDICT_WORD[v] }) : statusTag("info");
   356	    const tr = el("tr", { class: it.status === "error" ? "is-error" : "" }, [
   357	      el("td", {}, statusCell),
   358	      el("td", {}, [el("div", { class: "app-id", text: it.application?.application_id || it.key }),
   359	        el("div", { class: "app-brand", text: it.application ? `${it.application.brand_name} · ${it.application.class_type}` : (it.fields?.largest_text ? `Read: ${it.fields.largest_text}` : "") })]),
   360	      el("td", {}, [el("div", { class: "files", text: it.files.map((f) => f.name).join(", ") || "none" }), el("div", { class: "files", text: it.method }),
   361	        it.missing && it.missing.length ? el("div", { class: "files text-secondary-dark", text: `Listed but not uploaded: ${it.missing.join(", ")}` }) : null]),
   362	      el("td", {}, it.status === "error" ? el("span", { class: "text-secondary-dark", text: it.error }) : it.status === "no-images" ? el("span", { text: "No images matched this row." })
   363	        : it.application ? issueList(it) : (it.fields ? el("ul", { class: "usa-list usa-list--unstyled issues" }, [
   364	          el("li", { text: `Alcohol: ${it.fields.alcohol_percent != null ? it.fields.alcohol_percent + "%" : "not read"}` }),
   365	          el("li", { text: `Net contents: ${it.fields.net_contents_ml.length ? it.fields.net_contents_ml.map((m) => m + " mL").join(", ") : "not read"}` }),
   366	          el("li", { text: `Warning: ${it.fields.warning_present ? "present" : "not found"}` })]) : null)),
   367	      el("td", {}, it.status === "done" && it.application ? decisionCell(it) : null),
   368	      el("td", {}, it.status === "done" ? el("button", { type: "button", class: "usa-button usa-button--unstyled", text: state.expanded === it.key ? "Hide" : "Details",
   369	        "aria-expanded": state.expanded === it.key ? "true" : "false", onclick: () => { state.expanded = state.expanded === it.key ? null : it.key; renderTable(); } }) : null),
   370	    ]);
   371	    rows.push(tr);
   372	    if (state.expanded === it.key && it.status === "done") {
   373	      let panel = state.detailCache.get(it.key);
   374	      if (!panel) {
   375	        panel = el("div", { class: "detail-panel" });
   376	        state.detailCache.set(it.key, panel);
   377	        renderDetail(panel, it);
   378	      }
   379	      rows.push(el("tr", { class: "detail-row" }, el("td", { colspan: "6" }, panel)));
   380	    }
   381	  }
   382	  $("#batch-table").replaceChildren(el("table", { class: "usa-table usa-table--stacked batch-table" }, [
   383	    el("thead", {}, el("tr", {}, ["Result", "Application", "Images", "What to look at", "Your decision", ""].map((h) => el("th", { scope: "col", text: h })))),
   384	    el("tbody", {}, rows.length ? rows : el("tr", {}, el("td", { colspan: "6", text: "Nothing to show for this filter." }))),
   385	  ]));
   386	  $("#pager-text").textContent = `Page ${state.page + 1} of ${pages} · ${items.length} application${items.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;
   387	  $("#pager-prev").disabled = state.page === 0;
   388	  $("#pager-next").disabled = state.page >= pages - 1;
   389	}
   390	
   391	async function renderDetail(panel, it) {
   392	  if (!it.result) {
   393	    panel.replaceChildren(el("p", { text: "Read without application data. Lines:" }), el("ul", { class: "ocr-lines" }, it.lines.map((l) => el("li", { text: l.text }))));
   394	    return;
   395	  }
   396	  const checklist = el("div"), warning = el("div"), figures = el("div");
   397	  panel.replaceChildren(el("p", { class: "text-bold", text: it.result.summary }), el("div", { class: "grid-row grid-gap-4" }, [
   398	    el("div", { class: "desktop:grid-col-7" }, [checklist, el("h4", { text: "Government warning" }), warning]),
   399	    el("div", { class: "desktop:grid-col-5" }, figures)]));
   400	  const crops = await makeCrops(it.files, it.images, it.result.checks);
   401	  const overlays = renderFigures(figures, it.images, it.files, it.lines);
   402	  const select = (check) => { drawOverlays(overlays, it.result.checks, it.result.warning, check.id); panel.querySelectorAll(".checklist tbody tr").forEach((tr) => tr.classList.toggle("is-active", tr.dataset.check === check.id)); };
   403	  renderChecklist(checklist, it.result.checks, select, crops);
   404	  renderWarning(warning, it.result.warning, select);
   405	  drawOverlays(overlays, it.result.checks, it.result.warning, null);
   406	}
   407	
   408	function renderUnpaired() {
   409	  const sec = $("#batch-unpaired");
   410	  sec.hidden = state.unpaired.length === 0;
   411	  const list = $("#unpaired-list");
   412	  list.replaceChildren(...state.unpaired.map((name) => {
   413	    const f = state.images.get(name);
   414	    const url = URL.createObjectURL(f);
   415	    const img = el("img", { src: url, alt: "" });
   416	    img.addEventListener("load", () => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), { once: true });
   417	    const sel = el("select", { class: "usa-select", "aria-label": `Application for ${f.name}` }, [
   418	      el("option", { value: "", text: "Choose an application…" }),
   419	      ...state.items.filter((i) => i.application).map((i) => el("option", { value: i.key, text: `${i.key} · ${i.application.brand_name}` })),
   420	    ]);
   421	    sel.addEventListener("change", async () => {
   422	      const it = state.items.find((i) => i.key === sel.value);
   423	      if (!it) return;
   424	      it.files.push(f); it.method = "assigned by agent"; it.status = "pending"; state.detailCache.delete(it.key);
   425	      state.unpaired = state.unpaired.filter((n) => n !== name);
   426	      renderUnpaired();
   427	      const ac = new AbortController();
   428	      await processItem(it, ac.signal);
   429	      renderTable(); renderSummary();
   430	    });
   431	    return el("li", {}, [img, el("div", {}, [el("div", { class: "file-name", text: f.name })]), sel]);
   432	  }));
   433	}

   436	function exportCsv() {
   437	  const rows = state.items.map((it) => exportRow({
   438	    application: it.application || (it.fields?.largest_text ? { brand_name: it.fields.largest_text } : null),
   439	    key: it.key, result: it.result, status: it.status, error: it.error,
   440	    decision: state.decisions.get(it.key), files: it.files, elapsedMs: it.ms,
   441	  }));
   442	  downloadCsv(`label-check-batch-${exportStamp()}.csv`, rows);
   443	}

   446	async function loadDemo() {
   447	  setStatus("Loading demo batch…", true);
   448	  const names = ["APP-001_front.png", "APP-001_back.png", "APP-002_front.png", "APP-002_back.png", "APP-003_front.png", "APP-003_back.png",
   449	    "APP-005_front.png", "APP-005_back.png", "APP-007_front.png", "APP-007_back.png"];
   450	  const files = [];
   451	  for (const n of names) { const b = await (await fetch(`/static/samples/batch/${n}`)).blob(); files.push(new File([b], n, { type: "image/png" })); }
   452	  state.images = new Map(); state.items = []; state.extractions = new Map(); state.decisions = new Map(); state.times = []; state.startedAt = 0;
   453	  addImages(files);
   454	  const csvBlob = await (await fetch("/static/samples/batch/applications.csv")).blob();
   455	  await setCsv(new File([csvBlob], "applications.csv", { type: "text/csv" }));
   456	  setStatus("");
   457	  await start();
   458	}

   460	// ------------------------------------------------------------------ boot
   461	function boot() {
   462	  const dz = $("#batch-dropzone");
   463	  if (!dz) return;
   464	  ["dragenter", "dragover"].forEach((t) => dz.addEventListener(t, (e) => { e.preventDefault(); dz.classList.add("is-dragover"); }));
   465	  ["dragleave", "drop"].forEach((t) => dz.addEventListener(t, (e) => { e.preventDefault(); dz.classList.remove("is-dragover"); }));
   466	  dz.addEventListener("drop", (e) => addImages(e.dataTransfer.files));
   467	  $("#batch-files").addEventListener("change", (e) => { addImages(e.target.files); e.target.value = ""; });
   468	  $("#batch-folder").addEventListener("change", (e) => { addImages(e.target.files); e.target.value = ""; });
   469	  $("#batch-csv").addEventListener("change", (e) => { if (e.target.files[0]) setCsv(e.target.files[0]); e.target.value = ""; });
   470	  $("#batch-start").addEventListener("click", start);
   471	  $("#batch-cancel").addEventListener("click", pause);
   472	  $("#batch-export").addEventListener("click", exportCsv);
   473	  $("#batch-demo").addEventListener("click", loadDemo);
   474	  document.querySelectorAll("[data-filter]").forEach((b) => b.addEventListener("click", () => {
   475	    state.filter = b.dataset.filter; state.page = 0;
   476	    document.querySelectorAll("[data-filter]").forEach((x) => x.classList.toggle("is-on", x === b));
   477	    renderTable();
   478	  }));
   479	  $("#pager-prev").addEventListener("click", () => { state.page--; renderTable(); });
   480	  $("#pager-next").addEventListener("click", () => { state.page++; renderTable(); });
   481	  window.addEventListener("beforeunload", (e) => { if (state.items.some((i) => i.status === "done")) { e.preventDefault(); e.returnValue = ""; } });
   482	}
   483	
   484	boot();


===== FILE: app/static/theme.js (current text at 3d9bdad, line-numbered) =====
     1	// Display theme, applied before the first paint so a dark choice never flashes light.
     2	// The choice ("light", "dark" or "system") lives in this browser's localStorage only; when storage
     3	// is unavailable (private windows, locked-down browsers) the page simply stays light.
     4	(function () {
     5	  var KEY = "labelcheck-theme";
     6	  function stored() {
     7	    try { return localStorage.getItem(KEY) || "light"; } catch (e) { return "light"; }
     8	  }
     9	  function resolve(choice) {
    10	    if (choice === "system") {
    11	      return window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)").matches ? "dark" : "light";
    12	    }
    13	    return choice === "dark" ? "dark" : "light";
    14	  }
    15	  function apply(choice) {
    16	    var c = choice || stored();
    17	    document.documentElement.dataset.themeChoice = c;
    18	    document.documentElement.dataset.theme = resolve(c);
    19	  }
    20	  apply();
    21	  if (window.matchMedia) {
    22	    window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)").addEventListener("change", function () { apply(); });
    23	  }
    24	  window.lcApplyTheme = function (choice) {
    25	    if (choice) { try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, choice); } catch (e) { /* stays for this page only */ } }
    26	    apply(choice);
    27	  };
    28	})();

   241	    <!-- ============================================================ About -->
   242	    <section id="view-about" class="usa-section padding-top-4" data-view-panel="about" hidden>
   243	      <div class="grid-container usa-prose">
   244	        <h1 class="margin-top-0">About this tool</h1>
   245	        <h2>What it does</h2>
   246	        <p>It reads the text on label images with an on-device text-recognition model, compares what it read with what the application says, checks the government warning statement word for word against 27 CFR 16.21, and shows you exactly where on the label it found each item. It works in a few seconds and makes no calls to outside services.</p>
   247	        <h2>What it does not do</h2>
   248	        <ul>
   249	          <li>It does not approve or reject applications. Every result is a recommendation for a compliance agent.</li>
   250	          <li>It does not store images, application data, or results. Refreshing the page clears everything.</li>
   251	          <li>It does not check permits, formulas, class/type validity, age statements or laboratory tolerances.</li>
   252	          <li>It does not measure physical type size or bold type from a photograph reliably; those items are marked for you to confirm.</li>
   253	        </ul>
   254	        <h2>How it decides</h2>
   255	        <p><strong>Match</strong> means the label says the same thing, allowing for letter case and accents. <strong>Needs review</strong> means it is close but you should look, which is usually OCR noise. <strong>Mismatch</strong> and <strong>Not found</strong> mean the label disagrees or the item could not be read. For the warning statement, only an exact match passes.</p>
   256	        <h2>For engineers</h2>
   257	        <p>API description: <a href="/api/v1/openapi.json">/api/v1/openapi.json</a>. Health and capacity: <a href="/api/v1/health">/api/v1/health</a>. Source code and documentation: <a id="repo-link" href="https://github.com/danielrcooley/ttb-label-check">repository</a>.</p>
   258	      </div>
   259	    </section>
   260

   271	            <div class="usa-radio"><input class="usa-radio__input usa-radio__input--tile" id="theme-dark" type="radio" name="theme" value="dark"><label class="usa-radio__label" for="theme-dark">Dark<span class="usa-radio__label-description">Light text on a dark page</span></label></div>

   273	          </div>
   274	        </fieldset>
   275	        <h2>How this tool is built to be usable</h2>
   276	        <ul>
   277	          <li>Built with the U.S. Web Design System. Target: WCAG 2.1 AA and Section 508.</li>
   278	          <li>Every status is an icon, a word and a color, never a color alone. A pressed button carries a check mark in its text.</li>
   279	          <li>Works with the keyboard alone, at 200 percent zoom, on a phone, and in Windows contrast themes; progress and results are announced to screen readers.</li>
   280	          <li>No time limits; the only motion is a progress spinner, which stops when your system asks for reduced motion.</li>
   281	        </ul>
   282	        <h2>If something does not work for you</h2>
   283	        <p>Please say so in the <a id="repo-link-a11y" href="https://github.com/danielrcooley/ttb-label-check">project repository</a>, with the page, the browser and what happened.</p>
   284	      </div>
