===== FILE: tools/evaluate.py (current text at 3d9bdad, line-numbered) =====
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python
     2	"""Accuracy and latency evaluation on the synthetic corpus, through the real pipeline.
     3	
     4	Tiers (from tests/fixtures/labels/manifest.json):
     5	  artwork   clean front + clean back for every application
     6	  degraded  each degraded image paired with its clean sibling (rotate, blur, glare, low contrast,
     7	            perspective, small, jpeg, sideways)
     8	  problem   labels with a planted defect; we check the defect is reported
     9	
    10	Reports per-field match rate on the artwork tier (recall), the false-alarm rate (checks on
    11	clean artwork that did not come back as Match), degraded-tier match rate, problem detection,
    12	and per-application latency. Writes docs/EVAL.md and docs/eval.json.
    13	
    14	Usage:
    15	    python tools/evaluate.py [--labels tests/fixtures/labels] [--workers 2] [--out docs/EVAL.md]
    16	"""
    17	
    18	from __future__ import annotations
    19	
    20	import argparse
    21	import asyncio
    22	import json
    23	import math
    24	import statistics
    25	import sys
    26	import time
    27	from collections import Counter, defaultdict
    28	from pathlib import Path
    29	
    30	sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
    31	
    32	from app.config import Settings
    33	from app.ocr.pool import OcrPool
    34	from app.ocr.rapid import RapidEngine
    35	from app.schemas import ApplicationFields
    36	from app.services import Upload, verify
    37	
    38	FIELDS = ["brand_name", "class_type", "alcohol_content", "net_contents", "bottler", "country_of_origin"]
    39	DEGRADATIONS = {"rotate7", "blur", "glare", "lowcontrast", "perspective", "small", "jpeg", "rotate90"}
    40	PROBLEM_EXPECTATIONS = {
    41	    "wrong_abv": lambda r: any(c.id == "alcohol_content" and c.status == "mismatch" for c in r.checks),
    42	    "titlecase": lambda r: r.warning.present and r.warning.anchor_caps == "needs_review",
    43	    "altered": lambda r: r.warning.present and not r.warning.exact and r.verdict == "issues_found",
    44	    "missing": lambda r: not r.warning.present and r.verdict == "issues_found",
    45	    "tiny": None,  # planted defect the tool does not assess (physical type size)
    46	    "allbold": lambda r: r.warning.present and r.warning.body_not_bold == "needs_review",
    47	}
    48	
    49	
    50	def app_fields(app: dict) -> ApplicationFields:
    51	    return ApplicationFields(
    52	        application_id=app["id"],
    53	        beverage_type=app["beverage_type"],
    54	        brand_name=app["brand"],
    55	        class_type=app["class_type"],
    56	        alcohol_content=app["alcohol_content"],
    57	        net_contents=app["net_contents"],
    58	        bottler=app["bottler"],
    59	        country_of_origin=app["origin"].replace("Product of ", ""),
    60	        imported="USA" not in app["origin"],
    61	    )
    62	
    63	
    64	async def run(labels: Path, workers: int) -> dict:
    65	    settings = Settings(ocr_workers=workers)
    66	    pool = OcrPool(settings, lambda: RapidEngine(settings))
    67	    pool.warmup()
    68	    manifest = json.loads((labels / "manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    69	
    70	    def upload(name: str) -> Upload:
    71	        return Upload(data=(labels / name).read_bytes(), filename=name)
    72	
    73	    tiers: dict[str, list[dict]] = defaultdict(list)
    74	    for app in manifest["applications"]:
    75	        imgs = {im["variant"] + ":" + im["side"]: im["file"] for im in app["images"]}
    76	        front, back = imgs.get("clean:front"), imgs.get("clean:back")
    77	        fields = app_fields(app)
    78	        cases = [("artwork", "clean", [front, back])]
    79	        for im in app["images"]:
    80	            v = im["variant"]
    81	            if v in DEGRADATIONS:
    82	                pair = [im["file"], back] if im["side"] == "front" else [front, im["file"]]
    83	                cases.append(("degraded", v, pair))
    84	            elif v in PROBLEM_EXPECTATIONS:
    85	                pair = [im["file"], back] if im["side"] == "front" else [front, im["file"]]
    86	                cases.append(("problem", v, pair))
    87	        for tier, variant, files in cases:
    88	            t0 = time.perf_counter()
    89	            res = await verify(fields, [upload(f) for f in files], settings, pool, interactive=True)
    90	            ms = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
    91	            tiers[tier].append(
    92	                {
    93	                    "app": app["id"],
    94	                    "variant": variant,
    95	                    "files": files,
    96	                    "ms": ms,
    97	                    "verdict": res.verdict,
    98	                    "checks": {c.id: c.status for c in res.checks},
    99	                    "warning_exact": res.warning.exact,
   100	                    "warning_present": res.warning.present,
   101	                    "detected": (PROBLEM_EXPECTATIONS[variant](res) if PROBLEM_EXPECTATIONS.get(variant) else None)
   102	                    if tier == "problem"
   103	                    else None,
   104	                    "assessed": tier == "problem" and PROBLEM_EXPECTATIONS.get(variant) is not None,
   105	                }
   106	            )
   107	    pool.shutdown()
   108	    return {"tiers": tiers, "engine": pool.info(), "workers": workers}
   109	
   110	
   111	def summarize(data: dict) -> tuple[str, dict]:
   112	    out: list[str] = [
   113	        "# Evaluation",
   114	        "",
   115	        "_Generated by `tools/evaluate.py` on the committed synthetic corpus (10 fictional "
   116	        "applications; front + back per case). Numbers are from the real pipeline on the machine "
   117	        "that ran it; deployed numbers are in the README._",
   118	        "",
   119	        "_Engine: {engine}; alphabet: {alphabet}; det {det}; rec {rec}; workers: {workers}._".format(
   120	            engine=data.get("engine", {}).get("engine", "?"),
   121	            alphabet=data.get("engine", {}).get("alphabet", "full"),
   122	            det=data.get("engine", {}).get("det", "?"),
   123	            rec=data.get("engine", {}).get("rec", "?"),
   124	            workers=data.get("workers", "?"),
   125	        ),
   126	        "",
   127	    ]
   128	    summary: dict = {}
   129	    for tier in ("artwork", "degraded", "problem"):
   130	        cases = data["tiers"].get(tier, [])
   131	        if not cases:
   132	            continue
   133	        lat = [c["ms"] for c in cases]
   134	        summary[tier] = {
   135	            "cases": len(cases),
   136	            "p50_ms": statistics.median(lat),
   137	            "p95_ms": sorted(lat)[max(0, math.ceil(0.95 * len(lat)) - 1)],  # nearest rank
   138	        }
   139	        out += [
   140	            f"## {tier.capitalize()} tier ({len(cases)} cases)",
   141	            "",
   142	            f"- Per-application latency (two images, parallel): median {summary[tier]['p50_ms']:.0f} ms, "
   143	            f"p95 {summary[tier]['p95_ms']:.0f} ms",
   144	            "",
   145	        ]
   146	        if tier in ("artwork", "degraded"):
   147	            out += ["| Field | Match | Needs review | Mismatch / not found | Match rate |", "|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|"]
   148	            rates = {}
   149	            for f in FIELDS:
   150	                cnt = Counter(c["checks"].get(f, "absent") for c in cases)
   151	                n = sum(v for k, v in cnt.items() if k != "absent")
   152	                match = cnt.get("match", 0)
   153	                rates[f] = match / n if n else None
   154	                out.append(
   155	                    f"| {f} | {match} | {cnt.get('needs_review', 0)} | {cnt.get('mismatch', 0) + cnt.get('not_found', 0)} | "
   156	                    f"{100 * match / n:.0f}% |"
   157	                    if n
   158	                    else f"| {f} | - | - | - | - |"
   159	                )
   160	            wex = sum(1 for c in cases if c["warning_exact"])
   161	            out.append(
   162	                f"| warning exact | {wex} | {sum(1 for c in cases if c['warning_present'] and not c['warning_exact'])} | "
   163	                f"{sum(1 for c in cases if not c['warning_present'])} | {100 * wex / len(cases):.0f}% |"
   164	            )
   165	            verdicts = Counter(c["verdict"] for c in cases)
   166	            out += ["", f"- Verdicts: {dict(verdicts)}"]
   167	            if tier == "artwork":
   168	                total_checks = sum(1 for c in cases for f in FIELDS if f in c["checks"])
   169	                non_match = sum(1 for c in cases for f in FIELDS if f in c["checks"] and c["checks"][f] != "match")
   170	                summary["false_alarm_rate"] = non_match / total_checks if total_checks else 0
   171	                out.append(
   172	                    f"- **False-alarm rate on clean artwork: {100 * summary['false_alarm_rate']:.1f}%** "
   173	                    f"({non_match} of {total_checks} field checks not reported as Match)"
   174	                )
   175	            summary[tier]["rates"] = rates
   176	            out.append("")
   177	            if tier == "degraded":
   178	                out += ["| Degradation | cases | verdicts | median ms |", "|---|---:|---|---:|"]
   179	                by = defaultdict(list)
   180	                for c in cases:
   181	                    by[c["variant"]].append(c)
   182	                for v, cs in sorted(by.items()):
   183	                    out.append(
   184	                        f"| {v} | {len(cs)} | {dict(Counter(x['verdict'] for x in cs))} | {statistics.median(x['ms'] for x in cs):.0f} |"
   185	                    )
   186	                out.append("")
   187	        else:
   188	            out += ["| Planted defect | detected | note |", "|---|---|---|"]
   189	            notes = {
   190	                "tiny": "NOT ASSESSED by design: physical type size",
   191	            }
   192	            det = 0
   193	            assessed = [c for c in cases if c["assessed"]]
   194	            for c in cases:
   195	                det += bool(c["detected"])
   196	                mark = ("yes" if c["detected"] else "NO") if c["assessed"] else "n/a"
   197	                out.append(f"| {c['variant']} ({c['app']}) | {mark} | {notes.get(c['variant'], '')} |")
   198	            summary["problem_detection"] = det / len(assessed) if assessed else None
   199	            out += [
   200	                "",
   201	                f"- Detection rate over the defects the tool assesses: {det}/{len(assessed)}; "
   202	                f"{len(cases) - len(assessed)} planted defects are outside this build's checks and are listed as n/a",
   203	                "",
   204	            ]
   205	    return "\n".join(out), summary
   206	
   207	
   208	def main() -> None:
   209	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
   210	    ap.add_argument("--labels", default="tests/fixtures/labels")
   211	    ap.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=2)
   212	    ap.add_argument("--out", default="docs/EVAL.md")
   213	    args = ap.parse_args()
   214	    data = asyncio.run(run(Path(args.labels), args.workers))
   215	    md, summary = summarize(data)
   216	    Path(args.out).write_text(md, encoding="utf-8")
   217	    Path(args.out).with_name("eval.json").write_text(
   218	        json.dumps({"summary": summary, **data}, indent=1, default=str), encoding="utf-8"
   219	    )
   220	    print(md)
   221	
   222	
   223	if __name__ == "__main__":
   224	    main()


===== FILE: tools/evaluate_real.py (current text at 3d9bdad, line-numbered) =====
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python
     2	"""Real-world evaluation on approved COLAs fetched by tools/cola_fetch.py.
     3	
     4	The COLA form carries brand name, class/type, origin and the applicant's name and address, but not
     5	alcohol content or net contents, so this runs the extraction path (no application needed) and
     6	scores what the registry knows:
     7	
     8	- brand name, class/type description, applicant name: the product's own matcher (best_span +
     9	  status_for) against the OCR lines, reported as Match / Needs review / Mismatch / Not found;
    10	- country of origin, imports only: the country name found on the label (fuzzy, partial);
    11	- government warning: the product's warning comparator, reported as exact / case / noise /
    12	  wording / absent, plus the capital-letters check on the heading;
    13	- alcohol content and net contents: whether a statement was read at all;
    14	- per-record latency, image count and image size.
    15	
    16	Writes docs/EVAL_REAL.md (aggregate numbers only) and <real>/results.csv (per record, for hand
    17	review; stays local with the images).
    18	
    19	Usage:
    20	    python tools/evaluate_real.py [--real tests/fixtures/real] [--workers 2] [--out docs/EVAL_REAL.md]
    21	"""
    22	
    23	from __future__ import annotations
    24	
    25	import argparse
    26	import asyncio
    27	import csv
    28	import json
    29	import math
    30	import statistics
    31	import sys
    32	import time
    33	from collections import Counter, defaultdict
    34	from collections.abc import Callable
    35	from pathlib import Path
    36	from typing import Any
    37	
    38	from rapidfuzz import fuzz
    39	
    40	sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
    41	
    42	from app.config import Settings
    43	from app.ocr.pool import OcrPool
    44	from app.ocr.rapid import RapidEngine
    45	from app.pipeline.compare import bottler_check
    46	from app.pipeline.match import best_span, status_for
    47	from app.pipeline.normalize import fold
    48	from app.pipeline.warning import build_report
    49	from app.schemas import OcrLine
    50	from app.services import Upload, extract
    51	
    52	Record = dict[str, Any]
    53	
    54	
    55	def _fit(path: Path, max_pixels: int) -> bytes:
    56	    """The registry occasionally stores artwork above the product's 25 MP limit (the product answers
    57	    413 and asks for a smaller file). For the evaluation, do what the user would: shrink it."""
    58	    from io import BytesIO
    59	
    60	    from PIL import Image
    61	
    62	    data = path.read_bytes()
    63	    with Image.open(BytesIO(data)) as im:
    64	        w, h = im.size
    65	        if w * h <= max_pixels:
    66	            return data
    67	        scale = (max_pixels / (w * h)) ** 0.5 * 0.98
    68	        im = im.convert("RGB").resize((max(1, int(w * scale)), max(1, int(h * scale))), Image.LANCZOS)
    69	        buf = BytesIO()
    70	        im.save(buf, "PNG")
    71	        print(f"{path.name}: {w}x{h} shrunk to fit {max_pixels // 1_000_000} MP", flush=True)
    72	        return buf.getvalue()
    73	
    74	
    75	def _status(expected: str, lines: list[OcrLine], s: Settings) -> tuple[str, str, int | None]:
    76	    cand = best_span(expected, lines)
    77	    st, _ = status_for(cand, expected, review_at=s.match_review_threshold, mismatch_at=s.match_mismatch_threshold)
    78	    return str(st), (cand.text if cand else ""), (int(cand.score) if cand else None)
    79	
    80	
    81	def _applicant_status(registered: str, lines: list[OcrLine], s: Settings) -> tuple[str, str, int | None]:
    82	    """The product's own bottler check on the registry's item 8 line, exactly as the batch screen
    83	    would receive it from a spreadsheet (names, address and the name used on the label, comma
    84	    separated)."""
    85	    check = bottler_check(registered, lines, s)
    86	    return str(check.status), check.found or "", int(check.score) if check.score is not None else None
    87	
    88	
    89	async def run(real: Path, workers: int) -> tuple[list[Record], Record]:
    90	    settings = Settings(ocr_workers=workers)
    91	    pool = OcrPool(settings, lambda: RapidEngine(settings))
    92	    pool.warmup()
    93	    with (real / "cola.csv").open(encoding="utf-8", newline="") as f:
    94	        rows = list(csv.DictReader(f))
    95	    results: list[Record] = []
    96	    for row in rows:
    97	        files = [n for n in row["images"].split(";") if n and (real / n).exists()]
    98	        if not files:
    99	            continue
   100	        uploads = [Upload(data=_fit(real / n, settings.max_image_pixels), filename=n) for n in files]
   101	        t0 = time.perf_counter()
   102	        res = await extract(uploads, settings, pool, interactive=True)
   103	        ms = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
   104	        lines = res.lines
   105	        applicant_line = ", ".join(part.strip() for part in row["applicant"].split(" | ") if part.strip())
   106	        rec: Record = {
   107	            "ttbid": row["ttbid"],
   108	            "beverage_type": row["beverage_type"],
   109	            "images": len(files),
   110	            "long_side_px": max((max(im.width, im.height) for im in res.images), default=0),
   111	            "readable": all(im.quality.readable for im in res.images),
   112	            "rotated": any(im.rotated_degrees for im in res.images),
   113	            "lines": len(lines),
   114	            "ms": ms,
   115	        }
   116	        for field, expected in (("brand", row["brand"]), ("class_desc", row["class_desc"])):
   117	            st, found, score = _status(expected, lines, settings) if expected else ("n/a", "", None)
   118	            rec[f"{field}_status"], rec[f"{field}_found"], rec[f"{field}_score"] = st, found, score
   119	            rec[f"{field}_expected"] = expected
   120	        st, found, score = _applicant_status(applicant_line, lines, settings) if applicant_line else ("n/a", "", None)
   121	        rec["applicant_name_status"], rec["applicant_name_found"], rec["applicant_name_score"] = st, found, score
   122	        rec["applicant_name_expected"] = applicant_line
   123	        if row["domestic"] == "no" and row["origin_desc"]:
   124	            country = fold(row["origin_desc"])
   125	            rec["origin_found"] = any(fuzz.partial_ratio(country, fold(ln.text)) >= 90 for ln in lines)
   126	        else:
   127	            rec["origin_found"] = None
   128	        w = build_report(lines, mismatch_similarity=settings.warning_mismatch_similarity)
   129	        rec.update(
   130	            {
   131	                "warning_present": w.present,
   132	                "warning_assessment": w.assessment,
   133	                "warning_anchor_caps": str(w.anchor_caps),
   134	                "warning_type_weight": str(w.anchor_bold),  # match / needs_review / not_checked (D-044)
   135	                "warning_similarity": round(w.similarity, 3) if w.similarity is not None else None,
   136	                "warning_diff": (w.diff or "")[:300],
   137	                "alcohol_read": res.fields.alcohol_percent is not None,
   138	                "alcohol_percent": res.fields.alcohol_percent,
   139	                "net_read": bool(res.fields.net_contents_ml),
   140	                "net_ml": ";".join(f"{v:g}" for v in res.fields.net_contents_ml),
   141	            }
   142	        )
   143	        results.append(rec)
   144	        print(
   145	            f"{rec['ttbid']} {rec['beverage_type']:7s} {rec['images']} img {rec['ms']:5d} ms  brand={rec['brand_status']:12s} "
   146	            f"class={rec['class_desc_status']:12s} applicant={rec['applicant_name_status']:12s} warning={w.assessment}",
   147	            flush=True,
   148	        )
   149	    pool.shutdown()
   150	    return results, {"engine": pool.info(), "workers": workers}
   151	
   152	
   153	def pct(n: int, d: int) -> str:
   154	    return f"{100 * n / d:.0f}%" if d else "n/a"
   155	
   156	
   157	def summarize(results: list[Record], meta: Record, window: str) -> str:
   158	    out = [
   159	        "# Evaluation on real approved COLAs",
   160	        "",
   161	        f"_Generated by `tools/evaluate_real.py` on {len(results)} approved label applications fetched from TTB's "
   162	        f"Public COLA Registry by `tools/cola_fetch.py` ({window}). The images and per-record results stay local "
   163	        "(the artwork belongs to the brand owners); this file holds aggregate numbers only._",
   164	        "",
   165	        f"_Engine: {meta['engine'].get('engine', '?')}; alphabet: {meta['engine'].get('alphabet', 'full')}; workers: {meta['workers']}._",
   166	        "",
   167	        "## Read this first",
   168	        "",
   169	        "- The registry stores the applicant's uploads, usually flat artwork, often downscaled by the registry "
   170	        "(see the image size row). This is the artwork tier of the real world, not the phone-photo tier.",
   171	        "- The COLA form has no alcohol content or net contents fields, so for those only the read rate is reported.",
   172	        '- Class/type is the registry\'s code description ("STRAIGHT BOURBON WHISKY", "TABLE RED WINE"), not the '
   173	        'label\'s wording ("Kentucky Straight Bourbon Whiskey", "Cabernet Sauvignon"), so the class row understates '
   174	        "what the tool would do with the application's actual wording. In the product a class/type that does not "
   175	        "match is a review item with the closest text, never an issue (D-041); this row reports the raw text match.",
   176	        '- The applicant is the permit holder; a label may lawfully name a different bottler ("bottled for"), '
   177	        "so the applicant row also understates.",
   178	        "- There is no ground truth for the warning statement beyond TTB's approval; the exact rate is what the "
   179	        "comparator reports on the registry's image, and the hand-checked cases are listed at the end.",
   180	        '- "Statement located" means the heading was found and a span accumulated behind it, at any similarity; '
   181	        "the exact / slips / wording split below it is the accuracy, not the located rate.",
   182	        '- "Country of origin found" is a proxy: the registry\'s country name matched somewhere on the label at '
   183	        "partial-ratio 90 or better, without checking that it sits in an origin statement.",
   184	        "- The alcohol and net-contents read rates count a parse anywhere in the concatenated text of all the "
   185	        "record's images, as the extract-only mode of the product does.",
   186	        "- Latency is the service call for the record (decode, all reads, the extra round when it runs); the file "
   187	        "read and the pre-fit to the pixel cap are outside it. p95 is the nearest-rank percentile.",
   188	        "- The applicant row runs the product's own bottler check on the registry's full item 8 line (names, "
   189	        "address, the name used on the label), exactly as a spreadsheet would carry it (D-041).",
   190	        "- Images above the product's 25 megapixel limit are shrunk to fit before the run; the deployed service "
   191	        "refuses them with a message instead.",
   192	        "",
   193	    ]
   194	    groups: dict[str, list[Record]] = defaultdict(list)
   195	    for r in results:
   196	        groups[r["beverage_type"]].append(r)
   197	    groups["all"] = results
   198	
   199	    def row(label: str, fn: Callable[[list[Record]], str]) -> str:
   200	        return (
   201	            "| "
   202	            + label
   203	            + " | "
   204	            + " | ".join(fn(groups[k]) for k in ("spirits", "wine", "malt", "all") if k in groups)
   205	            + " |"
   206	        )
   207	
   208	    heads = [k for k in ("spirits", "wine", "malt", "all") if k in groups]
   209	    out += ["| | " + " | ".join(f"{k} (n={len(groups[k])})" for k in heads) + " |", "|---|" + "---:|" * len(heads)]
   210	
   211	    def rate(field: str, statuses: tuple[str, ...]) -> Callable[[list[Record]], str]:
   212	        return lambda g: pct(sum(r[f"{field}_status"] in statuses for r in g), len(g))
   213	
   214	    for field, label in (
   215	        ("brand", "Brand name"),
   216	        ("class_desc", "Class/type (registry description)"),
   217	        ("applicant_name", "Applicant name"),
   218	    ):
   219	        out.append(row(f"{label}: match", rate(field, ("match",))))
   220	        out.append(row(f"{label}: match or needs review", rate(field, ("match", "needs_review"))))
   221	    out.append(
   222	        row(
   223	            "Country of origin found (imports only)",
   224	            lambda g: pct(sum(bool(r["origin_found"]) for r in g), sum(r["origin_found"] is not None for r in g)),
   225	        )
   226	    )
   227	    out.append(
   228	        row("Warning statement located (heading found)", lambda g: pct(sum(r["warning_present"] for r in g), len(g)))
   229	    )
   230	    out.append(row("Warning exact (of all)", lambda g: pct(sum(r["warning_assessment"] == "exact" for r in g), len(g))))
   231	    out.append(
   232	        row(
   233	            "Warning exact (of located)",
   234	            lambda g: pct(sum(r["warning_assessment"] == "exact" for r in g), sum(r["warning_present"] for r in g)),
   235	        )
   236	    )
   237	    out.append(
   238	        row(
   239	            "Warning heading all capitals (of located)",
   240	            lambda g: pct(sum(r["warning_anchor_caps"] == "match" for r in g), sum(r["warning_present"] for r in g)),
   241	        )
   242	    )
   243	    out.append(
   244	        row(
   245	            "Warning heading measured heavier than the body (of located)",
   246	            lambda g: pct(
   247	                sum(r.get("warning_type_weight") == "match" for r in g), sum(r["warning_present"] for r in g)
   248	            ),
   249	        )
   250	    )
   251	    out.append(
   252	        row(
   253	            "Warning type weight not measured, small print or inconclusive (of located)",
   254	            lambda g: pct(
   255	                sum(r.get("warning_type_weight") == "not_checked" for r in g), sum(r["warning_present"] for r in g)
   256	            ),
   257	        )
   258	    )
   259	    out.append(row("Alcohol statement read", lambda g: pct(sum(r["alcohol_read"] for r in g), len(g))))
   260	    out.append(row("Net contents read", lambda g: pct(sum(r["net_read"] for r in g), len(g))))
   261	    out.append(row("All images readable", lambda g: pct(sum(r["readable"] for r in g), len(g))))
   262	    out.append(row("Images per record, median", lambda g: f"{statistics.median(r['images'] for r in g):g}"))
   263	    out.append(row("Longest image side, median px", lambda g: f"{statistics.median(r['long_side_px'] for r in g):.0f}"))
   264	    out.append(row("Latency per record, median", lambda g: f"{statistics.median(r['ms'] for r in g):.0f} ms"))
   265	    out.append(
   266	        row(
   267	            "Latency per record, p95",
   268	            lambda g: f"{sorted(r['ms'] for r in g)[max(0, math.ceil(0.95 * len(g)) - 1)]} ms",  # nearest rank
   269	        )
   270	    )
   271	    out.append("")
   272	    out += ["## Warning assessment, all records", ""]
   273	    c = Counter(r["warning_assessment"] for r in results)
   274	    out += ["| assessment | records |", "|---|---:|"] + [
   275	        f"| {k} | {v} |" for k, v in sorted(c.items(), key=lambda kv: -kv[1])
   276	    ]
   277	    out += ["", "## Hand-checked cases", ""]
   278	    hand = Path("docs/EVAL_REAL_HANDCHECK.md")
   279	    if hand.exists():  # written by a person after looking at the images; kept out of the generated part
   280	        out.append(hand.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip())
   281	    else:
   282	        out.append("_Not yet: the cases to look at are listed in results.csv (local)._")
   283	    return "\n".join(out) + "\n\n" + f"_Regenerated {time.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')}._\n"
   284	
   285	
   286	_BOOL = {"True": True, "False": False, "": None}
   287	
   288	
   289	def _load_results(real: Path) -> tuple[list[Record], Record]:
   290	    """Re-read a previous run (results.csv + results_meta.json) so the report can be regenerated
   291	    without another OCR pass, e.g. after the hand-checked section was written."""
   292	    with (real / "results.csv").open(encoding="utf-8", newline="") as f:
   293	        rows = list(csv.DictReader(f))
   294	    results: list[Record] = []
   295	    for r in rows:
   296	        rec: Record = dict(r)
   297	        for k in ("images", "long_side_px", "lines", "ms"):
   298	            rec[k] = int(r[k])
   299	        for k in ("readable", "rotated", "warning_present", "alcohol_read", "net_read"):
   300	            rec[k] = _BOOL.get(r[k], False)
   301	        rec["origin_found"] = _BOOL.get(r["origin_found"])
   302	        results.append(rec)
   303	    meta_path = real / "results_meta.json"
   304	    meta: Record = (
   305	        json.loads(meta_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if meta_path.exists() else {"engine": {}, "workers": "?"}
   306	    )
   307	    return results, meta
   308	
   309	
   310	def main() -> None:
   311	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
   312	    ap.add_argument("--real", default="tests/fixtures/real")
   313	    ap.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=2)
   314	    ap.add_argument("--out", default="docs/EVAL_REAL.md")
   315	    ap.add_argument("--window", default="records completed in the window given to cola_fetch.py")
   316	    ap.add_argument("--summarize-only", action="store_true", help="rebuild the report from the last run's results.csv")
   317	    args = ap.parse_args()
   318	    real = Path(args.real)
   319	    if args.summarize_only:
   320	        results, meta = _load_results(real)
   321	    else:
   322	        results, meta = asyncio.run(run(real, args.workers))
   323	        if not results:
   324	            print("no records with images found", file=sys.stderr)
   325	            sys.exit(1)
   326	        with (real / "results.csv").open("w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as f:
   327	            w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=list(results[0].keys()))
   328	            w.writeheader()
   329	            w.writerows(results)
   330	        (real / "results_meta.json").write_text(json.dumps(meta, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
   331	    Path(args.out).write_text(summarize(results, meta, args.window), encoding="utf-8")
   332	    print(f"wrote {args.out} from {len(results)} records")
   333	
   334	
   335	if __name__ == "__main__":
   336	    main()


===== FILE: tools/loadtest.py (current text at 3d9bdad, line-numbered) =====
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python
     2	"""Black-box load test against a running instance (local or deployed).
     3	
     4	Two modes:
     5	  steady  N requests through a pool of C concurrent clients that honor 429 + Retry-After, the way
     6	          the batch screen does. Reports throughput and latency percentiles.
     7	  burst   Fire C requests at the same instant with no backoff, to show the 429 path: how many
     8	          were served, how many refused, and that health kept answering.
     9	
    10	Usage:
    11	    python tools/loadtest.py --url http://127.0.0.1:8000 --mode steady --n 100 --concurrency 4
    12	    python tools/loadtest.py --url https://<host> --mode burst --concurrency 32
    13	    python tools/loadtest.py --url ... --mode steady --n 40 --endpoint verify   # front+back application
    14	"""
    15	
    16	from __future__ import annotations
    17	
    18	import argparse
    19	import asyncio
    20	import json
    21	import random
    22	import time
    23	from pathlib import Path
    24	
    25	import httpx
    26	
    27	ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    28	SAMPLES = ROOT / "app" / "static" / "samples"
    29	APPLICATION = {
    30	    "application_id": "LOADTEST",
    31	    "beverage_type": "spirits",
    32	    "brand_name": "OLD TOM DISTILLERY",
    33	    "class_type": "Kentucky Straight Bourbon Whiskey",
    34	    "alcohol_content": "45% Alc./Vol. (90 Proof)",
    35	    "net_contents": "750 mL",
    36	    "bottler": "Distilled and Bottled by Old Tom Distillery, Bardstown, Kentucky",
    37	    "country_of_origin": "USA",
    38	    "imported": False,
    39	}
    40	
    41	
    42	def pct(xs: list[float], p: float) -> float:
    43	    if not xs:
    44	        return 0.0
    45	    s = sorted(xs)
    46	    return s[min(len(s) - 1, int(p * (len(s) - 1)))]
    47	
    48	
    49	async def one(
    50	    client: httpx.AsyncClient, url: str, endpoint: str, files: list[tuple[str, bytes]], batch: bool, retry: bool
    51	) -> tuple[int, float, int, float | None]:
    52	    """Returns (final status, wall ms including retries, number of 429s seen, server-side ms or None)."""
    53	    headers = {"X-Batch": "1"} if batch else {}
    54	    refused = 0
    55	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    56	    for attempt in range(8):
    57	        form = [("images", (n, b, "image/png")) for n, b in files]
    58	        data = {"application": json.dumps(APPLICATION)} if endpoint == "verify" else None
    59	        try:
    60	            r = await client.post(f"{url}/api/v1/{endpoint}", files=form, data=data, headers=headers)
    61	        except httpx.HTTPError:
    62	            return 0, (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000, refused, None
    63	        if r.status_code == 429 and retry:
    64	            refused += 1
    65	            base = float(r.headers.get("Retry-After", "1"))
    66	            await asyncio.sleep(base * (attempt + 1) + random.random() * 0.5)  # grows like the browser client
    67	            continue
    68	        server_ms: float | None = None
    69	        if r.status_code == 200:
    70	            try:
    71	                server_ms = float(r.json()["timing"]["total_ms"])  # the server's own clock, network excluded
    72	            except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
    73	                server_ms = None
    74	        return r.status_code, (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000, refused, server_ms
    75	    return 429, (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000, refused, None
    76	
    77	
    78	async def steady(args: argparse.Namespace, files: list[tuple[str, bytes]]) -> str:
    79	    sem = asyncio.Semaphore(args.concurrency)
    80	    results: list[tuple[int, float, int, float | None]] = []
    81	    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=60) as client:
    82	
    83	        async def task() -> None:
    84	            async with sem:
    85	                results.append(
    86	                    await one(client, args.url, args.endpoint, files, batch=not args.interactive, retry=True)
    87	                )
    88	
    89	        t0 = time.perf_counter()
    90	        await asyncio.gather(*(task() for _ in range(args.n)))
    91	        wall = time.perf_counter() - t0
    92	    ok = [ms for st, ms, _, _ in results if st == 200]
    93	    server = [sv for st, _, _, sv in results if st == 200 and sv is not None]
    94	    codes: dict[int, int] = {}
    95	    for st, _, _, _ in results:
    96	        codes[st] = codes.get(st, 0) + 1
    97	    refused = sum(r for _, _, r, _ in results)
    98	    return "\n".join(
    99	        [
   100	            f"### steady{' interactive' if args.interactive else ''}: {args.n} x {args.endpoint} "
   101	            f"({len(files)} image(s) each), concurrency {args.concurrency}, host {args.url}",
   102	            f"- wall {wall:.1f} s, throughput {args.n / wall:.2f} req/s ({args.n * len(files) / wall:.2f} images/s)",
   103	            f"- wall latency ms per successful request, including any backoff waits: p50 {pct(ok, 0.5):.0f}, "
   104	            f"p95 {pct(ok, 0.95):.0f}, max {max(ok) if ok else 0:.0f}",
   105	            f"- server-side latency ms (the response's own timing.total_ms, network excluded): p50 {pct(server, 0.5):.0f}, "
   106	            f"p95 {pct(server, 0.95):.0f}, max {max(server) if server else 0:.0f}",
   107	            f"- final status codes: {codes}; 429 responses absorbed by backoff along the way: {refused}",
   108	        ]
   109	    )
   110	
   111	
   112	async def burst(args: argparse.Namespace, files: list[tuple[str, bytes]]) -> str:
   113	    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=60) as client:
   114	        t0 = time.perf_counter()
   115	        health_task = asyncio.create_task(client.get(f"{args.url}/api/v1/health"))
   116	        results = await asyncio.gather(
   117	            *(one(client, args.url, args.endpoint, files, batch=True, retry=False) for _ in range(args.concurrency))
   118	        )
   119	        wall = time.perf_counter() - t0
   120	        h = await health_task
   121	    codes: dict[int, int] = {}
   122	    for st, _, _, _ in results:
   123	        codes[st] = codes.get(st, 0) + 1
   124	    served = [ms for st, ms, _, _ in results if st == 200]
   125	    return "\n".join(
   126	        [
   127	            f"### burst: {args.concurrency} simultaneous {args.endpoint} requests, no backoff, host {args.url}",
   128	            f"- status codes: {codes} (429 = refused immediately with Retry-After)",
   129	            f"- served latency ms: p50 {pct(served, 0.5):.0f}, max {max(served) if served else 0:.0f}; wall {wall:.1f} s",
   130	            f"- health during burst: HTTP {h.status_code}, in_flight={h.json().get('in_flight')}",
   131	        ]
   132	    )
   133	
   134	
   135	def main() -> None:
   136	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
   137	    ap.add_argument("--url", default="http://127.0.0.1:8000")
   138	    ap.add_argument("--mode", choices=["steady", "burst"], default="steady")
   139	    ap.add_argument("--endpoint", choices=["extract", "verify"], default="extract")
   140	    ap.add_argument("--n", type=int, default=50)
   141	    ap.add_argument("--concurrency", type=int, default=0, help="0 = the server's max_concurrency from /health")
   142	    ap.add_argument("--images", nargs="*", default=None, help="image paths; default: bundled clean sample")
   143	    ap.add_argument("--report", default="docs/LOADTEST.md")
   144	    ap.add_argument("--interactive", action="store_true", help="no X-Batch header: measures the person-facing path")
   145	    args = ap.parse_args()
   146	    paths = (
   147	        [Path(p) for p in args.images]
   148	        if args.images
   149	        else (
   150	            [SAMPLES / "clean_front.png", SAMPLES / "clean_back.png"]
   151	            if args.endpoint == "verify"
   152	            else [SAMPLES / "clean_front.png"]
   153	        )
   154	    )
   155	    files = [(p.name, p.read_bytes()) for p in paths]
   156	    if args.concurrency <= 0:
   157	        args.concurrency = int(httpx.get(f"{args.url}/api/v1/health", timeout=10).json().get("max_concurrency", 2))
   158	    block = asyncio.run(steady(args, files) if args.mode == "steady" else burst(args, files))
   159	    print(block)
   160	    rp = Path(args.report)
   161	    if not rp.exists():
   162	        rp.write_text(
   163	            "# Load test log\n\n_Appended by `tools/loadtest.py`. Each block names the host it ran against._\n\n",
   164	            encoding="utf-8",
   165	        )
   166	    with rp.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
   167	        f.write(f"{block}\n- run at {time.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}\n\n")
   168	
   169	
   170	if __name__ == "__main__":
   171	    main()


===== FILE: tests/unit/test_typeface.py (current text at 3d9bdad, line-numbered) =====
     1	"""Type weight from pixels: stroke width over height, on drawn strokes of known width."""
     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	import numpy as np
     6	from app.pipeline.typeface import MIN_HEIGHT, measure_line, stroke_ratio
     7	
     8	
     9	def _strokes(width: int, height: int = 48, count: int = 8, canvas_w: int = 320, ink: int = 0) -> np.ndarray:
    10	    """A white canvas with `count` vertical bars of the given stroke width, like a line of print."""
    11	    arr = np.full((height, canvas_w, 3), 255, dtype=np.uint8)
    12	    step = canvas_w // (count + 1)
    13	    for i in range(1, count + 1):
    14	        x = i * step
    15	        arr[6 : height - 6, x : x + width] = ink
    16	    return arr
    17	
    18	
    19	def _gray(arr: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    20	    return arr[:, :, 0]
    21	
    22	
    23	def test_bold_strokes_measure_heavier_than_regular_in_proportion():
    24	    regular = stroke_ratio(_gray(_strokes(4)))
    25	    bold = stroke_ratio(_gray(_strokes(7)))
    26	    assert regular is not None and bold is not None
    27	    assert 1.35 < bold / regular < 2.1  # 7 px over 4 px; the estimator overstates thin strokes a little
    28	    assert 0.08 < regular < 0.14 and 0.14 < bold < 0.22
    29	
    30	
    31	def test_too_small_or_empty_crops_are_not_measured():
    32	    assert stroke_ratio(_gray(_strokes(4, height=MIN_HEIGHT - 4))) is None
    33	    assert stroke_ratio(np.full((48, 320), 255, dtype=np.uint8)) is None  # blank
    34	    assert stroke_ratio(_gray(_strokes(1, height=48))) is None  # hairline strokes are noise
    35	
    36	
    37	def test_light_print_on_a_dark_ground_measures_the_same():
    38	    dark = _strokes(6)
    39	    inverted = 255 - dark
    40	    a, b = stroke_ratio(_gray(dark)), stroke_ratio(_gray(inverted))
    41	    assert a is not None and b is not None and abs(a - b) < 0.01
    42	
    43	
    44	def test_heading_line_is_split_into_heading_and_remainder():
    45	    # a line whose left 40% carries heavy strokes (the heading) and whose right part is regular
    46	    canvas = np.full((48, 600, 3), 255, dtype=np.uint8)
    47	    canvas[:, :240] = _strokes(7, canvas_w=240, count=6)
    48	    canvas[:, 260:] = _strokes(4, canvas_w=340, count=8)
    49	    text = "GOVERNMENT WARNING: (1) According to the Surgeon General,"  # the heading is ~19 of 57 characters
    50	    box = ((0, 0), (600, 0), (600, 48), (0, 48))
    51	    lw = measure_line(canvas, box, text)
    52	    assert lw.weight is not None and lw.head is not None and lw.tail is not None
    53	    assert lw.head > lw.tail * 1.3
    54	    plain = measure_line(canvas, box, "because of the risk of birth defects")
    55	    assert plain.head is None and plain.tail is None and plain.weight is not None
    56	
    57	
    58	def test_solid_blocks_and_bar_codes_are_not_print():
    59	    """A crop that is mostly ink, or whose 'strokes' are wider than half the height (a bar code or a
    60	    filled panel the detector boxed as a line), must not yield a weight; one of these crashed the
    61	    real-label evaluation with a ratio of 2.2 before the cap."""
    62	    assert stroke_ratio(np.zeros((48, 320), dtype=np.uint8)) is None
    63	    assert stroke_ratio(_gray(_strokes(60, count=2))) is None

    56	def test_warning_read_from_a_rotated_image_accumulates_its_vertical_lines():
    57	    """A sideways photo, or a label that prints the statement vertically along its edge, is read
    58	    from a rotated array; mapped back, its lines are vertical strips stacked left to right. The
    59	    column filter must follow the text direction or the span stops after the anchor."""
    60	    from app.schemas import OcrLine
    61	
    62	    lines = []
    63	    for i, text in enumerate(wrapped(CANONICAL)):
    64	        x = 1800 + 40 * i  # each line is a tall narrow box; consecutive lines step right
    65	        lines.append(
    66	            OcrLine(image_index=0, text=text, confidence=0.99, box=((x, 170), (x + 36, 170), (x + 36, 1300), (x, 1300)))
    67	        )
    68	    r = report(lines)
    69	    assert r.present and r.exact, (r.assessment, r.similarity, r.diff)

   243	    text = CANONICAL.upper().replace("A CAR OR OPERATE", 'A CAR OR88 186"223932 OPERATE')

   246	    assert "OR88" in (r.diff or "")

   248	    assert classify_difference(CANONICAL, CANONICAL.replace("a car or operate", "a car nor88 operate")) == "wording"

   250	
   251	def _weighted(head, tail, body):
   252	    """A statement whose heading line carries head/tail weights and whose other lines carry body."""
   253	    lines = make_lines(wrapped(CANONICAL))
   254	    out = [lines[0].model_copy(update={"weight": head, "weight_head": head, "weight_tail": tail})]
   255	    out += [ln.model_copy(update={"weight": body}) for ln in lines[1:]]
   256	    return out
   257	
   258	
   259	def test_type_weight_bold_heading_over_regular_body_matches():
   260	    r = report(_weighted(0.145, 0.118, 0.118))
   261	    assert r.exact and r.anchor_bold is Status.match and r.body_not_bold is Status.match
   262	    assert "heavier" in r.notes[2]
   263	
   264	
   265	def test_type_weight_same_weight_is_review_on_both_counts():
   266	    """All bold, or a heading that is not bold: the measurement cannot tell them apart, so both
   267	    format rows ask the person, with one note saying why."""
   268	    all_bold = report(_weighted(0.146, 0.141, 0.141))
   269	    assert all_bold.anchor_bold is Status.needs_review and all_bold.body_not_bold is Status.needs_review
   270	    assert "same weight" in all_bold.notes[2] and "whole statement" in all_bold.notes[2]
   271	    light = report(_weighted(0.115, 0.113, 0.113))
   272	    assert light.anchor_bold is Status.needs_review and light.body_not_bold is Status.needs_review
   273	
   274	
   275	def test_type_weight_unmeasured_or_inconclusive_is_not_checked():
   276	    r = report(_weighted(None, None, None))
   277	    assert r.anchor_bold is Status.not_checked and r.body_not_bold is Status.not_checked
   278	    assert "could not be measured" in r.notes[2]
   279	    r2 = report(_weighted(0.160, 0.145, 0.145))  # ratio 1.10: between "same" (1.05) and "heavier" (1.15)
   280	    assert r2.anchor_bold is Status.not_checked and "confidence" in r2.notes[2]
   281	
   282	
   283	def test_type_weight_heading_alone_on_its_line_compares_stroke_pixels_with_the_other_lines():
   284	    lines = make_lines(["GOVERNMENT WARNING:", *wrapped(CANONICAL[len("GOVERNMENT WARNING: ") :], 60)])
   285	    out = [lines[0].model_copy(update={"weight": 0.20, "weight_head": 0.20, "weight_tail": None})]
   286	    out += [ln.model_copy(update={"weight": 0.13}) for ln in lines[1:]]
   287	    r = report(out)
   288	    assert r.anchor_bold is Status.match and "other lines" in r.notes[2]
   289	    same = [out[0].model_copy(update={"weight": 0.13, "weight_head": 0.13}), *out[1:]]
   290	    assert report(same).anchor_bold is Status.needs_review

     1	import pytest

    57	
    58	def test_alcohol_matches_within_tolerance():
    59	    a, b = parse_alcohol("45% alc/vol"), parse_alcohol("45.0% ALC. BY VOL.")
    60	    assert alcohol_matches(a, b)
    61	    assert not alcohol_matches(a, parse_alcohol("40% alc/vol"))
    62

    64	@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    65	    ("text", "mls"),
    66	    [
    67	        ("750 mL", [750.0]),
    68	        ("750ML", [750.0]),
    69	        ("1 L", [1000.0]),
    70	        ("1.75 L", [1750.0]),
    71	        ("75 cl", [750.0]),
    72	        ("0,75 L", [750.0]),
    73	        ("1,750 mL", [1750.0]),
    74	        ("12 FL OZ (355 mL)", [354.88, 355.0]),
    75	        ("16 FL. OZ.", [473.18]),
    76	        ("1 PINT", [473.18]),
    77	        ("7S0 mL", [750.0]),
    78	    ],
    79	)
    80	def test_parse_volumes_formats(text, mls):
    81	    got = [v.ml for v in parse_volumes(text)]
    82	    assert got == pytest.approx(mls, abs=0.01)
    83

    85	def test_parse_volumes_ignores_non_volume_text():
    86	    assert parse_volumes("EST. 1925 Kentucky Straight Bourbon") == []
    87	    assert parse_volumes("45% Alc./Vol. (90 Proof)") == []
    88	    assert parse_volumes("OLD TOM DISTILLERY") == []

    91	def test_volumes_match_allows_unit_conversion_rounding():
    92	    assert volumes_match(750, 751.2)
    93	    assert volumes_match(355, 354.88)
    94	    assert not volumes_match(750, 700)

   120	def test_compare_flags_proof_disagreement_and_missing_required_statement():
   121	    from app.config import Settings
   122	    from app.pipeline.compare import compare
   123	    from app.schemas import ApplicationFields
   124	
   125	    from tests.unit.conftest import make_lines
   126	
   127	    s = Settings(ocr_workers=1)
   128	    lines = make_lines(
   129	        ["OLD TOM DISTILLERY", "Kentucky Straight Bourbon Whiskey", "45% Alc./Vol. (80 Proof)", "750 mL"]
   130	    )
   131	    app = ApplicationFields(
   132	        beverage_type="spirits",
   133	        brand_name="OLD TOM DISTILLERY",
   134	        class_type="Kentucky Straight Bourbon Whiskey",
   135	        alcohol_content="45% Alc./Vol. (90 Proof)",
   136	        net_contents="750 mL",
   137	    )
   138	    alc = next(c for c in compare(app, lines, [], s).checks if c.id == "alcohol_content")
   139	    assert alc.status == "mismatch" and "proof differs" in alc.note

   142	    alc2 = next(c for c in compare(app2, lines, [], s).checks if c.id == "alcohol_content")
   143	    assert alc2.status == "needs_review"  # required for spirits, missing from the application

   188	def test_conflicting_alcohol_statements_across_images_are_a_mismatch():
   189	    from app.config import Settings
   190	    from app.pipeline.compare import compare
   191	    from app.schemas import ApplicationFields
   192	
   193	    from tests.unit.conftest import make_lines
   194	
   195	    s = Settings(ocr_workers=1)
   196	    front = make_lines(["OLD TOM DISTILLERY", "40% Alc./Vol. (80 Proof)", "750 mL"], image_index=0)
   197	    back = make_lines(["Product of USA", "45% Alc./Vol. (90 Proof) 750 mL"], image_index=1)
   198	    app = ApplicationFields(
   199	        beverage_type="spirits",
   200	        brand_name="OLD TOM DISTILLERY",
   201	        class_type="Bourbon",
   202	        alcohol_content="45% Alc./Vol. (90 Proof)",
   203	        net_contents="750 mL",
   204	    )
   205	    alc = next(c for c in compare(app, front + back, [], s).checks if c.id == "alcohol_content")
   206	    assert alc.status == "mismatch" and "40%" in alc.note and "45%" in alc.note
   207	    assert len(alc.evidence) == 2

   210	def _spirits_app(**over):
   211	    from app.schemas import ApplicationFields
   212	
   213	    base = {
   214	        "beverage_type": "spirits",
   215	        "brand_name": "OLD TOM DISTILLERY",
   216	        "class_type": "Bourbon",
   217	        "alcohol_content": "45% Alc./Vol. (90 Proof)",
   218	        "net_contents": "750 mL",
   219	    }
   220	    base.update(over)
   221	    return ApplicationFields(**base)

   224	def _alc(app, lines):
   225	    from app.config import Settings
   226	    from app.pipeline.compare import compare
   227	
   228	    return next(c for c in compare(app, lines, [], Settings(ocr_workers=1)).checks if c.id == "alcohol_content")

   230	
   231	def test_wrapped_percent_and_proof_lines_are_one_statement():
   232	    from tests.unit.conftest import make_lines
   233	
   234	    lines = make_lines(["OLD TOM DISTILLERY", "Bourbon", "45% Alc./Vol.", "(90 Proof)", "750 mL"])
   235	    alc = _alc(_spirits_app(), lines)
   236	    assert alc.status == "match" and "45" in alc.note
   237	    # the two halves disagree: that is one inconsistent statement (review), not two contradicting labels
   238	    lines = make_lines(["OLD TOM DISTILLERY", "Bourbon", "45% Alc./Vol.", "(80 Proof)", "750 mL"])
   239	    alc = _alc(_spirits_app(alcohol_content="45% Alc./Vol."), lines)
   240	    assert alc.status == "needs_review" and "does not equal twice" in alc.note
   241	    # and when the application states a proof, a different proof on the label is a mismatch
   242	    alc = _alc(_spirits_app(), lines)
   243	    assert alc.status == "mismatch" and "proof differs" in alc.note
   244	
   245	
   246	def test_equal_percents_with_conflicting_proofs_are_a_mismatch():
   247	    from tests.unit.conftest import make_lines
   248	
   249	    front = make_lines(["OLD TOM DISTILLERY", "45% ALC/VOL (90 PROOF)"], image_index=0)
   250	    back = make_lines(["45% ALC/VOL (80 PROOF)", "750 mL"], image_index=1)
   251	    alc = _alc(_spirits_app(), front + back)
   252	    assert alc.status == "mismatch" and "different proofs" in alc.note

   283	def test_bottler_as_registered_matches_the_label_as_printed():
   284	    """Real labels prefix and abbreviate ("Brewed by GREEN CHEEK BEER CO."); applications spell out."""
   285	    from app.config import Settings
   286	    from app.pipeline.compare import bottler_check
   287	    from app.pipeline.normalize import fold_company
   288	
   289	    from tests.unit.conftest import make_lines
   290	
   291	    assert fold_company("Brewed & Packaged by GREEN CHEEK BEER CO.") == "green cheek beer"
   292	    assert fold_company("Green Cheek Beer Company, LLC") == "green cheek beer"
   293	    assert fold_company("Imported by: T. Elenteny Imports, New York, NY") == "t elenteny imports new york ny"
   294	    s = Settings(ocr_workers=1)
   295	    lines = make_lines(["BREWED BY GREEN CHEEK BEER CO.", "ORANGE, CA", "12 FL OZ"])
   296	    check = bottler_check("Green Cheek Beer Company, Orange, CA", lines, s)
   297	    assert check.status == "match", check
   298	    assert check.found == "BREWED BY GREEN CHEEK BEER CO. ORANGE, CA" and "Label says" in (check.note or "")
   299	    assert check.expected == "Green Cheek Beer Company, Orange, CA"
   300	    # a different company is still a difference
   301	    other = bottler_check(
   302	        "T. Elenteny Holdings, LLC", make_lines(["IMPORTED BY: T. ELENTENY IMPORTS", "NEW YORK, NY"]), s
   303	    )
   304	    assert other.status in ("mismatch", "needs_review")
   305	
   306	
   307	def test_unparseable_required_field_blocks_ready_and_omitted_optional_fields_do_not():
   308	    from app.config import Settings
   309	    from app.pipeline.compare import compare
   310	    from app.pipeline.warning import CANONICAL
   311	
   312	    from tests.unit.conftest import make_lines
   313	
   314	    s = Settings(ocr_workers=1)
   315	    lines = make_lines(["OLD TOM DISTILLERY", "Bourbon", "45% Alc./Vol. (90 Proof)", "750 mL", CANONICAL])
   316	    # bottler and country of origin omitted from the application: not compared, Ready still allowed
   317	    res = compare(_spirits_app(), lines, [], s)
   318	    assert res.warning.exact, res.warning
   319	    assert {c.status for c in res.checks if c.id in ("bottler", "country_of_origin")} == {"not_checked"}
   320	    assert res.verdict == "ready_for_approval"
   321	    # a required value the tool cannot interpret must never be silently skipped
   322	    res = compare(_spirits_app(net_contents="seven fifty"), lines, [], s)
   323	    net = next(c for c in res.checks if c.id == "net_contents")
   324	    assert net.status == "needs_review"
   325	    assert res.verdict == "needs_review" and "net contents" in res.summary
   326	    # imported without a stated country of origin is a question for the agent, not a pass
   327	    res = compare(_spirits_app(imported=True), lines, [], s)
   328	    assert next(c for c in res.checks if c.id == "country_of_origin").status == "needs_review"
   329	    assert res.verdict == "needs_review"
   330

   332	def test_bottler_with_a_different_corporate_form_is_review_not_match():
   333	    """An omitted form is no difference in who bottled it; two different forms name two different
   334	    legal entities and go to the person (review 005, item 1.2)."""
   335	    from app.config import Settings
   336	    from app.pipeline.compare import bottler_check
   337	    from app.pipeline.normalize import company_forms
   338	
   339	    from tests.unit.conftest import make_lines
   340	
   341	    assert company_forms("ACME Brewing LLC, Denver, CO") == frozenset({"LLC", "Company"})
   342	    assert company_forms("Green Cheek Beer Company") == company_forms("GREEN CHEEK BEER CO.")
   343	    s = Settings(ocr_workers=1)
   344	    differs = bottler_check("ACME Brewing LLC", make_lines(["BREWED BY ACME BREWING INC."]), s)
   345	    assert differs.status == "needs_review", differs
   346	    assert "LLC" in (differs.note or "") and "Inc." in (differs.note or "")
   347	    omitted = bottler_check("ACME Brewing Company", make_lines(["BREWED BY ACME BREWING"]), s)
   348	    assert omitted.status == "match", omitted
   349	    same = bottler_check(
   350	        "Green Cheek Beer Company, Orange, CA", make_lines(["BREWED BY GREEN CHEEK BEER CO.", "ORANGE, CA"]), s
   351	    )
   352	    assert same.status == "match", same

   355	def test_blank_net_contents_is_review_with_the_label_value_never_ready():
   356	    """The COLA form carries no net contents, so the application may leave it blank (D-040): the
   357	    result shows what the label says and asks the agent to confirm it; Ready is impossible."""
   358	    from app.config import Settings
   359	    from app.pipeline.compare import compare
   360	    from app.schemas import ApplicationFields
   361	
   362	    from tests.unit.conftest import make_lines
   363	
   364	    s = Settings(ocr_workers=1)
   365	    app = ApplicationFields(
   366	        beverage_type="spirits",
   367	        brand_name="OLD TOM DISTILLERY",
   368	        class_type="Kentucky Straight Bourbon Whiskey",
   369	        alcohol_content="45% Alc./Vol. (90 Proof)",
   370	        net_contents=None,
   371	    )
   372	    import textwrap
   373	
   374	    from app.pipeline.warning import CANONICAL
   375	
   376	    front = make_lines(
   377	        ["OLD TOM DISTILLERY", "Kentucky Straight Bourbon Whiskey", "45% Alc./Vol. (90 Proof)", "750 mL"]
   378	    )
   379	    back = make_lines(textwrap.wrap(CANONICAL, 60), image_index=1)
   380	    res = compare(app, front + back, [], s)
   381	    net = next(c for c in res.checks if c.id == "net_contents")
   382	    assert net.status == "needs_review" and net.found == "750 mL" and net.expected is None
   383	    assert "confirm" in (net.note or "") and len(net.evidence) == 1
   384	    assert res.verdict == "needs_review", res.summary
   385	    blank = ApplicationFields(beverage_type="spirits", brand_name="X", class_type="Vodka", net_contents="")
   386	    assert blank.net_contents == ""  # whitespace-stripped blank from a form is treated as absent
   387	    none_read = compare(blank, make_lines(["X", "VODKA"]), [], s)
   388	    net2 = next(c for c in none_read.checks if c.id == "net_contents")
   389	    assert net2.status == "needs_review" and net2.found is None
   390

   393	    ("class_type", "required"),
   394	    [
   395	        ("Table Wine", False),
   396	        ("Light Wine", False),
   397	        ("Table White Wine", False),  # the registry's class description
   398	        ("Table Red Wine", False),

   406	
   407	def _bottler(expected, label_lines):
   408	    from app.config import Settings
   409	    from app.pipeline.compare import bottler_check
   410	
   411	    from tests.unit.conftest import make_lines
   412	
   413	    return bottler_check(expected, make_lines(label_lines), Settings(ocr_workers=1))

   415	
   416	def test_bottler_registered_line_against_the_label_four_ways():
   417	    """D-041: name and address read is a match; name only is review; a different name on the
   418	    label's own bottler line, or nothing resembling the name, is review with the reason."""
   419	    registered = (
   420	        "Green Cheek Beer Company, Green Cheek Beer Company, Inc., 2957 RANDOLPH ST UNIT A2 & B, Costa Mesa, CA, 92626"
   421	    )
   422	    both = _bottler(registered, ["BREWED BY GREEN CHEEK BEER CO.", "COSTA MESA, CA", "12 FL OZ"])
   423	    assert both.status == "match", both
   424	    name_only = _bottler(registered, ["BREWED BY GREEN CHEEK BEER CO.", "ORANGE, CA", "12 FL OZ"])
   425	    assert name_only.status == "needs_review" and "Costa Mesa" in (name_only.note or ""), name_only
   426	    other = _bottler(registered, ["BOTTLED BY SOMEBODY ELSE BREWING", "ORANGE, CA"])
   427	    assert (
   428	        other.status == "needs_review"
   429	        and "does not resemble" in (other.note or "")
   430	        and other.found == "BOTTLED BY SOMEBODY ELSE BREWING"
   431	    )
   432	    nothing = _bottler(registered, ["FINE ALE", "12 FL OZ"])
   433	    assert nothing.status == "needs_review" and "No line resembling" in (nothing.note or "")
   434	    marker = chr(160) + "(Used on label)"
   435	    used = _bottler(
   436	        "INVOER EKKE LLC, 20 PARADISE AVE, PIERMONT, NY, 10968, CANOPY WINE SELECTIONS" + marker,
   437	        ["IMPORTED BY CANOPY WINE SELECTIONS", "PIERMONT, NY", "750 ML"],
   438	    )
   439	    assert used.status == "match", used
   440

   512	def test_bottler_name_printed_whole_inside_the_label_line_with_its_address_is_a_match():

   514	    registered = "RUBIN WINES LLC, 5220 ROSS RD, SEBASTOPOL, CA, 95472, RIVER ROAD FAMILY VINEYARDS AND WINERY" + marker
   515	    check = _bottler(

   517	    )
   518	    assert check.status == "match", check

     1	from app.pipeline.normalize import (

    20	    assert key("STONE'S THROW") == key("Stones Throw")

    25	    assert fold_digits("9O PROOF") == "90 PROOF"
    26	    assert fold_digits("ALC. 45% BY VOL.") == "ALC. 45% BY VOL."

    30	def test_join_hyphenated_repairs_line_break_hyphenation_for_letter_continuations():
    31	    assert join_hyphenated(["during preg-", "nancy because"]) == "during pregnancy because"
    32	    assert join_hyphenated(["(2) CONSUMP-", "TION OF ALCOHOLIC"]) == "(2) CONSUMPTION OF ALCOHOLIC"  # capitals too
    33	    assert join_hyphenated(["750 mL -", "12 FL OZ"]) == "750 mL - 12 FL OZ"  # a continuation must start with a letter
    34	    assert join_hyphenated(["", "  a  ", "b"]) == "a b"

    39	    assert not case_only_difference("STONE'S THROW", "STONE'S THROW")
    40	    assert not case_only_difference("STONES THROW", "Stone's Throw")

    43	def test_split_registered_party_takes_apart_colas_item_8():

    45	
    46	    p = split_registered_party(
    47	        "Green Cheek Beer Company, Green Cheek Beer Company, Inc., 2957 RANDOLPH ST UNIT A2 & B, Costa Mesa, CA, 92626"
    48	    )
    49	    assert p.names == ("Green Cheek Beer Company",)  # the legal name folds to the same company
    50	    assert (p.city, p.state, p.zip_code) == ("Costa Mesa", "CA", "92626")

    52	    marker = chr(160) + "(Used on label)"

    54	    assert q.names == ("CANOPY WINE SELECTIONS", "INVOER EKKE LLC")  # the name used on the label comes first
    55	    assert (q.city, q.state) == ("PIERMONT", "NY")
    56	    r = split_registered_party(
    57	        "T ELENTENY IMPORTS, T. ELENTENY HOLDINGS, LLC, 66 W BROADWAY   SUITE 301-304, NEW YORK, NY, 10007"
    58	    )
    59	    assert r.names == ("T ELENTENY IMPORTS", "T. ELENTENY HOLDINGS, LLC") and r.city == "NEW YORK"
    60	    short = split_registered_party("Distilled and Bottled by Old Tom Distillery, Bardstown, Kentucky")
    61	    assert short.names == ("Distilled and Bottled by Old Tom Distillery",)
    62	    assert (short.city, short.state, short.state_forms()) == ("Bardstown", "KY", ("ky", "kentucky"))
    63	    bare = split_registered_party("Old Tom Distillery")
    64	    assert bare.names == ("Old Tom Distillery",) and bare.city is None and bare.state is None

===== FILE: tests/unit/test_services.py (current text at 3d9bdad, line-numbered) =====
     1	"""Orchestration behaviours that need an engine but not a real one: the warning rescue (one extra
     2	round of reads, interactive only) and what the rotation retry leaves behind for it."""
     3	
     4	from __future__ import annotations
     5	
     6	import asyncio
     7	import textwrap
     8	from io import BytesIO
     9	
    10	import numpy as np
    11	from app.config import Settings
    12	from app.ocr.base import RawLine
    13	from app.ocr.pool import OcrPool
    14	from app.pipeline.warning import CANONICAL
    15	from app.schemas import ApplicationFields
    16	from app.services import Upload, verify
    17	from PIL import Image
    18	
    19	Box = tuple[tuple[float, float], tuple[float, float], tuple[float, float], tuple[float, float]]
    20	
    21	
    22	def _box(x0: float, y0: float, x1: float, y1: float) -> Box:
    23	    return ((x0, y0), (x1, y0), (x1, y1), (x0, y1))
    24	
    25	
    26	def _front_lines() -> list[RawLine]:
    27	    return [
    28	        RawLine("OLD TOM DISTILLERY", 0.99, _box(10, 10, 300, 50)),
    29	        RawLine("Kentucky Straight Bourbon Whiskey", 0.99, _box(10, 60, 400, 90)),
    30	        RawLine("45% Alc./Vol. (90 Proof) 750 mL", 0.99, _box(10, 100, 300, 130)),
    31	    ]
    32	
    33	
    34	def _statement_lines(text: str = CANONICAL, x0: float = 10, x1: float = 180) -> list[RawLine]:
    35	    return [RawLine(t, 0.99, _box(x0, 10 + 30 * i, x1, 34 + 30 * i)) for i, t in enumerate(textwrap.wrap(text, 60))]
    36	
    37	
    38	def _turn(rgb: np.ndarray) -> int:
    39	    """Which way a landscape test image was turned, from the marker pixel painted at its top-left
    40	    corner: numpy's 90-degree turn puts it bottom-left, the 270-degree turn top-right."""
    41	    h, w = rgb.shape[:2]
    42	    if w >= h:
    43	        return 0
    44	    return 90 if rgb[-1, 0].sum() == 0 else 270
    45	
    46	
    47	class SidewaysWarningEngine:
    48	    """Upright (landscape array): brand, class and net contents read perfectly, no warning.
    49	    Turned (portrait array): the warning statement, as a label that prints it vertically along one
    50	    edge would present it once the image is turned. Turned 270 (the first turn the round tries) it
    51	    reads with one word wrong, turned 90 it reads exactly."""
    52	
    53	    name = "fake"
    54	
    55	    def __init__(self) -> None:
    56	        self.calls: list[tuple[int, int]] = []
    57	
    58	    def recognize(self, rgb: np.ndarray) -> list[RawLine]:
    59	        h, w = rgb.shape[:2]
    60	        self.calls.append((w, h))
    61	        turn = _turn(rgb)
    62	        if turn == 0:
    63	            return _front_lines()
    64	        if turn == 270:
    65	            return _statement_lines(CANONICAL.replace("may cause", "can cause"))
    66	        return _statement_lines()
    67	
    68	    def info(self) -> dict[str, str]:
    69	        return {"engine": "fake"}
    70	
    71	
    72	def _png(w: int = 400, h: int = 200) -> bytes:
    73	    im = Image.new("RGB", (w, h), "white")
    74	    im.putpixel((0, 0), (0, 0, 0))  # orientation marker, see _turn
    75	    buf = BytesIO()
    76	    im.save(buf, "PNG")
    77	    return buf.getvalue()
    78	
    79	
    80	APP = ApplicationFields(
    81	    beverage_type="spirits",
    82	    brand_name="OLD TOM DISTILLERY",
    83	    class_type="Kentucky Straight Bourbon Whiskey",
    84	    alcohol_content="45% Alc./Vol. (90 Proof)",
    85	    net_contents="750 mL",
    86	)
    87	
    88	
    89	def _run(settings: Settings, engine=None, *, interactive: bool = True, png: bytes | None = None):
    90	    engine = engine or SidewaysWarningEngine()
    91	    pool = OcrPool(settings, lambda: engine)
    92	    pool.warmup()
    93	    engine.calls.clear()
    94	    res = asyncio.run(
    95	        verify(APP, [Upload(data=png or _png(), filename="label.png")], settings, pool, interactive=interactive)
    96	    )
    97	    pool.shutdown()
    98	    return engine, res
    99	
   100	
   101	def test_rescue_finds_a_warning_printed_sideways_and_maps_it_back():
   102	    engine, res = _run(Settings(ocr_workers=1))
   103	    assert res.warning.present, res.warning
   104	    assert engine.calls[0] == (400, 200) and engine.calls[1:] == [(200, 400)]  # upright, then ONE turned read
   105	    assert res.images[0].rotated_degrees == 0  # the upright read stayed the main read
   106	    for ev in res.warning.evidence:  # mapped back into the upright image: tall narrow boxes inside it
   107	        xs, ys = [p[0] for p in ev.box], [p[1] for p in ev.box]
   108	        assert max(ys) - min(ys) > max(xs) - min(xs)
   109	        assert min(xs) >= 0 and max(xs) <= 400 and min(ys) >= 0 and max(ys) <= 200
   110	    assert next(c for c in res.checks if c.id == "brand_name").status == "match"
   111	
   112	
   113	def test_rescue_is_one_round_across_the_workers_and_keeps_the_best_read():
   114	    """With two workers both turns run in the same round; the exact read wins over the one with a
   115	    wrong word (review 005, item 1.4). With one worker only the first turn (270) runs, the round
   116	    being the budget, and the verdict is whatever that read gave."""
   117	    engine, res = _run(Settings(ocr_workers=2))
   118	    assert len(engine.calls) == 3 and engine.calls[0] == (400, 200)
   119	    assert res.warning.present and res.warning.exact, res.warning
   120	    assert res.verdict == "ready_for_approval", res.summary
   121	    engine, res = _run(Settings(ocr_workers=1))
   122	    assert len(engine.calls) == 2
   123	    assert res.warning.present and res.warning.assessment == "wording", res.warning
   124	
   125	
   126	def test_batch_requests_read_every_image_exactly_once():
   127	    """The batch screen reads images before it knows their application; a front label carries no
   128	    statement by design and must not pay for re-reads (review 005, section 3)."""
   129	    engine, res = _run(Settings(ocr_workers=2), interactive=False)
   130	    assert engine.calls == [(400, 200)]
   131	    assert not res.warning.present and res.warning.assessment == "absent"
   132	
   133	
   134	class PoorUprightEngine:
   135	    """A sideways photo: upright reads two low-confidence lines (so the general rotation retry
   136	    fires); turned 90 the page is full of confident text but no statement; turned 270 it holds the
   137	    statement and little else. The retry keeps the 90 read (more text); the rescue must take the
   138	    statement from the 270 read it already has, without another engine call."""
   139	
   140	    name = "fake"
   141	
   142	    def __init__(self) -> None:
   143	        self.calls: list[tuple[int, int]] = []
   144	
   145	    def recognize(self, rgb: np.ndarray) -> list[RawLine]:
   146	        h, w = rgb.shape[:2]
   147	        self.calls.append((w, h))
   148	        turn = _turn(rgb)
   149	        if turn == 0:
   150	            return [RawLine("~~~", 0.5, _box(10, 10, 60, 30)), RawLine("~~", 0.5, _box(10, 40, 60, 60))]
   151	        if turn == 90:
   152	            return [RawLine(f"LINE {i}", 0.99, _box(10, 10 + 30 * i, 180, 34 + 30 * i)) for i in range(12)]
   153	        return _statement_lines()
   154	
   155	    def info(self) -> dict[str, str]:
   156	        return {"engine": "fake"}
   157	
   158	
   159	def test_rotation_retry_reads_are_reused_before_any_rescue_read():
   160	    engine, res = _run(Settings(ocr_workers=1), PoorUprightEngine())
   161	    assert engine.calls == [(400, 200), (200, 400), (200, 400)]  # upright, 90, 270: nothing more
   162	    assert res.images[0].rotated_degrees == 90
   163	    assert res.warning.present and res.warning.exact, res.warning
   164	
   165	
   166	class TinyPrintEngine:
   167	    """Large artwork: at the working size the heading reads but the statement's body is unreadable
   168	    (only the brand reads); turned it reads nothing; at full resolution the statement appears."""
   169	
   170	    name = "fake"
   171	
   172	    def __init__(self) -> None:
   173	        self.calls: list[tuple[int, int]] = []
   174	
   175	    def recognize(self, rgb: np.ndarray) -> list[RawLine]:
   176	        h, w = rgb.shape[:2]
   177	        self.calls.append((w, h))
   178	        if w < h:
   179	            return []
   180	        if w <= 1280:
   181	            return [*_front_lines(), RawLine("GOVERNMENT WARNING: ~ ~~ ~", 0.9, _box(700, 10, 900, 20))]
   182	        return _statement_lines(x0=1500, x1=2000)
   183	
   184	    def info(self) -> dict[str, str]:
   185	        return {"engine": "fake"}
   186	
   187	
   188	def test_rescue_reads_large_artwork_once_at_full_resolution():
   189	    """The heading was seen but the body was not: the full-resolution read goes first in the plan,
   190	    so it is the one read a single worker gets."""
   191	    engine, res = _run(Settings(ocr_workers=1), TinyPrintEngine(), png=_png(3000, 1200))
   192	    assert res.warning.present and res.warning.exact, res.warning
   193	    assert res.images[0].width == 3000 and res.images[0].height == 1200
   194	    assert engine.calls == [(1280, 512), (2048, 819)]  # working size, then full resolution, nothing else
   195	    for ev in res.warning.evidence:  # boxes are in the original image's space
   196	        xs, ys = [p[0] for p in ev.box], [p[1] for p in ev.box]
   197	        assert min(xs) >= 2100 and max(xs) <= 3000 and min(ys) >= 0 and max(ys) <= 1200
   198	
   199	
   200	def test_rescue_can_be_switched_off():
   201	    engine, res = _run(Settings(ocr_workers=1, warning_rescue=False))
   202	    assert not res.warning.present and res.warning.assessment == "absent"
   203	    assert engine.calls == [(400, 200)]


===== FILE: tests/integration/test_verify_api.py (current text at 3d9bdad, line-numbered) =====
     1	"""End-to-end checks through the API with the real OCR engine on committed fixtures."""
     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	import pytest
     6	
     7	from tests.integration.conftest import app_json, image_files
     8	
     9	pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration
    10	
    11	
    12	def _app(manifest, app_id):
    13	    return next(a for a in manifest["applications"] if a["id"] == app_id)
    14	
    15	
    16	def _check(resp, check_id):
    17	    return next(c for c in resp["checks"] if c["id"] == check_id)
    18	
    19	
    20	def test_clean_front_and_back_is_ready_for_approval(client, manifest):
    21	    app = _app(manifest, "APP-001")  # Old Tom Distillery, the brief's sample
    22	    r = client.post(
    23	        "/api/v1/verify",
    24	        data={"application": app_json(app)},
    25	        files=image_files("APP-001_front_clean.png", "APP-001_back_clean.png"),
    26	    )
    27	    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    28	    body = r.json()
    29	    assert body["verdict"] == "ready_for_approval", body["summary"]
    30	    assert body["warning"]["present"] and body["warning"]["exact"]
    31	    for cid in ("brand_name", "class_type", "alcohol_content", "net_contents", "bottler", "country_of_origin"):
    32	        assert _check(body, cid)["status"] == "match", (cid, _check(body, cid))
    33	    assert all(c["evidence"] for c in body["checks"] if c["status"] == "match")
    34	    assert body["timing"]["total_ms"] < 15000  # generous for CI; the deployed gate is measured separately
    35	
    36	
    37	def test_wrong_abv_on_label_is_an_issue(client, manifest):
    38	    app = _app(manifest, "APP-001")
    39	    r = client.post(

    43	    )
    44	    body = r.json()
    45	    assert body["verdict"] == "issues_found"
    46	    assert _check(body, "alcohol_content")["status"] == "mismatch"
    47	
    48	
    49	def test_title_case_warning_anchor_needs_review(client, manifest):

    51	    r = client.post(
    52	        "/api/v1/verify",

    58	    assert body["warning"]["anchor_caps"] == "needs_review"

    61	
    62	def test_sideways_back_label_still_yields_an_exact_warning(client, manifest):
    63	    """A back label photographed sideways is recovered by the rotation retry; its warning lines
    64	    map back as vertical strips and must still be joined into one statement."""
    65	    from io import BytesIO
    66	
    67	    from PIL import Image
    68	
    69	    from tests.integration.conftest import FIXTURES
    70	
    71	    back = Image.open(FIXTURES / "APP-001_back_clean.png").convert("RGB").rotate(90, expand=True)
    72	    buf = BytesIO()
    73	    back.save(buf, "PNG")
    74	    app = _app(manifest, "APP-001")
    75	    r = client.post(
    76	        "/api/v1/verify",
    77	        data={"application": app_json(app)},
    78	        files=[*image_files("APP-001_front_clean.png"), ("images", ("back_sideways.png", buf.getvalue(), "image/png"))],
    79	    )
    80	    body = r.json()
    81	    assert body["images"][1]["rotated_degrees"] in (90, 270), body["images"][1]
    82	    assert body["warning"]["present"] and body["warning"]["exact"], body["warning"]
    83	    assert body["verdict"] == "ready_for_approval", body["summary"]
    84	    for ev in body["warning"]["evidence"]:  # vertical strips in the sideways image
    85	        xs, ys = [p[0] for p in ev["box"]], [p[1] for p in ev["box"]]
    86	        assert max(ys) - min(ys) > max(xs) - min(xs)
    87	
    88

   193	def test_early_rejections_carry_security_headers_and_do_not_crash(client):
   194	    r = client.post(
   195	        "/api/v1/extract",
   196	        content=b"x",
   197	        headers={"Content-Type": "multipart/form-data; boundary=x", "Content-Length": "abc"},
   198	    )
   199	    assert r.status_code == 400 and "Content-Security-Policy" in r.headers
   200	    assert r.headers["Server-Timing"].startswith("total;dur=")
   201	    r = client.post(
   202	        "/api/v1/extract",
   203	        content=b"x" * 10,
   204	        headers={"Content-Type": "multipart/form-data; boundary=x", "Content-Length": "99999999999"},
   205	    )
   206	    assert r.status_code == 413 and r.json()["code"] == "request_too_large"
   207	    assert r.headers["Server-Timing"].startswith("total;dur=") and r.headers["X-Request-ID"]
   208
